### [164] [A Spatio-Temporal Self-Propagating Log-Gaussian Cox-Hawkes Process for Star Formation Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05548)
*Qihan Zou*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种连续时空点过程模型——时空自传播对数高斯Cox-Hawkes过程，用于描述恒星形成事件的位置和时间，整合了自发形成、环境相关性、向外传播激发、局部抑制、较差自转和饱和等机制，并通过观测层生成理想化的恒星形成臂发射率与年轻恒星面亮度图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机自传播恒星形成模型多基于离散空间格点与时步，难以精细刻画真实星系中连续的时空演化；需发展连续、物理机制更完备的统计模型以支持高分辨率观测的统计推断。

Method: 扩展时空对数高斯Cox-Hawkes框架，构建连续时空点过程模型；引入较差自转、局部抑制与饱和等新成分；设计观测层将条件事件强度映射为理想化星形成 emissivity 与年轻恒星表面亮度图；通过蒙特卡洛模拟分析不同动力学设定（含/不含旋转、定常前沿）下的事件产出与形态特征。

Result: 模型在无预设螺旋几何条件下生成瞬态絮状螺旋样结构；蒙特卡洛实验显示：含/不含较差自转时事件总数相近，但定常前沿情形事件更少；较差自转影响活动区的缠绕程度与空间排布；生成的地图支持其对观测结构的解释力。

Conclusion: 该框架为从时空分辨的恒星形成观测数据中开展统计建模与参数推断提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Stochastic self-propagating star formation models describe galactic structure through local triggering and feedback but are commonly formulated using discrete spatial cells and time steps. We extend the spatio-temporal log-Gaussian Cox-Hawkes framework to obtain the Spatio-Temporal Self-Propagating Log-Gaussian Cox-Hawkes Process, a continuous point process model for the locations and times of star-forming events. The model combines spontaneous formation, correlated environmental effects, outwardly propagating excitation, local inhibition, differential rotation, and saturation. An observation layer transforms the conditional event intensity into idealised maps of instantaneous star-forming arm emissivity and recent young stellar surface brightness. For the selected parameter setting, the displayed realisation exhibits transient flocculent spiral-like patterns without any deterministic spiral geometry being imposed. The Monte Carlo experiment finds similar event production with and without rotation, whereas the stationary-front case produces fewer events. The displayed maps further suggest that differential rotation contributes to the winding and spatial arrangement of activity. The proposed framework provides a basis for future statistical inference from spatially and temporally resolved observations of star formation.

</details>


### [165] [Baryon-Accelerated Core Collapse in SIDM Halos and its Imprint on Galactic Disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05600)
*Zeineb Mezghanni,Elliot Y. Davies,Adriana Dropulic,Gonzalo Herrera,Abdelaziz Hussein,Lina Necib*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过N体模拟研究了自相互作用暗物质（SIDM）在银河系尺度星系中受重子成分影响的演化，发现重子（恒星盘与核球）可使核心坍缩时间缩短约40倍，导致星系盘变薄与翘曲，揭示了重子与SIDM双向耦合的新观测窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自相互作用暗物质（SIDM）是暗物质粒子性质的重要探针，但其在银河系尺度下受重子结构影响的演化尚未被充分研究。

Method: 开展冷暗物质（CDM）和SIDM（σ/m = [1.0, 2.5, 5.0] cm²/g）的N体模拟，对比含/不含重子成分（恒星盘与核球）的银河系尺度星系模型，并结合数值与半解析分析。

Result: 重子成分使SIDM晕核心坍缩时间大幅缩短（约40倍），坍缩在银河系寿命内发生；由此增强的中平面恢复力导致恒星盘翘曲（flaring）和变薄（thinning）。

Conclusion: 重子与SIDM存在显著双向耦合：重子加速核心坍缩，而坍缩后的晕演化又重塑星系盘结构；该耦合为探测暗物质性质提供了新观测途径。

Abstract: The gravitational coupling between dark matter (DM) halos and the baryonic structures they host is one of the most powerful windows into the particle nature of DM. Self-interacting dark matter (SIDM) presents a minimal, well-motivated extension to the dark sector with dramatic consequences for the structure of galaxies and their halos. However, the impact of baryons on SIDM halo evolution and the resulting galactic structure has been underexplored in Milky Way (MW)-size galaxies. In this paper, we demonstrate that the inclusion of a baryonic component in a MW-size galaxy causes accelerated core collapse to begin within the MW's lifetime for a cross section as low as $σ/m = 1 \, \rm{cm}^2/\rm{g}$. We present a suite of $N$-body simulations of cold dark matter and SIDM MW-size galaxies with and without a baryonic component for cross sections $σ/m =[1.0, 2.5, 5.0]$ cm$^2$/g. We find numerically, and semi-analytically, that the presence of a stellar disk and bulge shortens the predicted core collapse timescales from the DM only simulations by a factor of $\sim 40$. Further, as the core collapse begins within the lifetime of the galaxy, the subsequent density increase strengthens the mid-plane restoring force exerted on stellar disk orbits, leading the disk to flare. This work quantifies both directions of the baryon--SIDM coupling: baryons accelerate core collapse in MW-sized halos, and the resulting halo evolution reshapes the disk through thinning and flaring. Both processes open new observational windows into DM.

</details>


### [166] [Discovery of Very Minor Merger-Induced Star-forming Clumps at $z=3.33$ with JWST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05611)
*Alexander de la Vega,Bahram Mobasher*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜（JWST）观测发现，高红移星系JADES 1034862中的一个致密星团（clump）并非由传统认为的盘不稳定性或大质量比并合（>1:10）形成，而是源于与一个质量仅为宿主星系约1/28的极小质量伴星系的弱引力相互作用；该过程在近1–3亿年内触发了剧烈恒星形成，表明低质量比并合可能是高红移星团形成的重要新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清高红移星系中致密星团（clumps）的形成机制，特别是检验除经典盘不稳定性与大质量比并合外，低质量比（<1:10）并合是否也能有效触发星团形成。

Method: 利用JWST对z=3.33星系JADES 1034862进行光谱与成像观测，结合星团与伴星系的SED拟合，重建其恒星形成历史，并估算质量比、恒星形成率及星团贡献。

Result: 发现该星团形成于与一个质量仅为主星系≈1/28的伴星系的弱相互作用；其恒星形成在近100–300 Myr内急剧上升，已形成~10^7.2 M⊙恒星，现贡献宿主总SFR的≈10%。

Conclusion: 低质量比（<1:10）并合在宇宙学上更频繁，可显著促成高红移星团形成，应被纳入星系演化理论模型。

Abstract: High-redshift galaxies often contain ''clumps:'' compact, $\sim$kpc-scale regions of intense star formation. These substructures make up significant fractions of the masses and star-formation rates of their host galaxies and are expected to affect the morphologies of their hosts, such as by building up bulges. Studying clumps can therefore inform theoretical models of galaxy evolution. Two main mechanisms have been proposed for their formation: gas fragmentation from disk instabilities and compression of gas during gravitational interactions with other galaxies. In the latter, only mergers with mass ratios greater than 1:10 have been considered. Here, we report James Webb Space Telescope spectroscopy and imaging of JADES 1034862, a galaxy at $z_{\textrm{spec}} = 3.33$, which hosts a clump that formed during an interaction with a companion with a stellar mass that is $\approx28$ times lower than the host. From fits to the spectral energy distributions of the clump and the companion, it is found that both of their star-formation histories rose sharply in the most recent 100-300 Myr, during which the clump formed $\sim10^{7.2} \ M_{\odot}$ in stars and currently makes up $\approx10\%$ of the total star-formation rate of its host. Given the higher frequency of mergers with low mass ratios ($<1:10$) to those with higher ratios, we infer that a substantial fraction of high-redshift clumps could have formed through weak gravitational interactions.

</details>


### [167] [A direct measurement of the tilting rate of the Milky Way disc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05615)
*Guillaume F. Thomas,Benoit Famaey,Alicia Rivero,Giuseppina Battaglia,Rodrigo Ibata*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用Gaia DR3数据首次直接测量了银河系盘的倾斜速率（14±2 deg/Gyr），发现其可能源于约80–100亿年前一次大质量卫星星系（如Gaia-Enceladus/Sausage）的逆行并合事件，为银河系形成历史提供了新证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河系盘在相互作用和并合过程中可能发生长期缓慢的取向变化（即tilting），但此前从未被直接观测到；本文旨在探测并量化这一效应。

Method: 基于Gaia DR3中1.1亿颗恒星的垂直方向自行运动数据，分析银河系盘的整体倾斜速率，并与高分辨率宇宙学模拟结果进行比对。

Result: 首次直接测得银河系盘围绕太阳–银心轴的倾斜速率为14±2 deg/Gyr，该信号统计显著，且与模拟中由大质量逆行并合事件引发的长期倾斜一致。

Conclusion: 观测到的倾斜速率很可能是约8–10 Gyr前一次大质量卫星星系（质量比约1:4，类似Gaia-Enceladus/Sausage）逆行并合的长期动力学遗迹，为银河系组装历史提供了独立支持，并开辟了通过当前恒星运动反演星系并合历史的新途径。

Abstract: The Milky Way disc is typically described, at lowest order, as a flattened rotating stellar component with a fixed spin axis. However, interactions and mergers with smaller galaxies are expected to have an impact on the disc properties. Such interactions can leave long-lasting signatures within galactic discs, including warps and corrugation waves that have already been detected in the Milky Way, but also a slow reorientation of the disc known as tilting, which has never been directly measured up to now. Here, using the vertical proper motions of 110 million stars from the Gaia DR3 catalogue, we present the first-ever direct measurement of the tilting rate of our Galaxy's disc. The detected signal of 14 +/- 2 deg/Gyr around the Sun-Galactic Centre axis is statistically significant and is consistent with typical values found in cosmological simulations. By comparing our measurements with high-resolution tailored simulations, we infer that the observed tilting rate is likely the long-term consequence of a major accretion event that occurred around 8-10 billion years ago. The signal is consistent with the merger of a massive satellite galaxy on a retrograde orbit with a ratio of about 1:4, probably associated with the Gaia-Enceladus/Sausage event. These results provide independent evidence for a key episode in the Milky Way's formation history and introduce a new way to reconstruct the assembly histories of disc galaxies from their present-day motions.

</details>


### [168] [Halo mass, star formation, and morphology in galaxies: A morphology-dependent connection between halo environment and star formation activity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05618)
*G. Mountrichas,F. Shankar,F. J. Carrera,A. Georgakakis*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了星系形态（盘状 vs. 椭球状）如何调制暗物质晕质量与恒星形成率之间的关系，发现该关系强烈依赖于形态：盘状星系的晕质量几乎不随恒星形成活动变化，而椭球状星系在高恒星形成活动中占据更低质量晕，在低活动中则趋于一致的较高晕质量，表明晕环境对恒星形成的抑制作用在椭球状星系中更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解星系恒星形成熄灭机制需区分内部结构（如形态）与大尺度环境（如暗物质晕质量）各自的作用。

Method: 利用VIPERS和Stripe 82巡天中z=0.5–1.2的非活动星系样本，结合DESI的Sérsic形态分类、SED拟合得到的恒星质量和SFR，将星系分为盘主导和核球主导两类，并按sSFR分位数分组以控制恒星质量差异；通过投影星系互相关函数推断各组典型暗物质晕质量，并以ΔMS表征恒星形成偏离主序的程度。

Result: 盘主导星系在ΔMS范围内 halo质量基本不变；核球主导星系在高ΔMS时占据较低质量晕，而在低ΔMS时 halo质量升高且趋于一致；该趋势不能主要由恒星质量差异解释。

Conclusion: 仅靠典型暗物质晕质量无法干净地预测星系是否熄灭；星系形态显著调制了晕环境与恒星形成活动之间的联系，该联系在核球主导星系中更强。

Abstract: Understanding the mechanisms responsible for quenching star formation in galaxies requires disentangling the roles of internal structure and large-scale environment. We investigate how galaxy morphology and specific star formation rate (sSFR) relate to the typical masses of host dark-matter halos (DMHs), and whether the halo--star formation connection depends on morphology. We analyse non-active galaxies in the VIPERS and Stripe 82 fields at $0.5 \leq z \leq 1.2$, combining DESI-based Sérsic classifications with stellar masses and SFRs from spectral energy distribution fitting. Galaxies are separated into disc- and bulge-dominated systems and divided into quantile-based sSFR bins designed to minimise stellar-mass differences between morphologies. Each bin is characterised by its median $Δ{\rm MS}=\log {\rm SFR}-\log {\rm SFR}_{\rm MS}$, and characteristic DMH masses are inferred from projected galaxy cross-correlations. The halo-mass relation with star formation activity depends strongly on morphology: disc-dominated galaxies show little variation in halo mass across the probed $Δ{\rm MS}$ range, whereas bulge-dominated galaxies occupy lower-mass halos at high $Δ{\rm MS}$ and higher, mutually consistent halo masses toward lower $Δ{\rm MS}$. These trends are unlikely to be driven mainly by stellar-mass differences. Typical halo mass alone therefore does not map cleanly onto quiescence; instead, the connection between halo environment and star formation activity is stronger in bulge-dominated than in disc-dominated galaxies.

</details>


### [169] [LOFAR radio properties of quasars hosting Extremely High Velocity Outflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05624)
*Amy L. Rankine,Paola Rodríguez Hidalgo*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了极端高速外流（EHVOs）是否对‘射电宁静’类星体的射电辐射有贡献，发现EHVO类星体射电探测率显著更高（38% vs. 24%），且其射电探测率与C IV蓝移相关但不能完全由其解释，暗示存在额外物理机制；紫外吸收谱无显著差异，但最大外流速度与射电光度呈弱正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究极端高速外流（EHVOs）是否为‘射电-宁静’类星体中观测到的射电辐射的潜在来源。

Method: 将SDSS中的EHVO星表与LOFAR LoTSS DR3射电巡天数据交叉证认，并构建基于C IV蓝移的近邻匹配控制样本；分析射电探测率、紫外复合谱及速度-光度关系。

Result: EHVO类星体射电探测率为38%，显著高于母样本的24%；仅匹配C IV蓝移可复现整体高探测率，但在蓝移分布上仍存残差差异；紫外吸收谱在射电探测/未探测EHVO间无显著区别，但最大外流速度与射电光度呈弱正相关。

Conclusion: EHVOs很可能是沿特定视线方向观测到的快速风子集，其关联的射电辐射仍是一种合理机制。

Abstract: Quasar winds are a potential contributor to the radio emission observed in the so-called 'radio-quiet' quasars. Extremely High Velocity Outflows (EHVOs), with bulk velocities >0.1c, represent some of the most energetic of these winds. In this work, we investigate the radio properties of quasars hosting EHVOs in order to assess whether such high-velocity winds contribute to the observed radio emission. We cross-match the catalogue of EHVOs from the Sloan Digital Sky Survey with the high-sensitivity Low-Frequency Array (LOFAR) Two-metre Sky Survey (LoTSS) Data Release 3. We find that EHVO quasars exhibit a significantly higher radio-detection fraction of 38 per cent compared to an underlying parent quasar population at 24 per cent. By constructing control samples using nearest-neighbour matching, we show that matching the C IV emission line blueshift alone is sufficient to reproduce the elevated radio-detection fraction in a global sense; however, differences still remain in the radio-detection fraction as a function of C IV emission blueshift between the EHVO distribution and any matched sample, suggesting that some additional mechanism is at play. Furthermore, composite spectra reveal no significant differences in the ultraviolet absorption profiles between radio-detected and radio-undetected EHVOs, although we identify a mild positive correlation between maximum outflow velocity and radio luminosity. Our results suggest that EHVOs likely represent a subset of the quasars which host fast winds that are observed along particular lines-of-sight, and that radio emission associated with such winds remains a plausible mechanism.

</details>


### [170] [Reigniting the FUSE I: Star Formation in Massive Elliptical Galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05675)
*Michael Reefe,Michael McDonald,Eric Miller,Thomas Brown*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文对30个大质量椭圆星系的远紫外光谱进行了全面、均一的再分析，结合多波段测光数据进行SED拟合，构建了高质量恒星质量、恒星形成历史、金属丰度和尘埃不透明度等参数星表，并提出年轻恒星的气体来源更可能来自星系际介质冷却或矮星系并合剥离，而非老恒星的回收气体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新系统性地分析FUSE存档中30个大质量椭圆星系的远紫外光谱，以获得更可靠、一致的恒星种群与星际介质物理参数，并探究其年轻恒星形成气体起源这一关键问题。

Method: 对FUSE远紫外光谱进行SED拟合，结合GALEX、SDSS、DES/DECaLS、2MASS和WISE等多波段孔径匹配测光；构建并公开发布一套高分辨率FUV、宽波段（90 Å–1000 μm）现代恒星模板库，兼容FSPS和Python FSPS。

Result: 获得了这批椭圆星系高质量的恒星质量、恒星形成历史、恒星与气体金属丰度、尘埃不透明度等参数；发现其最年轻的恒星并非由老恒星反馈气体形成，而更可能源于热CGM/ICM冷却气体或邻近/并合矮星系剥离的冷气体。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解椭圆星系晚期恒星形成机制提供了关键观测约束，支持外部气体吸积主导模型，并为后续聚焦O VI发射线与冷却流问题的研究奠定基础。

Abstract: In this inaugural paper of our "Reigniting the FUSE" series, we present the data collection & reduction, methodology, and preliminary results for a comprehensive and homogeneous reanalysis of all 30 massive elliptical galaxies that have archival far-ultraviolet (FUV) spectra with the Far Ultraviolet Spectroscopic Explorer (FUSE). We perform SED fitting to these spectra, with aperture-matched photometry from GALEX, SDSS, DES/DECaLS, 2MASS, and WISE, and construct a catalogue of high quality constraints on their stellar masses, star formation histories, stellar and gas-phase metallicities, and dust opacities. As a part of this analysis, we have also compiled a comprehensive modern set of stellar template libraries, with high resolution in the FUV, and broad coverage from $90\,\mathring{\rm A}$ - $1000\,{\rm μm}$ at lower resolution, which is made available through modified versions of FSPS and Python FSPS. We find strong evidence to suggest that the youngest stars in these systems do not form out of recycled gas from the old stellar populations, but instead are fueled either by gas cooling out of the hot circumgalactic/intracluster medium, or cool gas stripped from nearby or merging dwarf systems. Future papers in this series will reexamine the O VI $λλ$1032,1038 $\mathring{\rm A}$ emission in the context of the cooling flow problem.

</details>


### [171] [Tracing the Hidden Molecular Gas in the Small Magellanic Cloud with SOFIA and APEX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05680)
*K. Rodriguez-Ambler,R. Herrera-Camus,M. Rúbio,A. D. Bolatto,D. Riquelme-Vásquez,K. Jameson,C. Muñoz-López,E. J. Tarantino,J. Stutzki*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用SOFIA/GREAT、APEX和ASKAP+Parkes数据，分析了SMC中[CII]发射的多相起源，发现约78%的[CII]来自分子气体，表明其分子气体主要为CO-faint相；推导出比银河系高4.5倍的H2-to-CO转换因子；指出CO-faint比例主要受尘埃与气体屏蔽影响，而非恒星形成活动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低金属丰度环境下，尘埃减少导致分子云对远紫外辐射屏蔽减弱，CO光致离解增强，产生大量CO-faint或CO-dark分子气体，需可靠示踪手段。[CII] 158μm谱线是探测这类气体的关键探针。

Method: 结合SOFIA/GREAT/upGREAT的[CII]、APEX的CO(2-1)及ASKAP+Parkes的H I 21 cm高分辨率数据，分解[CII]辐射的原子与分子成分，并量化CO-faint分子气体占比，推导局地H2-to-CO转换因子X_CO。

Result: 平均78±1%的[CII]来自分子气体，18±1%来自原子气体；[CII]示踪的H2占总分子气体柱密度的77±4%，表明SMC分子气体以CO-faint为主；X_CO≈8.9×10^20 cm^-2 (K km s^-1)^-1（4 pc尺度），是银河系典型值的4.5倍；CO-dark分数与总柱密度或面恒星形成率Σ_SFR无显著相关。

Conclusion: [CII]是SMC中分子气体的主要示踪剂，证实低金属丰度环境中存在广泛、稳定的CO-faint分子相，其比例主要由尘埃和气体屏蔽能力决定。

Abstract: In low-metallicity environments, reduced dust abundance weakens molecular cloud shielding against far-ultraviolet radiation, enhancing CO photodissociation and producing extended CO-faint or CO-dark molecular gas. The [CII] 158 $μ$m line is a key tracer of these CO-faint phases. We characterize the multiphase origin of [CII] emission and quantify the CO-faint molecular gas fraction across several regions of the Small Magellanic Cloud (SMC), the nearest low-metallicity ($\sim 0.2 Z_\odot$) galaxy. We combine high spectral resolution SOFIA/GREAT and upGREAT [CII], APEX CO(2-1), and ASKAP+Parkes H I 21 cm data to decompose [CII] into its atomic and molecular contributions. On average, $(78\pm 1)\%$ of [CII] intensity originates from molecular gas and $(18\pm 1)\%$ from atomic gas. The [CII]-traced H$_2$ accounts for $(77\pm4)\%$ of the total molecular gas column density, indicating that the SMC molecular reservoir is dominated by CO-faint gas. We derive a H$_2$-to-CO conversion factor $X_{CO}\approx 8.9\times10^{20}$ cm$^{-2}$ (K km s$^{-1}$)$^{-1}$ on 4 pc scales in CO-emitting regions, 4.5 times the canonical Galactic value. We find no significant correlation between the CO-dark fraction and either total column density or $Σ_{SFR}$, suggesting that the CO-faint fraction is primarily governed by dust and gas shielding rather than current star formation activity. Our results demonstrate that [CII] is the dominant tracer of molecular gas in the SMC and confirm the prevalence of an extended, stable CO-faint molecular phase in low-metallicity environments.

</details>


### [172] [FAST Observations of Filamentary and Compact H I Structure in a Magellanic Stream IV Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06090)
*Yuxin Xiao,Qingzheng Yu,Taotao Fang,Di Li,Shulan Yan,Shuo Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用CRAFTS巡天数据对麦哲伦流IV区进行了高分辨率HI观测，揭示了该区域存在三个具有不同速度趋势的纤维状HI结构，并通过高斯分解和位置-速度图分析，排除了云-云碰撞，支持视线投影解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 麦哲伦流是研究星系际气体剥离、碎裂、混合与演化的理想实验室，但其精细结构和多相气体性质仍需高分辨率观测验证。

Method: 基于CRAFTS巡天的HI射电观测数据，开展3.8°×2.2°天区的成像与谱线分析；采用高斯分解识别并重建HI源；结合位置-速度（P-V）图检验空间重叠区域的动力学关系。

Result: 测得HI总质量约5.3×10⁶ (d/120 kpc)² M⊙；分辨出一个主导的纤维状HI复合体及若干小尺度运动学团块；仅一个源呈现多速度成分；P-V图未发现中间速度桥或V形结构，表明重叠更可能是视线投影所致。

Conclusion: 高灵敏度、高角分辨率HI观测对解析麦哲伦流中微弱发射、致密形态与运动学结构具有关键价值，有助于厘清其气体演化机制。

Abstract: The Magellanic Stream (MS) is believed to have formed from gas removed from the Large and Small Magellanic Clouds through tidal forces and hydrodynamic interactions with the Milky Way's gaseous halo. It provides an important laboratory for studying how stripped gas fragments, mixes, and evolves in a circumgalactic environment. In this work, we present HI observations of a $3.8^\circ\times2.2^\circ$ field in the MS IV region, using the data from the Commensal Radio Astronomy FasT Survey (CRAFTS). The total HI mass in the analyzed field is $\simeq 5.3 \times 10^{6} (d/120\,{\rm kpc})^{2}\,M_{\odot}$, where $d$ is the distance to the MS IV gas. The data resolve the emission into three coherent filamentary HI structures with related but distinct velocity trends. To characterize the HI structures and study potential multiphase gas, we adopt a Gaussian decomposition procedure to identify and reconstruct sources. Our results indicate that the field is dominated by one major filamentary HI complex, together with several smaller kinematic clump-like components. Among these identified sources, only one source likely shows multiple velocity components. Because several sources appear spatially overlapped in projection, we further examine their apparent overlap regions using position-velocity (P-V) diagrams. P-V diagrams across the apparent overlap region show no clear intermediate-velocity bridge or V-shaped structure, favoring line-of-sight projection over direct cloud-cloud collision. These results demonstrate the value of deep, high-angular-resolution HI observations for resolving faint emission, compact morphologies, and kinematic structure in the MS.

</details>


### [173] [Connecting the little dots in polarized light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06239)
*Piero Madau,Roberto Maiolino,Francesco D'Eugenio*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模型解释LRD（小红点）SDSS J1025+1402的光学连续谱与宽Hα线偏振差异：LRD是高倾角下被尘埃遮蔽的LBD（小蓝点），由超爱丁顿吸积供能；连续谱偏振源于内厚吸积流中的电子散射，而宽Hα因缺乏该成分导致偏振度更低、位置角不同；模型成功定量复现观测，但吸积率与倾角存在简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LRD SDSS J1025+1402中光学连续谱（~1.5%）与宽Hα（~0.7%，不同位置角）偏振差异的现象，并探究其物理起源。

Method: 构建一个物理模型：将LRD视为高倾角、尘埃遮蔽的LBD，由超爱丁顿吸积驱动；连续谱偏振来自几何厚内吸积流中电子散射，外薄盘贡献未偏振光稀释信号，环核尘埃屏产生消光偏振；宽Hα无内盘成分，故偏振特性不同；通过模型拟合观测偏振数据。

Result: 该模型能定量重现观测到的连续谱和宽Hα的偏振度及位置角差异；揭示了吸积流几何结构与取向对偏振的影响；指出吸积率与视线倾角之间存在参数简并。

Conclusion: 谱偏振观测是研究‘小点’类星系吸积流几何与取向的有力工具，可与能谱拟合和谱线诊断形成互补。

Abstract: Recent observations of the local Little Red Dot (LRD) analog SDSS~J1025+1402 have revealed that the optical continuum is polarized at the ~1.5% level, while broad Halpha has lower polarization (~0.7%) and a different polarization angle. We explain these properties in a scenario in which LRDs are dust-obscured Little Blue Dots (LBDs) viewed at high inclination and powered by super-Eddington accretion. In this framework, electron scattering in the geometrically thick inner accretion flow produces intrinsic continuum polarization, while unpolarized emission from the outer thin disk dilutes the signal, particularly at optical wavelengths. A circumnuclear dust screen contributes dichroic polarization to both the continuum and broad-line emission. Broad Halpha lacks the intrinsic disk component and therefore has lower polarization and a different position angle. We show that this model quantitatively reproduces the observed polarization properties, although degeneracies between accretion rate and inclination remain. These results illustrate the potential of spectropolarimetry as a probe of accretion-flow geometry and orientation in the little-dot population, complementary to spectral-energy-distribution fitting and line diagnostics.

</details>


### [174] [Interstellar Complex Organic Molecules and Molecular Outflows in NGC 1333 IRAS 4B and 4B' Observed Using NOEMA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06295)
*C. C. Sarver,M. M. Giese,D. C. Lis,S. L. Widicus Weaver*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用NOEMA对NGC 1333中的原恒星IRAS 4B和4B'进行干涉观测，系统探测了其内包层中11种分子（含10种复杂有机分子iCOMs）的空间分布与物理参数，揭示IRAS 4B为富含有机物的双星系统并驱动喷流与外向流，而IRAS 4B'未探测到共空间分子辐射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管原恒星（尤其是0型原恒星）富含气相星际复杂有机分子（iCOMs），但其空间分布仍知之甚少，亟需高分辨率观测厘清其在原恒星包层中的分布特征及物理化学环境。

Method: 使用北延展毫米波阵列（NOEMA）在145 GHz频段对NGC 1333 IRAS 4B和4B'开展干涉成像观测，识别并拟合数百条分子谱线，提取旋转温度、柱密度、速度偏移（LSR）、线宽（FWHM）等物理参数，并构建分子与连续谱的强度及参数空间分布图。

Result: 在IRAS 4B暖内包层中探测到11种分子（含CH₃OH及其同位素体、HCOOCH₃、CH₃OCH₃、CH₃CHO、C₂H₅OH、CH₂OHCHO、CH₃COCH₃、HNCO），其中10种为iCOMs；发现iCOMs辐射与连续谱高度空间相关，且彼此间亦强相关；确认IRAS 4B为驱动南北向喷流和西北–东南向外向流的双星系统；IRAS 4B'仅探测到西–东向外向流，无与其连续谱共位置的分子辐射。

Conclusion: IRAS 4B是迄今已知最丰富的iCOMs原恒星源之一，其iCOMs空间分布受动力学结构（喷流/外向流）和热结构共同调控；而IRAS 4B'缺乏iCOMs辐射暗示其化学演化阶段或物理条件显著不同，凸显原恒星化学多样性。

Abstract: We present interferometric observations with the Northern Extended Millimeter Array (NOEMA) of NGC 1333 IRAS 4B and 4B', two young stellar objects (YSOs) within the Perseus molecular cloud. YSOs, especially Class 0 protostars, are rich in gas-phase interstellar complex organic molecules (iCOMs), but relatively little is known about the spatial distribution of these important prebiotic molecules. Various molecules were imaged within IRAS 4B and the surrounding area in the 145 GHz spectral range, revealing hundreds of molecular transitions within the warm inner envelope of IRAS 4B. This work provides derived physical parameters (rotational temperature, column density, velocity shift with respect to local standard of rest, and full-width at half-maximum spectral line width) for 11 molecules toward IRAS 4B including methanol (CH$_3$OH and isotopologues), methyl formate (HCOOCH$_3$), dimethyl ether (CH$_3$OCH$_3$), acetaldehyde (CH$_3$CHO), ethanol (C$_2$H$_5$OH), glycolaldehyde (CH$_2$OHCHO), acetone (CH$_3$COCH$_3$), and isocyanic acid (HNCO). Out of these molecules, 10 are iCOMs (excluding HNCO) that trace the inner envelope of IRAS 4B and mark it as a rich source of organic material. There is a strong spatial correlation between the continuum emission of IRAS 4B and iCOM molecular emission and among various iCOMs with each other. Physical parameter and integrated intensity maps show that IRAS 4B is a binary protostar system that drives a jet in a north-south orientation and an outflow in a northwest-southeast orientation. IRAS 4B' also drives a west-east outflow but shows no evidence of molecular emission cospatial with its continuum emission.

</details>


### [175] [Deep learning from the crowd Fundamentals of morphological galaxy classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06316)
*Luis Enrique Sucar,Carlos del Burgo,Jonathan Serrano-Pérez*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何利用众包标注（crowd annotations）训练深度神经网络进行星系形态分类，重点探讨了训练策略、标注者一致性及层级结构对模型性能的影响。实验表明，全网络微调、引入层级信息、分阶段迁移学习和集成学习均能显著提升准确率，尤其在数据有限或标注一致性较低时效果更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用众包标注训练深度学习模型进行星系形态分类面临额外挑战，如标注噪声大、一致性低、缺乏明确真值等，需专门设计训练策略以提升鲁棒性与准确性。

Method: 基于Galaxy Zoo 1数据集，采用卷积神经网络（CNN），系统评估五种策略：(i)仅微调最后一层 vs 全网络训练；(ii)忽略 vs 利用形态层级结构；(iii)不同数据量与标注者一致性的组合影响；(iv)分阶段迁移学习（课程学习）；(v)多模型集成。

Result: （i）全网络训练使精确匹配率提升10%；（ii）数据量与标注一致性存在权衡；（iii）引入层级结构可在小样本下提升精度；（iv）分阶段迁移学习在数据受限时更有效；（v）集成可进一步提效；（vi）对困难样本虽整体精度低，但高层级分类仍可达99%以上准确率。

Conclusion: 从众包标注中训练深度模型需兼顾标注质量、层级先验与训练范式，不能简单套用传统监督学习方法；全参数微调、层级建模与迁移/集成策略是关键。

Abstract: Aims. The objective of this work is to adapt a deep neural network model to perform galaxy morphological classification trained from crowd annotations, considering the training scheme, the agreement between the annotators, and the hierarchy.
  Methods. We use Galaxy Zoo 1 as our experimental testbed and trained a convolutional neural network (CNN) for the automatic classification of galaxies' morphologies. We analyze the impact of the following aspects on the classification accuracy and training efficiency: (i) Training only the last layer vs. training all the network; (ii) Classification with only the CNN vs. considering the hierarchy; (iii) Comparing the models trained with different amounts of data and levels of agreement between the annotators; (iv) Training by stages, transferring knowledge from one model to another; and (v) Combining several models as an ensemble.
  Results From the experiments, we derive the following results: (i) Training all the layers in the network significantly improves the accuracy (10% increase in exact match), compared to training only the last layer; (ii) There is a tradeoff between the amount of data and the level of agreement between the annotators used for training; (iii) Using the hierarchy can improve accuracy when the amount of training data is reduced; (iv) Training by stages through transfer learning (curriculum learning) produces higher accuracy for limited data; (v) Ensembles can improve accuracy; (vi) Models achieve a low accuracy for the most difficult cases, but, if we consider hierarchical measures, we can derive useful results for upper levels in the hierarchy. An accuracy above 99% is achieved when training all layers of the network and considering a high agreement between the annotators.
  Conclusions. Training deep learning models from crowd annotations involves additional challenges than learning from hard annotations.

</details>


### [176] [A Direct DESI--SDSS Two-Fibre Test of Aperture Bias in Optical Emission-Line Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06493)
*Shihong Liu,Yu Rong*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用DESI和SDSS对同一组星系的双光纤观测，系统研究了固定角直径光纤口径引起的光谱孔径效应，发现即使在统一处理连续谱和发射线拟合的情况下，不同口径仍导致显著且诊断依赖的发射线比值偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定角直径的光纤口径会采样邻近星系的不同物理区域，从而可能偏差光学发射线诊断结果；需量化并分离这种孔径效应与测量系统误差。

Method: 选取20545个被DESI（1.5角秒）和SDSS（3角秒）共同观测的高置信度发射线星系，将DESI光纤嵌套于SDSS光纤内构成‘双光纤实验’；对所有光谱采用统一的eMILES恒星连续谱与发射线模型重拟合，并保留各自波长相关的点扩散函数，最终获得20200个质量可控的匹配样本。

Result: 相对于SDSS，DESI更小口径导致显著偏移：Δlog(N2)=+0.0067±0.0002，Δlog(S2)=−0.0148±0.0003，Δlog(O3)=−0.0246±0.0006，ΔO3N2=−0.0327±0.0007，Δlog(Hα/Hβ)=−0.0195±0.0003；这些偏移在BPT选的恒星形成星系及近同心子样本中依然存在，且随红shift和光纤相对覆盖度联合变化。

Conclusion: 对于高信噪比发射线星系，即使固定连续谱建模、发射线拟合与光谱分辨率处理，DESI与SDSS之间的比较仍存在可观测、且依赖于诊断指标的光纤孔径效应。

Abstract: Fixed angular apertures sample different physical regions of nearby galaxies and can therefore bias optical emission-line diagnostics. We use 20,545 high-confidence emission-line galaxies observed by both the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) and the Sloan Digital Sky Survey (SDSS) as a two-fibre experiment, in which the DESI 1.5 arcsec fibre is nested within the SDSS 3 arcsec fibre. To isolate aperture effects from line-measurement systematics, we refit every spectrum using the same eMILES stellar-continuum and emission-line model while preserving the wavelength-dependent line-spread function of each spectrum. This common refit yields 20,200 quality-controlled matched galaxies. Relative to SDSS, the smaller DESI aperture produces small but highly significant offsets: $Δ\log({\rm N2})=+0.0067\pm0.0002$, $Δ\log({\rm S2})=-0.0148\pm0.0003$, $Δ\log({\rm O3})=-0.0246\pm0.0006$, $Δ{\rm O3N2}=-0.0327\pm0.0007$, and $Δ\log({\rm H}α/{\rm H}β)=-0.0195\pm0.0003$, where $Δ$ denotes DESI minus SDSS. These offsets persist in Baldwin--Phillips--Terlevich-selected star-forming galaxies and in a near-concentric subset. Their amplitudes vary across the redshift distribution and retain a measurable dependence on apparent fibre coverage after redshift is controlled, showing that redshift and relative aperture coverage jointly modulate the aperture terms. We conclude that, for high-S/N emission-line galaxies, DESI--SDSS comparisons carry measurable, diagnostic-dependent fibre-aperture terms even when continuum modelling, emission-line fitting, and spectral-resolution treatment are held fixed.

</details>


### [177] [ADF22-WEB: Massive galaxy formation shaped by cosmic web filaments in the z = 3.1 proto-cluster core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06679)
*Hideki Umehata,Ian Smail,Charles C. Steidel,Bret D. Lehmer,Kouichiro Nakanishi,Bunyo Hatsukade,Mariko Kubo,Norika Okauchi,Yoichi Tamura,Masato Hagimoto*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了18个位于SSA22原星系团核心、红移z=3.09的尘埃遮蔽星暴星系（DSFGs），结合ALMA、JWST和JVLA观测，分析其尘埃、分子气体及结构特性，发现恒星与尘埃分布结构紧密相关，分子气体激发主要受恒星形成调控，并揭示致密环境中大质量星系演化受宇宙网中重子循环驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高红移致密环境中大质量星系如何通过气体吸积、恒星形成与结构演化协同演化的物理机制，特别是宇宙网 filaments 对 DSFGs 演化的影响。

Method: 利用多波段ALMA（含最高CO(12-11)）、JWST（高分辨率~1 kpc成像）和JVLA CO(1-0)数据，结合Herschel/SPIRE，构建远红外SED，测量尘埃温度、红外光度、分子气体丰度与激发态比例，并分析恒星/尘埃面密度相关性及气体分数、消耗时标等演化参数。

Result: DSFGs 的 L_FIR 跨越两个数量级，中位 T_dust=25 K；恒星与尘埃面密度呈正相关；CO线探测显示 r31≈0.66，高-J CO激发偏低且与Σ_SFR相关；低质量星系气体分数与消耗时标高于场星系；识别出从气体富集、结构弥散到气体贫乏、结构致密的演化序列。

Conclusion: 在原星系团核心的宇宙网环境中，大质量星系演化由连接气体供给、恒星形成与结构致密化的重子循环主导，而非单纯由内部过程或并合驱动。

Abstract: We present a census of dust, molecular gas, and galaxy structure in 18 dusty star-forming galaxies (DSFGs) at $z=3.09$ embedded in Ly$α$-traced cosmic web filaments in the core of the SSA22 proto-cluster, using multi-band ALMA, JWST imaging, and JVLA CO(1--0). Using up to six ALMA bands and Herschel/SPIRE data, we construct rest-frame far-infrared spectral energy distributions. The DSFGs span two orders of magnitude in far-infrared luminosity ($L_{\rm FIR}\sim10^{11}$--$10^{13}L_\odot$), with median values of $\log(L_{\rm FIR}/L_\odot)=11.56^{+0.32}_{-0.37}$ and $T_{\rm dust}=25^{+6}_{-3}{\rm K}$. For eight DSFGs with high-resolution ($\sim1$ kpc) JWST and ALMA imaging, we measure stellar and dust surface densities and find a positive correlation, suggesting that structural compaction of the stellar component proceeds together with that of the interstellar medium. We report 12 detections of CO(1--0), 18 of CO(3--2), four each of CO(8--7) and CO(9--8), and one of CO(12--11). The median brightness temperature ratio between CO(3--2) and CO(1--0) is $r_{31}=0.66^{+0.05}_{-0.04}$, consistent with field galaxies at similar redshifts. The high-$J$ CO lines show relatively low excitation, and the CO(8--7)/CO(3--2) ratio correlates with star-formation rate surface density, suggesting that molecular gas excitation is primarily regulated by star formation. Lower-mass galaxies show excess molecular gas fractions and depletion times relative to field scaling relations. By combining quiescent galaxy samples in the same field, we identify a sequence from gas-rich systems with low stellar surface density to gas-poor, compact systems with high stellar surface density. These results suggest that massive galaxy evolution in dense environments is governed by a baryon cycle linking gas supply, star formation, and structural transformation within the cosmic web environment.

</details>


### [178] [Fractional Bose-Einstein Condensate Dark Matter Cores: Numerical GPP Solutions and SPARC Rotation Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06763)
*Akila Labadi,Mohamed Benarous,Ahmed Hocine*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了分数玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚暗物质模型，其中超轻玻色子成分形成自引力核心，并嵌入以冷暗物质为主的晕中；通过数值求解超越托马斯-费米近似的定态GPP系统，得到能量束缚的平衡构型，并成功拟合了30个SPARC星系的旋转曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种新的暗物质模型——分数玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（BEC）暗物质，以解释星系尺度上的动力学观测（如旋转曲线），并弥补标准冷暗物质模型在小尺度上的不足。

Method: 构建包含超轻玻色子自引力核心与冷暗物质晕的混合暗物质模型；采用定态Gross-Pitaevskii-Poisson（GPP）方程组进行数值求解，且不依赖托马斯-费米（TF）近似。

Result: 获得了能量上束缚的稳定平衡构型，并对30个SPARC星系的观测旋转曲线实现了良好拟合。

Conclusion: 分数BEC暗物质模型是一种可行的暗物质候选方案，其理论预测与星系尺度观测一致，支持超轻玻色子作为暗物质成分的可能性。

Abstract: We investigate a fractional Bose Einstein condensate dark matter scenario in which an ultralight bosonic component forms a self gravitating core embedded in a predominantly cold dark matter halo. The stationary GPP system solved numerically beyond the TF approximation leads to energetically bound equilibrium configurations and good phenomenological fits to the selected rotation curves from 30 SPARC galaxies.

</details>


### [179] [A Unified Magnetohydrodynamic Scaling Relation for the Multiphase Interstellar Medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06900)
*Mengke Zhao,Guang-Xing Li,Keping Qiu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过整合脉冲星和塞曼观测数据，构建了跨越十个数量级密度范围的统一磁场-密度关系数据集，发现磁场演化由阿尔文马赫数主导，并提出了一个渐进过渡模型来解释磁场与密度之间的连续关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往由于观测数据不足，磁场-密度关系只能用分段幂律描述，存在理论与观测脱节的问题；本文旨在通过更全面的数据揭示其连续物理本质。

Method: 整合脉冲星（低密度）和Zeeman（高密度）观测数据，构建统一磁场-密度关系；引入阿尔文马赫数作为组织参数，提出‘渐进过渡’模型并检验其三个可验证预测。

Result: 观测数据支持以阿尔文马赫数为标度的连续磁场演化：低密度区磁主导（MA<1），高密度区动能主导（MA>1）；模型预测的指数β≈0.15–0.21与理论值0.147吻合；致密气体拟合外推准确覆盖弥散脉冲星数据；磁场能谱在大尺度趋近k^{-5/3}，小尺度因引力压缩而偏离。

Conclusion: 传统‘断开幂律’只是连续磁状态方程的分段近似；其拟合转折密度可能物理对应引力主导运动开始的临界点，即跨阿尔文平衡点。

Abstract: The interplay of magnetic fields, turbulence, and gravity governs the structural evolution of the interstellar medium (ISM) and the initial conditions of star formation, yet observational gaps have long enforced a broken power-law description of the magnetic field--density relation. Here we assemble a unified dataset spanning ten orders of magnitude in density ($10^{-26}$--$10^{-16}\,\mathrm{g\,cm^{-3}}$) by combining pulsar and Zeeman observations. The unified data are consistent with a continuous magnetic-field evolution organised by the Alfvén Mach number $\mathcal{M}_{\rm A}=\sqrt{E_K/E_B}$. Within this interpretation, the low-density gas is magnetically dominated ($\mathcal{M}_{\rm A}<1$), whereas the high-density gas becomes kinetically dominated ($\mathcal{M}_{\rm A}>1$) as gravity increasingly contributes to the kinetic-energy budget, with magnetic tension continuing to influence the collapse geometry. Within the Gradual Transition interpretation, the empirical break density traces the vicinity of the trans-Alfvénic equipartition point, $\mathcal{M}_{\rm A}=1$. This Gradual Transition model makes three predictions tested here. Its exponent and background field are fixed in advance by turbulent physics and recovered by the fits ($β\approx0.15$--$0.21$ against a predicted $0.147$; $B_c\approx2.0\,μ$G). A dense-gas fit, extrapolated blindly across four decades, passes through the diffuse pulsar data. And, under the adopted scale mappings, the implied magnetic-energy spectrum approaches a $k^{-5/3}$-like scaling on large scales and departs from this extrapolation on small scales, where gravitational compression amplifies the field. The broken power law can therefore be viewed as a piecewise approximation to the continuous magnetic equation of state, with its fitted transition density potentially retaining a physical connection to the onset of gravity-driven motions.

</details>


### [180] [Catching Up with the Fastest Star in the Galaxy: A Two-Passage GRAVITY+ Campaign to Detect S301's Schwarzschild Precession](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06936)
*Joseph Catanzarite*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用GRAVITY+观测S301恒星在2031.8年和2040.5年近心点通过期间的天体测量数据，以探测广义相对论预言的轨道进动效应。研究设计了最优观测策略，构建了包含光传播延迟的七参数轨道模型，并评估了系统误差，最终预测可在31σ置信水平上检测到0.0074 deg/yr的进动率，实现对广义相对论的自旋无关检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证广义相对论在强引力场（如银河系中心超大质量黑洞Sgr A*附近）下的Schwarzschild轨道进动效应，且不依赖黑洞自旋假设。

Method: 基于GRAVITY观测能力，构建相对论性光变曲线模型；设计131次观测的Fisher最优时间采样方案（含近心点观测下限）；拟合含光传播延迟的七参数轨道；综合建模白噪声、每次运行的校准偏移、Sgr A*光度中心漂移等系统误差，并对质量、距离和参考零点进行边缘化处理。

Result: 在207微角秒白噪声下，拟合得到进动率为0.2324 ± 0.0023 deg/yr；计入真实系统误差后，剩余可检测进动率为0.0074 deg/yr，达31σ显著性；太阳遮挡导致首次近心点观测缺失153天，但不影响进动率精度。

Conclusion: 该GRAVITY+观测计划有望以高显著性实现对广义相对论在强场下的无自旋假设检验，是未来十年内极具前景的相对论天体物理实验。

Abstract: S301 is the fastest known star in the Galaxy. It orbits Sgr A* every 8.68 yr on an e = 0.9832 orbit that carries it within 136 Schwarzschild radii at 25,600 km/s (GRAVITY Collaboration 2026). An orbit like that must precess, by the same Schwarzschild effect that turns Mercury's perihelion, whatever the spin of the black hole. We ask whether an astrometry-only GRAVITY+ campaign covering the 2031.8 and 2040.5 periapsis passages can catch it. We validate a relativistic light-curve model, design a 131-epoch Fisher-optimal cadence with a periapsis floor, fit a seven-parameter orbit including the light-travel-time delay, and build a systematic error budget. With white noise at the 207 microarcseconds achieved on S301, the fit recovers the injected rate, 0.2307 deg/yr, as 0.2324 +/- 0.0023 deg/yr (+/-0.0012 at the forecast 100 microarcseconds). Real astrometric noise is not white. We add per-run calibration offsets and a wandering Sgr A* photocentre at GRAVITY's published amplitudes, marginalize over the reference zero point, mass and distance, add the bounded systematics in quadrature, and inflate by the excess scatter of real GRAVITY orbit fits. What survives is 0.0074 deg/yr, a 31 sigma detection. The Sun hides the first periapsis for 153 d, at a cost to the period and periapsis epoch but not to the precession rate. Radial velocities are out of reach at S301's magnitude; photometry only validates the light-curve model. The spin of Sgr A* could shift the rate by 5.17-5.74 per cent and cannot be bounded, so the campaign is a spin-agnostic test of general relativity, and a forecast until the star comes round.

</details>


### [181] [Between Little Red Dots and Star-Forming Galaxies: A Sequence in the Optical Continuum Slope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07025)
*Kristiphong Boonmee,Akio K. Inoue,Masafusa Onoue,Xingyao Cai*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST的NIRSpec/PRISM光谱数据，在β_UV-β_opt平面上分析约3500个高红移星系，发现‘小红点’（LRDs）与普通恒星形成星系之间并无明确分界，而存在一条连续的垂直序列，暗示LRDs可能是黑洞主导型星系（BH*）与宿主星系贡献比例渐变的极端端点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验‘小红点’（LRDs）是否构成一个与普通恒星形成星系有本质边界的独立天体类别。

Method: 基于DAWN JWST档案中~3500个z≈3–8星系的NIRSpec/PRISM光谱，拟合其紫外到光学连续谱为幂律，构建β_UV–β_opt分布图；引入黑洞星（BH*）模板（源自‘The Cliff’候选体）与恒星形成谱的线性混合模型，估算黑洞-恒星质量比。

Result: 发现一条从恒星形成星系延伸至LRD区域的连续垂直序列，中间段（-1≲β_opt≲0）包含约50个被忽视的过渡候选体；其堆叠图像显示形态介于点源LRD与延展星系之间；LRD端推得黑洞-恒星质量比η≈0.006–0.1。

Conclusion: LRDs并非孤立类别，而是BH*与宿主星系相对贡献连续变化的致密极端；建议将-1≲β_opt≲0作为搜寻过渡天体的实用选择窗口。

Abstract: The "Little Red Dots" (LRDs) uncovered by JWST are typically analyzed as a distinct class of objects; however, whether they are separated from ordinary star-forming galaxies by a genuine boundary has not been tested at the population level. Using a sample of $\sim3,500$ galaxies at $3 \lesssim z \lesssim 8$ with NIRSpec/PRISM spectra from the DAWN JWST Archive, we modeled their rest-frame UV-to-optical continuum emission with power laws and located the sources in the $β_{\rm{UV}}$-$β_{\rm{opt}}$ plane. Alongside a dense locus of star-forming galaxies and a diagonal sequence which can be explained by dust reddening and/or quiescence, we identify a vertical sequence extending from the locus of star-forming galaxies into the LRD region. This sequence is smoothly and continuously populated, with no clear boundary between the two populations. Its middle section, $-1 \lesssim β_{\rm{opt}} \lesssim 0$, hosts an intermediate population of $\sim50$ sources that falls outside the LRD selection boundary and has been largely overlooked; NIRCam imaging shows their rest-frame UV and optical median stacked images lie between point-like LRDs and extended star-forming galaxies. We modeled the vertical sequence as linear mixtures of stacked star-forming spectra and a black hole star (BH*) template derived from "The Cliff", one of the most compelling BH* candidates, and we infer black hole-to-stellar mass ratios $η= M_{\rm{BH}}/M_* \approx 0.006-0.1$ at the LRD end. We propose $-1 \lesssim β_{\rm{opt}} \lesssim 0$ as a practical selection window for possible transition candidates and argue that LRDs may be the compact, BH*-dominated extreme of a continuous population in which the relative contributions of the host and BH* vary smoothly.

</details>


### [182] [Recovering the characteristic halo mass of rare populations from clustering: a benchmark using the Millennium Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07040)
*Amrita Banerjee,Darren Croton*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用Millennium模拟数据，通过仅依赖聚类测量来恢复稀有大质量暗物质晕的特征质量，并评估其准确性。研究发现，特征质量的恢复精度中位数绝对偏差为0.103 dex，不确定性随样本数量增加而显著下降，但参考样本自身误差会系统性传播，限制最终精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估仅用聚类观测（如交叉相关）能否准确恢复稀有大质量天体（如暗物质晕）的特征质量，为后续引入星系-晕关联等复杂因素提供基准。

Method: 使用Millennium数值模拟中的暗物质晕，构建不同大小的稀有晕样本（25–10000个）与参考晕样本（5000–50000个），在6个红移（0 ≤ z ≤ 5.29）下计算其交叉相关函数；在r = 8 h⁻¹ Mpc处测量偏置并反推特征质量，与模拟中直接得到的平均对数质量对比。

Result: 特征质量恢复的中位数绝对偏差为0.103 dex；不确定性从25个对象时的±0.71 dex降至10000个时的±0.08 dex；参考样本自身误差不可忽略且不随稀有样本增大而减小；最佳配置下，偏置-质量关系校准误差（~0.07 dex）已与测量误差相当。

Conclusion: 仅靠聚类测量可较准确恢复稀有大质量晕的特征质量，但精度受限于参考样本误差和偏置-质量关系校准；增大极小稀有样本收益最大，而进一步增大大样本收效甚微。

Abstract: We measure how accurately the characteristic halo mass of a rare, massive population can be recovered from clustering alone, using dark matter haloes in the Millennium simulation, where the true masses are known. Massive samples of 25 to 10000 haloes are cross-correlated against field reference samples of 5000 to 50000 haloes at six redshifts over $0 \leq z \leq 5.29$. The bias is measured at $r = 8\,h^{-1}\,\mathrm{Mpc}$ and inverted to return a characteristic mass, which we compare with the mean logarithmic halo mass measured directly from the simulation. Across 192 configurations the characteristic mass is recovered with a median absolute offset of $0.103$ dex. We show that (i) accuracy depends only weakly on the number of rare objects while the uncertainty falls from $\pm 0.71$ dex at 25 objects to $\pm 0.08$ dex at 10000; (ii) enlarging the smallest samples gives the greatest return, with a doubling from 25 to 50 objects reducing the uncertainty by about a third, against a tenth from 5000 to 10000; and (iii) the reference population carries an error of its own, which propagates coherently into every cross-correlation measured against it and is not reduced by observing more rare objects. Working with haloes rather than galaxies isolates the clustering measurement from the galaxy--halo connection, providing a benchmark before population-specific complications are introduced. For the best configurations, the uncertainty contributed by the calibration of the bias--mass relation, at $\sim 0.07$ dex, already matches that of the measurement itself.

</details>


### [183] [Irreducible-Mass Balance for Magnetized Null Horizons with an Internal Current Sheet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07449)
*Remo Ruffini,Giorgio Sonnino*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文探讨了非稳态磁化黑洞视界下不可约质量的变化，利用零测地线Raychaudhuri方程建立了一般化的面积（能量）平衡关系，并在弱场慢转近似下复现并深化了Wang的分裂单极Blandford-Znajek机制结果，揭示了不可约质量对旋转功不可逆吸收的物理意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非稳态、轴对称磁化黑洞视界下不可约质量如何演化，特别是当视界不处于定态时，传统稳态能量守恒框架失效，需构建更普适的几何-能量平衡关系。

Method: 基于零测地线Raychaudhuri方程推导适用于任意光滑零视界的精确面积平衡式；引入可积拟局部哈密顿量使其转化为能量平衡；结合Wang模型，在弱场（P/M_H≪1）和慢转（a/M_H≪1）下进行摄动分析，辅以世界体积作用量与幂次计数估计修正项。

Result: 在阻抗匹配条件下，瞬时旋转功一半转化为电磁辐射功率，另一半增加视界面积与不可约质量；明确了该简化BZ轨迹的有效适用范围为O(p²)+O(ε²)修正量级；指出向高自旋外推需额外假设，且视界能量损失需结合无穷远通量计算才能关联到观测能量。

Conclusion: 不可约质量M_irr是刻画黑洞视界不可逆吸积旋转能的关键量；本文建立的非稳态能量平衡框架统一处理了面积增长、转动/电磁功、非稳态聚焦及规范通量修正等效应，为Blandford-Znajek机制提供了更坚实、可推广的几何动力学基础。

Abstract: We begin with a general question: how does the irreducible mass of a magnetized black hole change when the horizon is not stationary? The null Raychaudhuri equation gives an exact area balance for any smooth null horizon, without assuming stationarity or axisymmetry. When the horizon is axisymmetric and admits an integrable quasilocal Hamiltonian, this geometrical identity becomes an energy balance. The equation keeps area growth, rotational and electromagnetic work, nonstationary focusing, canonical-flux corrections, and internal-boundary terms separate. We then turn to Wang's self-gravitating split-monopole Blandford-Znajek engine. In the weak-field and slow-rotation regime, P/M_H\ll1 and a/M_H\ll1, our general balance reproduces Wang's mass- and angular-momentum loss rates. The irreducible mass, M_{irr}=\sqrt{A_H/(16π)}, reveals what those rates do not show by themselves. It measures the part of the rotational work absorbed irreversibly by the horizon. At fixed magnetic flux and under impedance matching, half of the instantaneous rotational work leaves as electromagnetic power. The other half increases the horizon area and M_{irr}. The assumptions behind this reduction are derived explicitly. A minimal nondissipative world-volume action makes the current-sheet contribution vanish through O(p^2ε^2). Direct power counting of Wang's perturbative fields, together with a compatible canonical-flux prescription, gives a relative focusing and canonical correction of O(p^2)+O(ε^2). These estimates mark the range in which the reduced Blandford-Znajek trajectory remains controlled. Extending that trajectory to high spin requires an additional extrapolation. Relating the quasilocal horizon loss to energy measured at infinity requires a separate asymptotic flux calculation.

</details>


### [184] [ALMA Reveals an Explosive Outflow Candidate in IRAS 16119--5048](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07469)
*Yongquan Luo,Jianjun Zhou,Tie Liu,Jarken Esimbek,Siju Zhang,Xindi Tang,Sami Dib,Prasanta Gorai,Mika Juvela,Leonardo Bronfman,Patricio Sanhueza,Jihye Hwang,Fengwei Xu,Kee-Tae Kim,Guido Garay,Chang Won Lee,Tapas Baug,L. Viktor Tóth,Gang Wu,Dalei Li,Yuxin He,Yingxiu Ma,Dongdong Zhou,Toktarkhan Komesh,Weiguang Ji,Dezhao Meng,Jiasheng Li*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过多波段观测研究了大质量恒星形成区IRAS 16119-5048，发现其具有低能量爆发性外流特征，可能由中心致密核的动力学相互作用触发，但尚需更高分辨率观测确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究IRAS 16119-5048这一大质量恒星形成区的外流性质及其驱动机制，尤其是其是否属于罕见的低能量爆发性外流。

Method: 结合ALMA ATOMS Band 3与QUARKS Band 6、ATCA射电连续谱及Spitzer中红外数据，采用CO(2-1)谱线成像、dendrogram速度通道分析、位置-位置-速度空间连接、致密核识别与碎裂模型比对等方法。

Result: 识别出16条汇聚于中心的高速流束结构；外流动能偏低但质量加载和动量率偏高；发现27个致密核沿碎裂丝状结构分布，呈现质量分聚；壳层结构暗示H II区反馈影响流束形态。

Conclusion: IRAS 16119-5048是潜在的低能量爆发性外流候选体，可能源于中心致密核的动力学相互作用，但复杂速度结构和单核外流污染使结论尚不确凿，需更高灵敏度与角分辨率观测进一步验证。

Abstract: We present a multiwavelength study of the massive star formation region IRAS 16119-5048 (I16119) using ALMA ATOMS Band 3 and QUARKS Band 6 observations, complemented by archival ATCA radio continuum and Spitzer mid-infrared data. The CO (2-1) emission reveals a system of high-velocity streamer-like structures around the central region. Using dendrogram analysis of velocity-channel maps followed by linking in position-position-velocity space, we identify 16 approximately radially distributed streamers whose projected trajectories converge toward a common central region. The kinetic energy of the outflows is at least an order of magnitude lower than those of most known explosive outflows, while their mass entrainment and momentum rates are high compared with typical protostellar outflows, suggesting that I16119 may represent a low-energy explosive outflow candidate. Dense-gas and photodissociation-region tracers reveal shell-like structures associated with the 8 $μ$m emission, indicating that feedback from the H II region may influence the streamer morphology. The 1.3 mm continuum resolves 27 dense cores along a fragmented filamentary structure. Their separations are consistent with thermal Jeans or cylindrical fragmentation, while the collect-and-collapse scenario is unsupported. The dense cores also show evidence of mass segregation, with the most massive cores concentrated near the inferred explosive centre. We suggest that I16119 is a plausible low-energy explosive outflow candidate, possibly triggered by dynamical interactions among centrally concentrated massive cores. However, the complex velocity structure and possible contamination from individual core-driven outflows prevent a definitive classification. More sensitive, higher angular-resolution observations are required to confirm the nature of the outflow.

</details>


### [185] [A new clump-based star formation model for galaxy simulations: implications for high-redshift compact star clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07635)
*Shu Horie,Hidenobu Yajima*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的星系模拟中的恒星形成模型，通过实时识别气体团块并依据其面密度设定恒星形成效率，成功再现了高红移致密盘中大质量团块、爆发式恒星形成及束缚星团的形成，并与JWST观测结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有星系模拟中恒星形成模型过于简化，难以准确刻画不同红移下气体团块演化与恒星形成效率的变化；需基于物理机制（如辐射流体模拟）校准，以更好解释高红移致密星系中观测到的团块和星团特征。

Method: 开发一种新星形成模型：在数值模拟中实时识别自引力束缚的气体团块，其恒星形成效率ε_SF由团块面密度Σ_c决定，并经辐射流体动力学模拟校准；将其应用于不同质量暗晕（10^9–10^11 M⊙）和红移（z=0–10）对应的孤立盘星系模拟。

Result: 致密盘形成更大更密的团块（最大质量达10^7 M⊙），团块面密度>300 M⊙ pc⁻²的比例随红移升高；恒星形成更爆发性，全局效率从z=0时的~0.005增至z=10时的~0.2–0.4；反馈可瓦解气体盘，激发螺旋臂；仅在致密盘中形成束缚星团，z≥8时束缚星团质量占比达40–70%，与JWST对高红移团块星系的观测一致。

Conclusion: 恒星形成效率应依赖于局部团块面密度而非全局气体密度；高红移致密盘是束缚星团形成的关键环境；该模型为理解早期星系中星团起源及JWST观测提供了可靠理论框架。

Abstract: We develop a new star formation model for galaxy simulations in which star-forming gas clumps are identified on-the-fly and converted into stars with an efficiency $ε_{\rm SF}$ set by the clump surface density $Σ_{\rm c}$, calibrated against radiation hydrodynamics simulations of star cluster formation. Applying this model to isolated disc galaxies embedded in haloes of $M_{\rm h}=10^{9}$, $10^{10}$ and $10^{11}\,\mathrm{M_\odot}$, with disc compactness corresponding to redshift $z=0$--$10$, we find that more compact discs form more massive and denser clumps. The maximum clump mass increases from $\sim 10^{5}$ to $\sim 10^{7}\,\mathrm{M_\odot}$, and the fraction of clumps exceeding the surface density of $300\,\mathrm{M_\odot\,pc^{-2}}$ rises from $0.25$ to $0.38$ between $z=0$ and $10$ in $10^{10}\,\mathrm{M_\odot}$ haloes. Star formation becomes correspondingly bursty, and the global star formation efficiency after three disc rotations increases from $5 \times 10^{-3}$ to $2 \times 10^{-1}$ in $10^{10}\,\mathrm{M_\odot}$ haloes and from $2 \times 10^{-2}$ to $4 \times 10^{-1}$ in $10^{11}\,\mathrm{M_\odot}$ haloes as the redshift increases from $z=0$ to $10$. The accompanying feedback disrupts the gas discs of the most compact systems, while prominent stellar spiral arms emerge. Self-gravitationally bound star clusters form only in compact discs. We find no bound star clusters at $z \leq 2$ in $10^{10}\,\mathrm{M_\odot}$ haloes, whereas the bound cluster mass fraction reaches $\approx 40$ per cent at $z \geq 8$ and $\approx 70$ per cent in the most compact $10^{11}\,\mathrm{M_\odot}$ halo. These fractions are broadly consistent with those inferred for high-$z$ clumpy galaxies observed by the James Webb Space Telescope.

</details>


### [186] [Improving constraints on the Yukawa correction at the Galactic Center with multiple stellar orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07693)
*GRAVITY+ Collaboration,:,A. Foschi,K. Abd El Dayem,N. Aimar,A. Berdeu,J. -P. Berger,G. Bourdarot,W. Brandner,Y. Cao,C. Correia,S. Cueves Cardona,R. Davies,D. Defrère,F. Delplancke-Ströbele,A. Drescher,F. Eisenhauer,L. Esteras~Otal,M. Fabricius,H. Feuchtgruber,S. Flesch,N. M. Förster Schreiber,Q. Fournier,P. Garcia,R. Garcia Lopez,R. Genzel,S. Gillessen,F. Gonté,X. Haubois,S. F. Hönig,M. Houllé,S. Joharle,J. Kammerer,A. Kaufer,P. Kervella,L. Kreidberg,L. Labadie,S. Lacour,O. Lai,R. Laugier,J. -B. Le Bouquin,J. Leftley,R. Li,B. Lopez,D. Lutz,F. Mang,A. Mérand,F. Millour,M. Montargès,N. Morujão,H. Nowacki,M. Nowak,J. Osorno,T. Ott,S. Pappert,C. Paladini,T. Paumard,K. Perraut,G. Perrin,R. Petrov,N. Pourré,S. Rabien,D. C. Ribeiro,S. Robbe-Dubois,M. Sadun Bordoni,J. Sanchez-Bermudez,J. Sauter,J. Scigliuto,J. Shangguan,T. T. Shimizu,F. Soulez,S. Spezzano,C. Straubmeier,E. Sturm,M. Subroweit,C. Sykes,L. J. Tacconi,P. Thévenet,I. Urso,F. H. Vincent,J. Woillez,G. Zins*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用多颗恒星（特别是新发现的S301）轨道数据，对银河系中心牛顿引力的Yukawa修正进行约束，显著提升了短程和长程尺度上的限制精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进仅用S2恒星轨道对Yukawa型引力修正的约束，拓展探测尺度范围，尤其是S2无法覆盖的短程区域。

Method: 采用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法，联合拟合S2、S55、S29、S38和S301五颗恒星1992–2025年的GRAVITY、GRAVITY+、NACO与SINFONI观测数据（天体测量与光谱）。

Result: 在λ≈200 AU处获得迄今最紧的|α| < 6×10⁻⁴约束（比仅用S2提升约5倍）；S301使短程约束延伸至λ=10¹² m（|α|<0.004）和5×10¹¹ m（|α|<0.006）；S29在λ≈2×10¹⁵ m处给出|α|<0.1，为当前超大质量黑洞周围该尺度下最严限制。

Conclusion: 多恒星联合分析显著增强对Yukawa引力修正的探测能力，尤其填补了S2单独无法覆盖的短程与长程空白，为检验超越广义相对论的新引力理论提供了更强观测约束。

Abstract: We investigate the presence of a Yukawa-like correction ($\propto \, αe^{- r/λ}$) to Newtonian gravity at the Galactic Center, using a multi-star fitting code, including the newly discovered star S$301$, to improve the constraints obtained using S$2$ orbit alone.
  We perform a Markov Chain Monte Carlo analysis using the astrometric and spectroscopic data of stars S$2$, S$55$, S$29$, S$38$ and S$301$ collected by GRAVITY, GRAVITY$^+$, NACO and SINFONI instruments, covering the period from $1992$ to $2025$.
  Compared to GRAVITY Collaboration 2025 (Paper I), in which only S$2$ was fitted, the tightest bound on the Yukawa coupling is again reached at $λ= 3\cdot10^{13}\,\rm m\ (\sim 200\,AU)$, where we find $|α| < 6 \cdot10^{-4}$, an improvement of roughly a factor five. The addition of S$301$ extends the constraint to short ranges that were inaccessible to S$2$, yielding $|α| < 0.004$ at $λ=10^{12}\,\rm m$ and $|α| < 0.006$ at $λ=5\cdot 10^{11}\,\rm m$, while S$29$, with its larger apoapsis, gives $|α| < 0.1$ at $λ\sim 2\cdot10^{15}\,\rm m$. The latter two represent the tightest limits at these scale lengths on a Yukawa correction obtained to date around a supermassive black hole.

</details>


### [187] [The MICADO first light imager for the ELT: Simulated Observations of Star Forming Clumps at Cosmic Noon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07824)
*Matteo Simioni,Carmelo Arcidiacono,Andrea Grazian,Marco Gullieuszik,Elisa Portaluri,Alvio Renzini,Benedetta Vulcani,Anita Zanella,Francesca Annibali,Michele Cirasuolo,Richard Davies,Natascha Förster Schreiber,Johanna Hartke,Hanindyo Kuncarayakti,Matteo Messa,Stefan Raffetseder,Letizia Scaloni,Elena Valenti,Eros Vanzella,Joël Vernet,Roland Wagner*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文评估了欧洲南方天文台极大望远镜（ELT）首光仪器MICADO在不依赖引力透镜的情况下，对红移z=2星系中致密星团（clumps）进行表征的能力，表明其可在紫外静止参考系下探测暗至M_UV ~ -15、有效半径低至~20 pc的星团，并强调精确点扩散函数（PSF）重建对小尺度（<40 pc）星团研究的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对z~2–6星系中致密星团的研究严重依赖强引力透镜，但该方法引入模型依赖性误差且样本受限；亟需发展对普遍存在的非透镜z≥2星系开展高分辨（~100 pc以下）、高灵敏度观测的新手段。

Method: 基于理想化z=2恒星形成星系模型构建星团，生成MICADO在宽波段与窄波段（利用星团强发射线）成像的模拟观测数据，并评估其探测极限、表征能力及视场效率。

Result: MICADO在最优条件下可探测UV静止参考系星等达M_UV ~ -15、有效半径低至R_e ~ 20 pc的星团；其约1平方角分视场支持多目标高效巡天；但PSF重建精度显著影响尤其是<40 pc尺度星团的探测与表征。

Conclusion: MICADO将填补关键观测空白，使非透镜z=2星系中星团的系统性研究成为可能，从而深化对宇宙正午时期星团形成与演化机制的理解。

Abstract: Galaxies at redshift z$\sim$2-6 exhibit prominent star-forming regions (clumps). Characterisation of these galactic structures requires both high sensitivity and spatial resolution of $\sim$100 pc or less. Currently, these spatial scales are achievable only with the aid of strong gravitational lensing. However, lensing introduces model-dependent uncertainties and limits the sample to galaxies behind massive clusters. The upcoming 40m-class telescopes, such as the ESO Extremely Large Telescope (ELT), will enable routine studies of clumps in ubiquitous, non-lensed z$\ge$2 galaxies. We assess the capability of MICADO, the first-light imager and spectrograph of the ELT, to characterise clumps in non-lensed z=2 galaxies. Specifically, we focus on two representative observing scenarios, providing the basis for an early scientific exploitation of the instrument. We modelled clumps in an idealised z=2 star-forming galaxy and produced mock MICADO observations in both broad- and narrow-band imaging. The latter leverages the strong emission lines typical of clumps to improve detection and characterisation. Reaching UV rest-frame magnitudes of clumps as faint as M$_{UV} \sim -15$ in optimal observing conditions, MICADO will be able to efficiently characterise clumps in non-lensed z=2 galaxies down to R$_{e} \sim 20$ pc. Moreover, the full MICADO $\sim$1 arcmin$^2$ field of view enables efficient surveys of multiple targets. This study demonstrates MICADO's potential to fill a crucial observational gap, shedding new light on clump formation and evolution at cosmic noon. Accurate PSF reconstruction will be crucial to fully harness this capability as it significantly impacts clump detection and characterisation, especially at the smallest sizes ($<$40 pc).

</details>


### [188] [A Dusty Quenching-candidate AGN host at z=5.7: massive quiescent galaxies may quench already during the dust-obscured phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07825)
*Francesco D'Eugenio,Matilde Brazzini,Roberto Maiolino,Elena Bertola,Stefano Carniani,Xihan Ji,Ignas Juodžbalis,Yixiao Liu,Minjung Park,Eleonora Parlanti,Robert G. Pascalau,Gabriele Pezzulli,Jan Scholtz,Sandro Tacchella,Stefano Zibetti*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文对高红移（z=5.7）尘埃遮蔽的宽线类星体RUBIES-EGS-9809进行了光谱-测光分析，发现其宿主星系已具备较老恒星种群，同时仍处于致密、尘埃浓厚阶段；结合强吸积、外流和高消光特征，提出AGN反馈可能在约1亿年内驱动快速熄灭，并通过‘尘埃路径’解释高红移缺乏中等年龄（50–100 Myr）过渡态星系的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释高红移（z=3–7）宇宙中，为何难以观测到连接尘埃遮蔽星暴与经典宁静星系之间的中等年龄（50–100 Myr）熄灭过渡相，即‘缺失环节’问题。

Method: 基于多波段测光与光谱数据（含Balmer break分辨、NaI吸收线、[O III]外流、X射线Compton厚特性等），开展SED建模、恒星质量/黑洞质量/光度估计，并结合外流与消光特征推断反馈机制与熄灭时标。

Result: 确认该z=5.7 AGN宿主具有高消光（A_V > 3 mag）、热/冷尘辐射、Compton厚X射线吸收、电离与中性气体外流；恒星质量log(M*/M⊙)=10.7±0.3，L_bol≈4×10⁴⁶ erg/s，黑洞质量log(M_BH/M⊙)≈8.1；Balmer break分辨表明恒星种群已年老，但星系仍高度遮蔽；熄灭过程很可能由AGN反馈主导，而非星暴驱动。

Conclusion: 提出了‘尘埃路径’（dusty path）熄灭模型：AGN反馈在~100 Myr内快速终止恒星形成，而尘埃尚未清除，掩盖了本应明亮的紫外熄灭相；待尘埃消散时，短寿命紫外星已衰减，直接呈现为经典宁静星系——从而自然解释高红移处中等年龄熄灭星系的缺失。

Abstract: We present a spectro-photometric analysis of RUBIES-EGS-9809, a broad-line AGN host at z=5.7 with a Balmer break and a dominant point source. The source is dust reddened ($A_V>$3 mag), shows hot- and cold-dust emission, and is X-ray Compton thick, with X-ray-to-bolometric ratio consistent with luminous AGN. The large $A_V$ agrees with strong NaI absorption (EW=17$\pm 2 Å$), possibly from a neutral-gas outflow (3 $σ$), while broad [O III] traces an ionized outflow, suggesting AGN feedback may be affecting the host. We estimate a stellar mass log(M*/M$_\odot$)=10.7$\pm$0.3, Lbol$\sim4\times10^{46}$ erg s$^{-1}$ , and black-hole mass log(M$_{BH}/M_\odot)\sim8.1$ (subject to large systematics). The resolved Balmer break (0.1 arcsec, 0.6 kpc) is consistent with a stellar origin, implying the galaxy is already old in stars while still heavily obscured. This shows that massive galaxies can host evolved stellar populations during their compact, dust-obscured phase, consistent with formation in an earlier dusty starburst. SED modelling suggests a declining SFR, but low-resolution spectroscopy alone cannot constrain the recent SFR, so we treat the source as a strong quenching candidate rather than a secure post-starburst system. Because quenching follows the decline in SFR, star-formation-driven outflows are disfavoured, leaving AGN feedback as a plausible driver. We speculate that such rapid feedback ($\sim$100 Myr timescale) may precipitate quenching while the post-quenching phase stays dust-enshrouded, concealing the UV-bright quenched phase that would otherwise be easily detected, if dust free. By the time dust clears, the UV-luminous stars may have already dwindled, leaving a classic dust-free quiescent galaxy. This 'dusty path' to quiescence may explain the lack of intermediate-age quenched progenitors (ages 50-100 Myr) linking dusty starbursts to massive quiescent systems at z=3-7.

</details>


### [189] [A New Sample of $\sim$ 100 Intermediate-mass Black Holes Reaching $z \approx 1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07836)
*Wen-Juan Liu,Luis C. Ho,Su Yao,Xiao-Bo Dong,Yaqi Zhao*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用DESI DR1光谱数据系统搜寻红移0.5 < z ≲ 1的中等质量黑洞（IMBH）活动星系核（AGN），发现98个宽线IMBH AGN，揭示其在高红移时具有更高吸积率和更显著的电离气体外流特征，表明IMBH可在宇宙较晚时期仍经历快速甚至超爱丁顿增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展对中等质量黑洞（IMBH）AGN的系统性搜寻至中等红移（0.5 < z ≲ 1），以探究其吸积状态与电离气体运动学随宇宙时间的演化，从而约束IMBH的成长路径与种子黑洞晚期演化。

Method: 基于DESI DR1光谱，通过定量光谱分解与宽Hβ发射线筛选，识别出98个黑洞质量M_BH < 10^6 M⊙的宽线IMBH AGN，并对比z<0.6样本，在匹配黑洞质量和爱丁顿比后分析[O III]谱线轮廓与蓝移翼成分等动力学特征。

Result: 发现0.5<z<1样本相比低红移样本具有更高的宽Hβ光度、更高的爱丁顿比（1.2–9.0）、更宽且更强蓝移[O III]翼成分；这些差异在质量与爱丁顿比匹配后依然存在。

Conclusion: IMBH AGN在z<1表现出两类演化特征：一是向高红移时可达更极端（含超爱丁顿）吸积态，二是电离气体运动学系统变化；说明种子黑洞可在宇宙黎明之后持续快速成长，并暗示其周围气体环境与外流活动随时间演化。

Abstract: We present a systematic search for intermediate-mass black hole (IMBH) active galactic nuclei (AGNs) at $0.5 < z \lesssim 1$ using DESI DR1 spectroscopy.We identify 98 broad-line IMBH AGNs with black hole masses $M_\mathrm{BH}<10^6$ $M_{\odot}$ through quantitative spectral decomposition and broad-H$β$ selection. This sample spans $M_\mathrm{BH}=10^{5.5}-10^{6.0}$ $M_{\odot}$ and Eddington ratios from 1.2 to 9.0 extending systematic IMBH AGN searches to intermediate redshift. Compared with a consistently selected $z<0.6$ IMBH sample, the $0.5<z<1$ sources reach higher broad-H$β$ luminosities and substantially higher Eddington ratios, indicating more extreme accretion states at earlier cosmic times. They also show broader [O III] profiles and stronger blueshifted wing components, with these kinematic differences persisting after matching in black hole mass and Eddington ratio. These results reveal two distinct signatures of evolution among IMBH AGNs at $z<1$: ability of IMBHs to reach increasingly extreme accretion states toward higher redshift, and systematic changes in their ionized-gas kinematics. The former demonstrates that rapid, including super-Eddington, growth of IMBHs can persist to relatively late cosmic times, while the latter may indicate evolution in the ionized-gas environment and associated outflow activity of actively growing IMBHs. Together, these findings provide new constraints on the evolutionary pathways of IMBHs at $z<1$, and show that seed-mass black holes can continue to undergo rapid growth well after the cosmic dawn.

</details>


### [190] [LEGGOS: A Shocking Lack of Evidence for Shocks at sub-kiloparsec Scales at 2 < z < 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07850)
*Nikko J. Cleri,Taylor A. Hutchison,Brian Welch,Gourav Khullar,Matthew B. Bayliss,Håkon Dahle,Michael Florian,Michael D. Gladders,Connor Luettgenau,Rion Oh,Jane R. Rigby,T. Emil Rivera-Thorsen,Julissa Sarmiento,Keren Sharon*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST/NIRSpec对六个红移2.37–3.625的引力透镜星系进行系统搜寻，未发现显著激波证据，表明激波在宇宙正午时期星系演化中可能并非主导机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统探测高红移星系中激波的存在，以理解其在星系演化（尤其是宇宙正午时期）中的作用。

Method: 基于LEGGOS巡天的JWST/NIRSpec积分场光谱，使用Hη、[O iii]、[O i]、Hα、[N ii]、[S ii]等激波敏感发射线的流量比和运动学诊断，并结合MAPPINGS V激波模型推断激波速度。

Result: 在像素、团块及星系整体尺度上均未发现显著激波信号；<8%的像平面像素落入激波诊断区域，且无空间相干性；推断的激波速度分布与激波主导气体不符。

Conclusion: 六例LEGGOS源中均无显著激波证据，暗示激波在该红移范围星系演化中可能不占主导地位。

Abstract: Here we present the first systematic search for shocks in six gravitationally lensed galaxies at 2.37 < z < 3.625 with JWST/NIRSpec integral field spectroscopy from the LEnsing and Galaxy Growth: Observing Substructures (LEGGOS) survey. We employ diagnostics that utilize the fluxes and kinematics of shock-sensitive rest-frame optical emission lines H\b{eta}, [O iii] λ5008, [O i] λ6302, Hα, [N ii] λ6585, and [S ii] λλ6718, 6733. We find that, on pixel, clump, and galaxy-integrated scales, the LEGGOS spectra show minimal if any evidence for shocks. The image plane pixels are < 8% within the shock regions of the rest-frame optical line ratio diagnostics for any individual galaxy, and the shock-identified pixels do not show a coherent spatial structure. We also leverage MAPPINGS V shock models to infer shock velocities from the observed emission lines, and find that the distributions of inferred shock velocities are inconsistent with those expected for shock-dominated gas. Altogether, none of these methods provide significant evidence for shocks in the six LEGGOS sources. We conclude by discussing the implications of the lack of evidence for shocks in the broader context of galaxy evolution at cosmic noon and earlier epochs.

</details>


### [191] [LEGGOS: Direct abundances of N, O, Ne, S, and Ar in five lensed galaxies at Cosmic Noon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07851)
*Brian Welch,Gourav Khullar,Taylor A. Hutchison,Keren Sharon,Pedram Abedi,Matthew B. Bayliss,Michael Florian,Dylan Berry,Jacqueline Antwi-Danso,Nikko J. Cleri,Håkon Dahle,Aleena Ebey Panzer,Michael D. Gladders,Rion Oh,Cole Panzer,Jane R. Rigby,T. Emil Rivera-Thorsen*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST/NIRSpec数据，对宇宙正午时期（z=2.48–3.63）五个强引力透镜星系进行了直接电子温度（Te）法化学丰度测量，发现O、N、Ne、S、Ar丰度总体符合本地与高红移星系趋势，S/O和Ar/O偏低支持核心坍缩超新星主导的金属增丰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高红移星系中恒星形成与金属增丰历史，特别是区分核心坍缩超新星（CCSNe）与Ia型超新星（SNIa）对气体金属丰度的相对贡献。

Method: 基于JWST/NIRSpec光谱，采用直接Te方法测量五颗强透镜星系（z≈2.5–3.6）的O、N、S、Ar、Ne等元素丰度；结合多电离区Te测量验证温度一致性；联合恒星形成历史建模分析增丰机制。

Result: 测得12+log(O/H)在8.04–8.79之间（约0.22–1.26 Z⊙）；N/O和Ne/O符合本地趋势，未见显著氮增丰（仅最年轻星系SGAS-J1050略高）；S/O和Ar/O偏低，支持CCSNe主导增丰；恒星形成历史显示近≤100 Myr活跃恒星形成，SNIa尚未显著贡献。

Conclusion: 宇宙正午时期的这些星系气体金属丰度主要由核心坍缩超新星驱动，其化学演化阶段仍早于Ia型超新星显著贡献时期，支持短时标恒星形成与快速金属增丰模型。

Abstract: We present direct $T_e$ chemical abundances in five strongly lensed galaxies at Cosmic Noon ($2.481 \leq z \leq 3.625$) using JWST/NIRSpec data from the LEGGOS survey. We measure gas-phase abundances of N, O, S, and Ar in all five galaxies, and Ne in two galaxies. Three galaxies have electron temperature constraints from multiple different ionization zones, and we find that these are broadly consistent with temperature scaling relations observed in both local and high-$z$ galaxies. We find a range of oxygen abundances $8.04 \leq 12+\log(\text{O/H}) \leq 8.79$ (22 -- 126\% $Z_{\odot}$). The ratios of N/O and Ne/O are consistent with trends observed in local galaxies. We do not observe any evidence for significant N enhancement in our sample, though the youngest galaxy in our sample (SGAS-J1050) has a mildly elevated $\log(\text{N/O}) = 1.20 \pm 0.08$, which we suggest may be driven by a population of young massive stars. The ratios of S/O and Ar/O are generally sub-solar, similar to trends observed in other high-$z$ galaxies. We find that the S/O and Ar/O abundances are sub-solar, consistent with enrichment from core-collapse supernovae (CCSNe). Modeling of the star formation histories of the LEGGOS galaxies supports CCSNe enrichment, as each galaxy shows a recent period of star formation lasting $\lesssim 100$ Myr, indicating that type Ia supernovae would not yet have had enough time to contribute significantly to the gas-phase abundances of these galaxies.

</details>


### [192] [A pilot submillimeter search for IceCube neutrino counterparts: JCMT follow-up and dusty-galaxy catalog associations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07863)
*Yuji Urata,Kuiyun Huang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过结合JCMT/SCUBA-2对IceCube中微子事件的亚毫米波后随观测与高红移尘埃遮蔽星系（DSFGs）星表交叉比对，发现HerBS星表中4个IceCube定位区存在显著超出预期的亮源（p=0.004），提示亚毫米波可作为中微子电磁对应体的有效探针；ALMA高分辨成像进一步揭示这些源为致密、气体丰富、具有极端动力学和可能掩埋AGN活动的高红移星暴星系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索亚毫米波段作为高能中微子电磁对应体的探测新途径，特别是追踪相对论性暂现源和尘埃遮蔽的高红移星系，并验证其是否满足‘热量计’条件及是否存在掩埋型活动星系核（AGN）。

Method: 1）开展JCMT/SCUBA-2对9个快速响应的IceCube事件进行亚毫米波后随观测；2）交叉比对IceCube定位区与HerBS和PHZ两个高红移尘埃星系星表；3）利用ALMA进行高分辨成像与光谱观测，分析源形态、气体动力学与核活动性质；4）通过方位角随机化与全局显著性校正评估统计显著性。

Result: 1）在9个JCMT观测场中发现3个有前景源，其中'Shadow Blaster'被ALMA证认为z=2.988强引力透镜的尘埃星暴星系；2）HerBS星表中51个历史IceCube事件中有4个定位区含至少1个源，显著超出随机预期（p=0.004，全局p=0.0135）；PHZ星表未达显著；3）4个HerBS关联场共含6个L_IR~10^13 L_sun的高红移DSFGs，ALMA显示其具致密或多组分结构、极端分子气体运动学及潜在掩埋AGN。

Conclusion: 亚毫米波是搜寻高能中微子电磁对应体的有力手段，尤其适用于探测尘埃遮蔽、高红移、气体丰富的星暴系统；建议采用‘宽场筛选+高分辨证认’的层级策略，以识别并确认满足热量计条件及存在 obscured AGN 的候选体。

Abstract: The submillimeter band offers a complementary route to identifying electromagnetic counterparts of high-energy neutrinos, tracing relativistic transients and persistent dust-obscured galaxies. We present a pilot search combining JCMT/SCUBA-2 follow-up of IceCube events with catalog searches for bright high-redshift dusty systems. Among nine rapidly observed IceCube fields, sources of particular interest were found in three: JCMT0402-0424 ("Shadow Blaster") in IC 210922A, a transient-like source following IC 220115A, and two known blazars in IC 240105A. ALMA resolved the Shadow Blaster into a strongly lensed dusty star-forming galaxy at z=2.988 with a compact gas-rich starburst core. Motivated by this result, we cross-matched IceCube localizations with the Herschel Bright Sources (HerBS) and Planck High-Redshift Source Candidate (PHZ) catalogs. Among 51 historical IceCube events with A_bbox,90 <= pi deg^2, four fields contain at least one HerBS source, compared with 0.723 expected from right-ascension scrambling (fixed-cut p=4.18e-3). Scanning the localization-area threshold gives p_global,A=0.0135. Applying the same IceCube selection to PHZ gives four associated fields versus 3.544 expected (p=0.478). Because the focused HerBS selection followed a broader exploratory analysis with trials-corrected p_global=0.130, the focused result is suggestive rather than definitive. The four well-localized HerBS fields contain six high-redshift DSFGs with apparent L_IR~10^13 L_sun. ALMA imaging and published spectroscopy reveal compact or multiple-component morphologies, extreme molecular-gas kinematics, and possible obscured nuclear activity. These results support a hierarchical strategy: identify luminous submillimeter candidates through wide-field imaging and catalogs, then characterize them at high angular resolution to test for compact gas-rich calorimetric conditions and obscured AGN activity.

</details>


### [193] [The Role of Dark Matter in Driving Disk Perturbations: Application to LMC-SMC Like Galaxy Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07867)
*Himansh Rathore,Hayden R. Foote,Gurtina Besla,Silvio Varela-Lavin,Nicolas Garavito-Camargo,Martin D. Weinberg,Facundo A. Gomez,Kathryne J. Daniel,Chervin F. P. Laporte*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用N体模拟和Basis Function Expansions（BFE）方法，量化了SMC和LMC暗物质晕对LMC恒星盘施加的时变扭矩，发现晕的四极矩畸变主导内盘（R<6 kpc）翘曲，且首次通过多通道奇异谱分析（mSSA）分离出晕与SMC各自导致的翘曲演化信号，使大麦哲伦云系统成为检验暗物质理论的精密实验室。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质模型预测卫星星系会扭曲其宿主星系的暗物质晕，进而影响宿主恒星盘；LMC-SMC系统质量比明确、轨道和盘扰动已较好观测，是检验暗物质理论的理想实验室，但需定量区分SMC与LMC自身晕对盘的扭矩贡献。

Method: 采用N体模拟构建LMC-SMC孤立相互作用系统，使用晕BFE量化SMC与LMC暗物质晕对LMC盘的时变扭矩；构建盘BFE以建模其扰动；应用多通道奇异谱分析（mSSA）分解盘BFE系数、晕四极矩及LMC-SMC轨道的时间序列，以分离不同起源的翘曲演化。

Result: 发现LMC暗物质晕的四极矩畸变是内盘翘曲（R<6 kpc）的主要驱动源；LMC盘在并合前已显著扰动，平均垂直翘曲达~1 kpc；mSSA成功分离出与晕和SMC分别相关的翘曲演化成分，并确认晕畸变与盘扰动存在强时间关联。

Conclusion: LMC-SMC系统中暗物质晕对恒星盘的动力学影响可被精确建模和观测区分，为利用近邻星系相互作用检验冷暗物质模型提供了新范式和关键诊断工具。

Abstract: Dark-matter (DM) models predict that satellite galaxies distort their host's DM halo. As such, both the satellite and the distorted halo can perturb the host's stellar disk. The LMC-SMC system is a nearby $\sim1:10$~mass-ratio binary with a well-constrained orbit and well-characterized LMC disk perturbations. Hence, the system offers unique opportunities to test DM theory, provided the impact of halo torques on the LMC's disk can be characterized along with the SMC's torques. We use halo Basis Function Expansions (BFEs) of an N-body simulation of an isolated LMC-SMC-like interaction to quantify the time-dependent torques exerted on the LMC's disk by the SMC and the LMC's DM halo. We find that the halo torques arise from the quadrupole halo distortion, and the inner (R$<6$ kpc) LMC disk warps are the most promising probe of this quadrupole. For the first time, we construct a BFE to quantify the LMC's disk perturbations resulting from the SMC and halo torques. We find that the LMC's disk is significantly perturbed prior to the Clouds' Milky Way infall, possessing warps with a mean vertical extent of $\sim1$ kpc. Finally, we apply multi-channel singular spectral analysis (mSSA) to the time-series of the disk BFE coefficients, halo quadrupole, and the LMC-SMC orbit. mSSA successfully separates the temporal evolution of the LMC warps into components correlated with the halo versus the SMC. Our framework identifies correlated evolution between the LMC's halo distortions and the LMC's disk perturbations, enabling the Clouds to be a precision laboratory for DM physics.

</details>


### [194] [Closed-Form of the Local Galactic Potential and Stellar Distribution Function from Gaia DR3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09011)
*Indranil Das,Adam Kamoski,Dora Demiri,Brianna Isola,Hanieh Karimi,Dmitrii S. Zagorulia*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新方法，通过线性化碰撞less玻尔兹曼方程（CBE）并结合符号回归，从恒星计数中直接测量本地引力场，从而更可靠地估计本地暗物质密度，避免了传统方法中分布函数与势能耦合导致的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于恒星运动估算本地暗物质密度的方法结果分歧大，甚至最新机器学习分析给出零密度，亟需更稳健的物理建模方法。

Method: 将CBE关于加速度线性化以独立估计相空间分布函数；利用恒星数密度（而非CBE残差）作为主要观测约束；通过符号回归拟合闭式引力势。

Result: 恢复出的垂直方向引力势与经典自引力等温盘模型一致，表明本地暗物质密度可能较低或为零，且恒星数密度是关键信息源。

Conclusion: 传统联合拟合分布函数与势能的方法存在根本性简并，应转而基于可观测的恒星数密度直接推断力场；该方法提高了本地暗物质密度估计的可靠性。

Abstract: The local dark matter density determines the strength of the signal expected in direct-detection experiments, yet published estimates from stellar motions disagree by more than their errors, and the most recent machine-learning analysis of Gaia data finds a local density consistent with zero. According to Jeans' theorem, a distribution function built from integrals of motion satisfies the collisionless Boltzmann equation (CBE) trivially for any choice of potential, so a search that simultaneously fits the distribution function and the potential to the CBE identifies neither. Our pipeline instead estimates the distribution function in isolation, linearizing the equation in terms of accelerations and allowing for direct measurement of the local force field, and then fits closed forms to that field via symbolic regression. Throughout, we find that the usable information lies not in the CBE residual but in the stellar number counts, the observable most distorted by survey selection. Along the vertical profile, our recovered potential agrees with the classical self-gravitating isothermal disc.

</details>


### [195] [Origins of complex organic molecules in L1551 IRS 5: Interplay of thermal heating and accretion shocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07991)
*Ji-hyun Kang,Jeong-Eun Lee,Seokho Lee,Shigehisa Takakuwa*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用ALMA高分辨率数据研究FU Orionis型双星系统L1551 IRS 5中复杂有机分子（COMs）的空间起源，发现COMs既来自爆发引起的辐射加热，也来自不同吸积流驱动的局域激波化学；北部源呈现热主导与双模吸积共存特征，南部源则因化学存活时间差异表现出CH3OH与SO空间分布分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在更演化的I类原恒星中，COMs通常被高尘埃消光遮蔽，而L1551 IRS 5因爆发导致冰升华至大半径，为探测盘化学提供了独特机会；本工作旨在区分中心辐射加热与异质吸积路径引发的激波化学对COMs产生的贡献。

Method: 分析ALMA存档的高角分辨率数据，聚焦于CH3OH、CH3OCHO等COMs及激波示踪分子SO，比较其空间形态与运动学特征，以诊断不同吸积流相关的激发条件。

Result: COMs发射源于辐射加热与局域吸积激波共同作用：北部源中低激发COMs广泛分布，主要由爆发热辐射驱动，并存在双模吸积——螺旋臂上COMs对应盘间稳态吸积，东部盘面高激发COMs与亮SO共现于包层-盘流束撞击区；南部源中甲醇局限于东南流束撞击点，SO则呈环状分布于潮汐截断半径处。

Conclusion: L1551 IRS 5是研究激波化学与双星调控吸积如何塑造原恒星盘化学结构的理想实验室；COMs的空间分布与激发态反映了不同物理机制（热辐射 vs 激波）及其化学时标效应。

Abstract: Complex organic molecules (COMs) are typically observed in the warm inner envelopes of Class 0 protostars. In more evolved Class I sources, COM emission is confined to the compact inner disk and obscured by high dust opacity, but the FU Orionis-type binary L1551 IRS 5 offers a valuable opportunity to probe disk chemistry, as its outburst luminosity thermally sublimates ices out to large radii. We sought to spatially resolve the origins of COMs in this system to distinguish between central radiative heating and shock-induced chemistry driven by heterogeneous accretion pathways. We analyzed high-angular-resolution ALMA archival data, focusing on COMs (e.g., CH3OH, CH3OCHO) and the shock tracer SO, and compared their spatial morphologies and kinematics to diagnose the excitation conditions associated with different accretion flows. We find that the COM emission in L1551 IRS 5 arises from both radiative heating and localized accretion shocks. In the northern source, the widespread low-excitation COM emission is primarily consistent with thermal heating by the recent outburst. This source also exhibits dual-mode accretion with distinct chemical signatures, with COMs along the spiral arm tracing steady equatorial circumbinary disk-to-circumstellar disk accretion and high-excitation COMs with bright SO along the eastern disk surface tracing a vertical envelope-to-disk streamer impact. In the southern source, methanol is confined to the southeastern streamer impact site, whereas SO forms a ring-like structure at the tidal truncation radius. We attribute this difference to differential chemical survival timescales. L1551 IRS 5 thus serves as an ideal laboratory to investigate how shock chemistry and binary-regulated accretion shape the chemical structure of protostellar disks.

</details>


### [196] [The Roman eXtreme Deep Field (RXDF)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08145)
*Haojing Yan,Anton M. Koekemoer,Yue Shen,Bangzheng Sun,Norman A. Grogin,Zihao Wu,Christian Kragh Jespersen,Rachel Somerville,Kyoung-Soo Lee,Dale D. Kocevski,Adam J. Burgasser,Pedro H. Bernardinelli,Yicheng Guo,Charles Steinhardt,Xiaohui Fan,Duncan Farrah,Gisella De Rosa,Feige Wang,Jinyi Yang,Lifan Wang,Fengwu Sun,Christopher N. A. Willmer,John David Silverman,Steven L. Finkelstein,Seth H. Cohen,Rolf A. Jansen,Rogier A. Windhorst,Brent M. Smith,Stefano Casertano,Anthony H. Gonzalez,Michael A. Strauss,Ray A. Lucas,Katherine E.
