Brandt,Denis Burgarella,Timothy Carleton,Delondrae D. Carter,Caitlin M. Casey,Christopher J. Conselice,Kyle W. Cook,Jeff Cooke,David A. Coulter,Tyler Desjardins,Tim Dewachter,Nicole E. Drakos,Simon P. Driver,Qiao Duan,Eiichi Egami,Andreas Faisst,Travis C. Fischer,Adriano Fontana,Ori Fox,Brenda Frye,Yoshinobu Fudamoto,Eric Gawiser,Mauro Giavalisco,Yuichi Harikane,Thomas Harvey,Nimish P. Hathi,Benne W. Holwerda,Taylor Hutchison,Olivier Ilbert,Akio K. Inoue,Lucy R. Ivey,Kartheik Iyer,Mathilde Jauzac,Ignas Juodzbalis,Jeyhan S. Kartaltepe,Daichi Kashino,Susan Kassin,Patrick Kelly,Kotaro Kohno,Stephanie LaMassa,Erini Lambrides,Rebecca Larson,Junyao Li,Zhiyuan Ma,Sangeeta Malhotra,Elizabeth McGrath,Peter Melchior,Marcio Melendez,Hironao Miyatake,Bahram Mobasher,Mireia Montes,Thomas Moore,Takahiro Morishita,Takashi Moriya,Leonidas Moustakas,Rohan Naidu,Rosalia O'Brien,Pascal A. Oesch,Masafusa Onoue,Rafael Ortiz,Masami Ouchi,Robert G. Pascalau,Molly Peeples,Andreea O. Petric,Sara Petty,Justin Pierel,Marc Rafelski,Eniko Regos,Armin Rest,Mitchell Revalski,James Rhoads,Pierluigi Rinaldi,Aaron Robotham,Kate Rowlands,Pablo M. Sanchez-Alarcon,Paola Santini,Alice E. Shapley,Raymond C. Simons,Swara Ravindranath,Takahiro Sumi,Takumi S. Tanaka,Masayuki Tanaka,Scott Tompkins,Christina C. Williams,Edward J. Wollack,John F. Wu,L. Y. Aaron Yung*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: RXDF是罗马太空望远镜的五大通用天体物理巡天项目之一，将在比哈勃极端深场大140倍的区域实现AB=30等深度成像，覆盖7个波段，并具备多历元观测能力，以支持再电离、大尺度结构、超大质量黑洞增长、小红点及高红移超新星等前沿研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有哈勃+韦布望远镜在极深场下的观测体积太小，而罗马核心社区巡天（CCS）又不够深，亟需一个兼具深度、面积与时间域能力的下一代深场巡天项目来突破限制。

Method: 利用罗马太空望远镜开展386.41小时成像观测，覆盖7个波段（RZYJHF K），在678.75 arcmin²全深度区达AB=30 mag（部分波段略浅），分3个主历元（间隔1年）、每历元再分3个子历元（间隔约10天）；选址于欧几里得超深场（EUDF）近北黄极，复用JWST NEXUS等多波段数据。

Result: 建成迄今最深、最大面积、且具多历元能力的光学-近红外深场，深度和面积均显著超越HST/XDF与Roman CCS；提供快速公开的数据集，支撑广泛科学目标。

Conclusion: RXDF将填补当前深场观测在深度、面积与时间域三方面的关键空白，成为研究宇宙早期演化与暂现现象的里程碑式设施，并通过开放数据推动天文学长期发展。

Abstract: The Roman eXtreme Deep Field (RXDF) program is one of the five General Astrophysics Survey (GAS) programs approved for observing time with the Nancy Grace Roman Space Telescope in Cycles 1 and 2. It has been allocated 386.41 hours to carry out an imaging survey to AB = 30 mag (5-sigma) over ~140x larger area than the Hubble eXtreme Deep Field (HXDF) full-depth area (ACS+WFC3/IR). The RXDF will cover the full Roman wavelength range with 7 bands, reaching AB = 30 mag in RZYJH, 29 mag in F, and 28 mag in K, over a full-depth area of 678.75 arcmin^2 embedded in a total area of 1,243 arcmin^2, and far exceeding the depths of the Roman Core Community Surveys (CCS). The RXDF is within the Euclid Ultra Deep Field (EUDF) near the North Ecliptic Pole (NEP), a strategic long-term field for generational space facilities, with a wealth of multi-wavelength data including extensive coverage from the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) NEXUS Treasury program. The observations will cover 3 epochs at a 1-year cadence, each epoch divided into 3 sub-epochs ~10 days apart, enabling time-domain studies on time baselines from ~10 days to over ~2 years. The RXDF is uniquely positioned to address critical questions in reionization, large scale structure (LSS), growth of supermassive black holes (SMBHs), little red dots (LRDs), and high-z supernovae (SNe); the volumes probed by HST+JWST are too small at these extreme depths, and even the deepest CCS tiers are too shallow. In addition to our key objectives, a wealth of additional science will be enabled by engaging the community with our rapidly released datasets, revolutionizing a wide range of science for a lasting legacy. This short document, which is converted from the approved RXDF proposal, aims to provide the community with a summary of the program.

</details>


### [197] [AEON-z5: A Candidate AGN-driven Outflow Enriching the Circumgalactic Medium at $z\simeq5.23$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08264)
*Xiaoyang Wei,Zheng Cai,Shiwu Zhang,Fujiang Yu,Yunjing Wu,Shuaiyi Li,Xiaojing Lin,Mingyu Li,Xuelun Mei*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文报告了在红移z~5.23处发现的星系AEON-z5，其拥有一个紧凑的核心和一个延伸至>4 kpc的电离气体晕；JWST观测揭示了高度不对称的Hα+[N II]谱线轮廓，支持存在由中心AGN驱动的双极外流，并发现气体金属丰度接近太阳值，表明早期AGN反馈可能已将重元素输运至星系周介质（CGM）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏z>5时星系化学增丰气体外流的直接观测证据，而该过程对理解星系演化至关重要。

Method: 利用JWST/NIRCam成像与F444W光栅光谱数据，分析AEON-z5的形态、发射线分布、运动学结构及金属丰度（基于[N II]/Hα比值），并结合多组分谱线拟合与物理模型解释外流机制。

Result: 发现AEON-z5具有延伸>4 kpc的电离晕；Hα+[N II]谱呈现+489 km/s速度偏移与宽达630 km/s的FWHM，暗示强AGN驱动外流；探测到可能的高速外流团块（Δv_LOS~2800 km/s）；[N II]/Hα比值显示金属丰度为0.7–1.0 Z⊙，最低仍>0.37 Z⊙；外晕速度随半径红移，达200–400 km/s。

Conclusion: AEON-z5是早期宇宙中AGN反馈驱动金属向CGM输运的一个关键候选体，为z>5时期星系-环境物质循环提供了首个直接观测证据。

Abstract: The dispersal of chemically enriched gas from galaxies into their surroundings is a key process in galaxy evolution, yet direct observational evidence at z>5 remains scarce. We present AEON-z5, a galaxy at z~5.23 in the COSMOS field comprising a compact continuum-emitting core surrounded by an extended, line-dominated ionized nebula. JWST/NIRCam imaging shows that H$α$+[N II] and H$β$+[O III] emission extends to projected radii of >4 kpc - 2-2.5 times the typical effective radius at the host stellar mass - reaching the outer ISM and the inner CGM. F444W grism data reveal a highly asymmetric H$α$+[N II] profile, which we interpret as a bipolar outflow and decompose into three kinematic components. The dominant component has a flux-weighted velocity offset of ~+489 km/s and FWHM~630 km/s, with a wing reaching $v_{84}$~792 km/s. A tentative detached feature at $Δv_{LOS}$~2800 km/s, detected at 1.5$σ$ in the 1D spectrum (2.4$σ$ in the 2D fit), may trace an outflow clump. These kinematics are most coherently explained by an AGN in the core. For the extended nebula, we derive a velocity curve shifting redward with radius, reaching 200-400 km/s at $r_p$~1.3-2.5 kpc - a trend that may reflect an accelerating outflow, corotating gas, or recycled inflow viewed in projection. Crucially, the measured [N II]/H$α$ ratios imply near-solar N2-based abundances (0.7-1.0 $Z_\odot$), remaining >0.37 $Z_\odot$ after allowing for AGN excitation and calibration systematics. The combination of large extent, enrichment, and extreme kinematics identifies AEON-z5 as a candidate snapshot of feedback-driven metal transport, offering a direct view of how early AGN activity may redistribute chemically processed gas into the CGM within the first ~1.1 billion years.

</details>


### [198] [Low-Redshift Interlopers in the SDSS DR16 $z > 5$ Quasar Catalogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08290)
*Vivek Kumar Jha,Harikumar N,Yogesh Wadadekar*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文分析了SDSS DR16中高红移类星体（z>5）的红移估计问题，发现大量源的管线红移（Z_DR16Q）存在显著偏差，其中约19%实为低红移活动星系核；错误主因是将Mg II、C III、C IV等线误认为Lyα，而Z_FIT算法在谱线显著时表现可靠，因此建议优先采用Z_FIT而非管线红移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高红移类星体对研究早期黑洞增长和再电离至关重要，但SDSS DR16中大量z>5源的管线红移估计存在可靠性问题，亟需系统评估与修正。

Method: 对SDSS DR16中655个z>5类星体进行系统红移比对（Z_DR16Q vs Z_FIT），结合人工目视检查，分析偏差来源（如谱线误认），并利用Gunn-Peterson吸收特征验证红移可靠性。

Result: 655个源中73.2%（480个）Z_DR16Q与Z_FIT显著不一致；其中125个（19.1%）经目视确认为低红移AGN；主要误判原因为将Mg II/C III/C IV或Hα误识为Lyα；Z_FIT在谱线清晰时能准确恢复红移。

Conclusion: SDSS DR16中z>5类星体的管线红移（Z_DR16Q或Z_SPEC）不可靠，应谨慎使用；Z_FIT是更优的红移估计器，尤其适用于高红移类星体研究。

Abstract: High-redshift quasars ($z>5$) are crucial for studying early black hole growth and reionisation. SDSS Data Release 16 catalogues 655 such sources with multiple redshift estimators. We systematically examined this subsample and found 480 sources (73.2\%) where pipeline redshift (Z\_DR16Q) disagrees with the derived estimate (Z\_FIT) significantly. Visual inspection confirms that 125 of these (19.1\% of the total) are actually low-redshift active galactic nuclei. In 114 cases, broad Mg\,{\sc ii} with C\,{\sc iii} and C\,{\sc iv} was misidentified as Ly$α$; in 11 cases, H$α$ was misidentified as Ly$α$. This error is immediately revealed by the absence of Gunn--Peterson trough blueward of Ly$α$. Crucially, Z\_FIT recovers correct redshift in the affected sources where emission lines are prominent, indicating the algorithm is sound; the issue lies in community preference for Z\_DR16Q or Z\_SPEC. We urge caution against uncritical use of pipeline redshifts for $z>5$ DR16Q sources and recommend Z\_FIT as the preferred estimator.

</details>


### [199] [Series of core collapse numerical simulations (SOLANUM) I: Modelling the early and late infall in a sun-like protostar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08376)
*D. Navarro-Almaida,B. Commerçon,U. Lebreuilly,P. Hennebelle,A. Ahmad,A. Fuente,P. Rivière-Marichalar,J. E. Pineda*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过磁流体动力学模拟研究了年轻恒星天体周围原恒星盘中晚期下落气体的物理历史与性质，发现晚期吸积气体通过磁交换不稳定性形成的丝状结构进入盘，其物理和化学特性与早期盘气体不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束晚期下落气体的物理历史与性质，并评估其对原恒星盘物理与化学性质的潜在影响。

Method: 开展5 M$_\odot$气体的核坍缩数值模拟（含示踪粒子与吸积粒子），历时约3.6×10⁵年；分析旋转支撑盘的演化、初始盘气体来源与物理历史，以及晚期下落示踪粒子的起源与热力学路径。

Result: 最终吸积体质量为1.02 M$_\odot$，盘质量稳定在~0.02 M$_\odot$；盘半径增至~40 au，密度下降约8倍；磁交换不稳定性催生连接包层与盘的丝状结构；晚期下落气体表现为类似观测到的‘streamers’，其物理历史明显区别于早期盘气体。

Conclusion: 晚期通过丝状通道吸积的气体具有不同于原盘气体的物理与化学演化历史，可能显著改变盘的化学组成；核心坍缩模拟可自然再现观测到的气体流结构。

Abstract: Magnetohydrodynamical simulations and interferometric observations toward young stellar objects reveal the accretion of gas that may alter the physical properties and chemical composition of protostellar disks. Our goal is to constrain the physical history and properties of late infalling gas, and assess its potential to change the physical and chemical properties of the disk. We carried out a core collapse simulation of 5 M$_{\odot}$ of gas endowed with tracer and sink particles during $\sim 3.6\times 10^{5}$ yr. We analyzed the properties of the rotationally supported disk, and the origin and physical history of the gas from which it is initially formed. We also selected the tracer particles that describe the late infall to analyze their origin and physical history, and compare them to the information obtained from the disk. The final mass of the sink reaches 1.02 M$_\odot$, while the mass of the disk stays fairly constant around 0.02 M$_\odot$. Throughout its evolution, the disk radius increases up to $\sim 40$ au and its density is reduced by a factor of $\sim 8$. Filaments that channel gas from the envelope to the disk and sink appear as a result of the magnetic interchange instability. Tracer particles can identify late infalling gas, whose physical properties resemble that of observed streamers and are only accreted in the final snapshots of the simulation. The origin and physical history of the late infalling gas are different from those of the gas that initially forms the disk. Core collapse simulations predict the appearance of filaments that connect the envelope to the disk. The late accretion of gas through these channels has properties similar to those of streamers and brings gas to the disk with a different physical history that could translate into a different chemical composition.

</details>


### [200] [The Impact of Circumgalactic Rotation on Ly$α$ Radiative Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08396)
*Hee-Gyeong Kym,Seok-Jun Chang,Max Gronke,Kwang-Il Seon*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过3D蒙特卡洛Lyα辐射转移模拟，研究了旋转CGM对Lyα谱线特征的影响，发现旋转会拓宽谱线、增大峰间距，但在高HI柱密度下与柱密度存在简并；空间分辨观测能更清晰识别旋转导致的红蓝移不对称性，有助于区分旋转、流入和流出效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lyα谱线是探测星系周介质（CGM）物理性质和运动学的重要探针，但其共振散射特性使解释复杂；CGM旋转可能影响常用于推断HI柱密度的Lyα峰间距，需定量评估其影响。

Method: 采用3D蒙特卡洛Lyα辐射转移模拟，在类CGM旋转介质中系统改变旋转速度、HI柱密度、视线角、介质团块性和源固有宽度，分析出射Lyα光谱特征及其空间分辨形态。

Result: 旋转使积分谱展宽、峰间距增大，且在视线角依赖最强时多普勒频移主导频率扩散；高NHI下多次散射削弱旋转敏感性，导致Vrot与NHI简并；空间分辨谱显示旋转介质两侧呈现系统性红/蓝移不对称；该信号可混淆为流入/流出，且在团块介质中仍显著、对源宽度不敏感。

Conclusion: 仅依赖Lyα峰间距会高估HI柱密度；空间分辨Lyα观测是区分CGM旋转与辐射转移效应的关键手段，应在解释速度图时纳入旋转模型。

Abstract: Hydrogen Lyman-alpha (Ly$α$) is a prominent emission line from the circumgalactic medium (CGM). Due to its resonant nature, Ly$α$ carries imprints of the physical properties and kinematics of the cold CGM. In particular, CGM rotation can modify the Ly$α$ peak separation, which is often interpreted as a tracer of H I column density. We present 3D Monte Carlo Ly$α$ radiative-transfer simulations in a CGM-like rotating medium and examine how the emergent spectra depend on rotational velocity ($V_{\rm rot}$), H I column density ($N_{\rm HI}$), viewing angle, clumpiness, and intrinsic source width. We find that rotation broadens the integrated spectra and increases the peak separation, with the strongest viewing-angle dependence when rotational Doppler shifts dominate over frequency diffusion. At high $N_{\rm HI}$, numerous scatterings reduce the sensitivity of integrated spectra to rotation, producing a degeneracy between $V_{\rm rot}$ and $N_{\rm HI}$. Consequently, Ly$α$ peak separation alone can overestimate $N_{\rm HI}$ in a rotating medium. Spatially resolved halo spectra provide a clearer diagnostic: opposite sides of the rotating medium show systematic redshifted and blueshifted asymmetries associated with the line-of-sight velocity of the last-scattering gas. Such rotation-driven signatures can also contribute to velocity-map patterns often interpreted in terms of inflow or outflow, highlighting the need to consider rotation in spatially resolved Ly$α$ observations. We further show that the main signatures persist in simple clumpy media, while the halo signatures are largely insensitive to the intrinsic source width. Our results demonstrate that spatially resolved Ly$α$ observation is essential for disentangling CGM rotation from radiative-transfer effects.

</details>


### [201] [J023721.13$-$010528.5: A Giant $S$-shaped Radio Galaxy Showing Four Episodes of Jet Activity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08506)
*Pavan Vijay Khadekar,Dharam Vir Lal,Ramana Athreya*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文报告了对射电源J023721.13−010528.5的高灵敏度、高分辨率射电观测，发现其为一种新型四重双瓣射电星系（quadruple-double radio galaxy），具有四次喷流活动的清晰空间证据，是迄今已知最大的S形射电星系之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别和研究多纪元喷流活动，特别是利用大尺寸、轴向演化的S形射电星系作为理想探针，以揭示活动星系核喷流的间歇性行为。

Method: 使用升级后的GMRT（频段3和4）、MeerKAT及VLA进行多频射电观测，结合空间分辨的谱指数分析、能量密度分布与年龄估算，分析源的形态、成分对称性与喷流活动历史。

Result: 确认该源为巨型S形射电星系（投影尺度1.2 Mpc），识别出围绕核心对称分布的四对射电成分，对应约4–21 Myr内四次独立喷流活动；提出其为首个‘四重双瓣射电星系’原型。

Conclusion: J023721.13−010528.5代表一类新射电星系——四重双瓣型，支持喷流活动具有显著间歇性；S形巨射电星系是探测多纪元喷流活动的优选目标。

Abstract: We present high-sensitivity, high-resolution radio observations of J023721.13$-$010528.5 using the upgraded Giant Metrewave Radio Telescope in band-3 (250--500\,MHz) and band-4 (550--950\,MHz), along with MeerKAT (1280\,MHz) and VLA (3\,GHz) survey images. The radio source, hosted by a bright cluster galaxy at redshift $z=0.372$, is a giant radio galaxy with a projected linear size between the outermost hotspots of 1.2~Mpc and is one of the largest $S$-shaped radio sources known. Its radio morphology revealed four pairs of components straddling the radio core, which were also local maxima in profiles of radio flux densities and equipartition energy density measured along the radio lobes. Spatially resolved multi-frequency spectral analysis showed progressively older emission from the inner to the outer components, spanning approximately 4--21 Myr. The high degree of symmetry of the inner three component-pairs with respect to the radio core makes it improbable that these are randomly located knots in the radio jet. Therefore, we present J023721.13$-$010528.5 as the prototype of a new class of radio galaxies with four episodes of jet activity --- a quadruple-double radio galaxy. We suggest that giant $S$-shaped radio sources may be among the most promising targets for identifying and studying multi-epoch radio jet activity, since their large linear sizes and jet-axis evolution can spatially separate hotspots from successive episodes, making them easier to detect and analyse.

</details>


### [202] [The environmental dependence of the circumgalactic medium in a high-resolution cosmological simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08654)
*Georg Herzog,Rajeshwari Dutta,Michele Fumagalli*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究首次建模了大尺度结构对星系外围介质（CGM）中温、冷气体相的影响，发现卫星星系的存在是导致观测中环境依赖性覆盖分数差异的关键因素，强调在观测研究中需区分中心星系与卫星星系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测表明星系的外围介质（CGM）性质依赖于其所处的大尺度结构环境，但其物理机制尚不明确；本文旨在通过高分辨率宇宙学模拟，首次探究大尺度结构对CGM中温、冷气体相的影响机制。

Method: 基于EAGLE星系形成模型的高分辨率宇宙学模拟（气体质量分辨率达~4.5×10⁴ M⊙），选取z=0、恒星质量>10⁸ M⊙的星系，用FOF算法划分成团星系与孤立星系样本；进一步按中心/卫星身份及恒星质量、暗晕质量匹配，分析CGM物理性质与冷/温气体覆盖分数的环境依赖性。

Result: 在仅比较质量匹配的中心星系时，团族与孤立环境的CGM物理性质和覆盖分数无显著差异；但加入卫星星系后，成功重现观测到的环境依赖性覆盖分数差异；该结果与子网格物理模型无关，表明卫星成分是关键驱动因素。

Conclusion: 观测中CGM的环境依赖性主要源于卫星星系的混入，而非大尺度结构对中心星系CGM的直接物理影响；因此，观测研究必须严格区分中心与卫星星系，才能准确揭示环境对CGM的真实作用。

Abstract: There is increasing evidence from observations that the circumgalactic medium (CGM) of galaxies depends on the large-scale structure in which they are embedded. When probing the CGM in absorption using quasar sightlines, studies find an enhanced sky coverage in the CGM of galaxies in overdensities compared to galaxies in isolation. However, the exact reason for this environmental dependence is still unclear. In this work we aim to model for the first time the influence of the large-scale structure on the cool and warm ($T\sim10^{4-5}$ K) gas phases of the CGM. We use a high-resolution ($m_{gas}\approx 4.5\times 10^4$ M$_{\odot}$, $m_{dm}\approx 2.4\times 10^5$ M$_{\odot}$) cosmological simulation based on the EAGLE model of galaxy formation. We select all galaxies at $z=0$ with stellar mass $M_*>10^8$ M$_\odot$ and split them into galaxies in overdensities (group galaxies) and galaxies in isolation using a Friends-of-Friends algorithm. For these two samples, we investigate how the large-scale structure influences the physical properties of the CGM and the measured covering fractions of the cool and warm gas phases. When the two samples of group and isolated galaxies are matched in stellar mass, halo mass, and we use only central galaxies, we do not find any significant difference in the physical properties of the CGM and the measured covering fractions. However, when satellite galaxies are included, we recover the observational trends in the difference of covering fractions with the environment. The difficulty of recovering the observational trends shows the complexity of capturing the multiphase CGM in simulations. However, since our results concerning the admixture of satellites are independent of the employed subgrid physics, this work shows that central galaxies and satellites need to be disentangled in observational studies to clearly discern the role of the environment on the CGM.

</details>


### [203] [Detection prospects for heavy WIMP dark matter around M31* in microwave band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08771)
*Andrei E. Egorov*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了在M31星系中心超大质量黑洞周围的暗物质密度尖峰中，利用ALMA望远镜在微波波段探测弱相互作用大质量粒子（WIMP）的可能性。通过建模WIMP湮灭产生的同步辐射，并结合ALMA灵敏度分析，发现该目标有望探测到质量达1 TeV（甚至100 TeV）的热产生s波湮灭WIMP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索暗物质在星系中心超大质量黑洞周围密度尖峰中的可观测信号，提升WIMP间接探测的灵敏度与可行性。

Method: 建模WIMP在M31*周围密度尖峰中湮灭产生的相对论性电子/正电子及其在吸积流强磁场中的同步辐射；结合ALMA微波波段观测灵敏度，分析不同暗物质与介质参数下的可探测性。

Result: ALMA在10–100小时曝光下可探测热产生s波湮灭、质量约1 TeV的WIMP；在乐观参数下，探测上限可达100 TeV；M31*被确认为微波波段探测暗物质尖峰信号的独特目标。

Conclusion: M31中心暗物质密度尖峰是ALMA微波观测极具前景的WIMP间接探测目标，其探测能力显著优于常规晕模型假设，且系统误差已初步讨论。

Abstract: This work analyzes the detection prospects for weakly interacting massive particles (WIMPs) in dark matter (DM) density spike around the supermassive black hole (SMBH) in Andromeda galaxy M31 in microwave band. WIMP annihilation produces relativistic electrons and positrons, which generate the synchrotron emission in strong magnetic field of the accretion flow medium at microwave frequencies. Properties of this emission, as well as their dependence on DM and medium parameters, were modeled. Atacama Large Millimeter Array (ALMA) sensitivity to this emission and underlying WIMP parameters was estimated. It was obtained that M31* in microwave band represents the unique target, where DM spike signal can be detected in case of realistic spike and halo density profiles. In such case ALMA may reach the thermal s-wave annihilating WIMPs with masses up to around 1 TeV with just 10--100 hours exposure. Optimistic parameter configurations may allow probing all possible WIMP masses up to 100 TeV. Related systematic uncertainties are discussed.

</details>


### [204] [Weak cool core, weak turbulence: XRISM, XMM-Newton and Chandra spectroscopy of the core of Abell 1413](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08778)
*Egemen Gormez,Murat Hudaverdi*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用XRISM Resolve、XMM-Newton和Chandra数据对星系团Abell 1413核心进行首次高分辨率X射线光谱分析，发现其为高温、大质量、松弛但弱冷核系统；测得较低的视线速度弥散（σ_v ≈ 170 km/s）和极小的非热压强占比（2.5%），表明动力学极为宁静，且该宁静状态不依赖中心冷却；Fe丰度精确测定为0.38倍太阳值，Ni/Fe比为太阳值，而Ne/Mg/Si等低能谱线因仪器系统差异未给出可靠SNIa贡献约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究弱冷核（weak cool-core）星系团中气体动力学是否依然宁静，并检验低湍流是否依赖于中心冷却过程；此前仅在A3571中开展过类似研究，亟需新样本验证。

Method: 联合XRISM/Resolve（104.7 ks）、XMM-Newton与Chandra存档光谱，对Abell 1413核心区域进行多仪器协同光谱拟合；采用速度展宽热等离子体模型提取温度、速度弥散、丰度及非热压强等参数；结合光学红移与BCG位置校准整体运动学。

Result: 测得kT = 7.06 keV，σ_v = 170 km/s，非热压强占比2.5±0.7%，对应静力学质量偏差b < 2.5%；Fe丰度0.38±0.03±0.04 Z☉，Ni/Fe为太阳值；v_bulk ≈ −24 km/s，不支持大尺度视线方向气体振荡；Ne/Mg/Si谱线因仪器间10–20%差异，SNIa比例未被有效约束。

Conclusion: Abell 1413与A3571共同证实：弱冷核星系团可具备与强冷核系统（如A2029）相当甚至更低的动力学扰动水平，说明低湍流并非冷却主导的冷核所独有，挑战了冷核形成与湍流抑制必须耦合的传统认知；金属丰度结果支持Ia型超新星主导铁峰元素增丰，但轻元素丰度比仍受限于低能谱精度。

Abstract: We present the first XRISM spectroscopy of the core of Abell 1413 (z = 0.143), combining 104.7 ks of Resolve with archival XMM-Newton and Chandra spectra of a matched sky region. A1413 is hot, massive and relaxed but lacks a strong cool core (t_cool = 6.4 Gyr), a regime in which the low turbulence reported to date has been tested only once, in A3571. The Resolve spectrum requires a single velocity-broadened component with kT = 7.06 (+0.33, -0.43) keV and a line-of-sight velocity dispersion sigma_v = 170 (+25, -24) km/s (M_3D = 0.217). The bulk velocity relative to the brightest cluster galaxy, v_bulk = -24 +/- 26 (z_ICM) +/- 71 (z_BCG) km/s, is limited by the optical redshift rather than by Resolve, and disfavours coherent line-of-sight sloshing. The non-thermal pressure fraction, 2.5 +/- 0.7 per cent, implies a hydrostatic mass bias b < 2.5 per cent, comparable to the strong cool-core cluster A2029 (2.6 +/- 0.3 per cent) and, at matched sampling scale, if anything below it. With A3571 (1.3 +/- 0.3 per cent), to which A1413 rescales within 0.2 sigma, two weak cool cores now show that this quiescence does not require central cooling. The resolved Fe-K He-alpha complex gives Fe = 0.38 +/- 0.03 (stat) +/- 0.04 (sys) solar; Ni/Fe, separated from Fe K-beta for the first time, is solar, as is the pattern as a whole. The Ne, Mg and Si lines that set Ne/Fe, Mg/Fe and Si/Fe lie below 2 keV, where our instruments disagree by 10-20 per cent; we therefore do not regard the SNIa fraction as constrained.

</details>


### [205] [Design and commissioning of a windowless gas-target system for high-current beams at JUNA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08782)
*Xuyang Wanga,Fuqiang Cao,Weike Nana,Gang Lian,Wei Nan,Hongwei Huang,Yuchen Jiang,Jingyu Dong,Minghao Zhu,Yuwen Chen,Yihui Liu,Jinlong Mab,Yueran Wang,Jie Chen,Yunju Li,Shengquan Yan,Youbao Wang,Xiao Fang,Luohuan Wang,Yangping Shen,Bing Guo,Weiping Liua*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种为锦屏深地核天体物理实验（JUNA）设计的毫安级束流窗口式气体靶系统，采用三级差分抽真空、闭环气体循环纯化、恒温功率补偿量热计及基于次级弹性散射的位置分辨靶厚监测等技术，实现了1–3 mbar靶压下稳定运行（8小时内压力波动<1%），并成功完成束流调试与γ射线探测验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统带窗气体靶因入口箔片导致低能核反应测量中束流能量损失和展宽，影响精度；为提升JUNA设施对低能核天体物理反应的直接测量能力，需发展高稳定性、高诊断精度的窗口式气体靶系统。

Method: 设计并集成三级差分抽真空、闭环气体回收与纯化、恒温功率补偿量热计、基于次级弹性散射的位置分辨靶厚监测；在CIAE 600 kV加速器上开展^{14}N(p,γ)^{15}O和^{12}C(p,γ)^{13}N反应束流调试，并结合气体输运计算与热学建模验证系统性能。

Result: 系统在1–3 mbar靶压下稳定运行8小时，压力波动<1%，加速器侧真空达~10^{-4} Pa；轴向压力不均匀性<1.6%；量热测量与热计算一致；γ探测与靶系统运行稳定，获得反应位置与束流加热影响信息。

Conclusion: 该窗口式气体靶系统具备优异的运行稳定性与多维诊断能力，满足JUNA未来高流强、低能核反应精确测量的需求。

Abstract: Windowless gas targets avoid the beam-energy loss and straggling introduced by entrance foils and are therefore well suited for direct measurements of low-energy nuclear reactions. A windowless gas-target system designed for operation with milliampere beams has been developed for the Jinping Underground Nuclear Astrophysics facility (JUNA). The system combines three-stage differential pumping, closed-loop gas recovery and purification, a constant-temperature power-compensation calorimeter, and a position-resolved target-thickness monitor based on secondary elastic scattering. Stable operation was achieved over a target-pressure range of 1-3 mbar, with pressure fluctuations below 1% during 8 h of continuous circulation, while the accelerator-side pressure was maintained at approximately \(10^{-4}\) Pa. The closed-loop gas-circulation system maintained stable target conditions, while gas-transport calculations indicated that the axial pressure nonuniformity remained within approximately 1.6% under representative operating conditions. Calorimeter measurements were consistent with the thermal calculations, supporting the sensitivity correction used for beam-power determination. Beam commissioning with \(^{14}\mathrm{N}(p,γ)^{15}\mathrm{O}\) and \(^{12}\mathrm{C}(p,γ)^{13}\mathrm{N}\) at the 600 kV Cockcroft-Walton accelerator of the China Institute of Atomic Energy (CIAE) demonstrated stable operation of the gas-target and \(γ\)-ray detection systems and provided information on the influences of reaction position and beam heating. These results demonstrate the operating stability and diagnostic capability of the system for future high-current, low-energy nuclear-reaction measurements at JUNA.

</details>


### [206] [Turbulence Cascade in Cygnus X Revealed by Multi-point VDF Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08894)
*Junjie Huang,Yangjun Pu,Keping Qiu,Junhao Liu,Yingxi Li,Mengke Zhao*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用仙台45米望远镜对天鹅座X的CO观测数据，采用多点速度弥散函数（VDF）方法研究分子云湍流性质，发现其湍流相关尺度约为2–5 pc，并指出VDF比速度弥散更能稳健约束该尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流在分子云中调控恒星形成活动起关键作用，但目前缺乏能直接揭示其性质与物理过程的有效方法。

Method: 基于Nobeyama 45m望远镜的天鹅座X CO巡天数据，采用多点速度弥散函数（VDF）分析非热运动，结合Friends In Velocity（FIVe）算法识别10个速度子结构，并滤除大尺度有序结构以提取小尺度湍流成分。

Result: 云复合体及10个速度子结构均表现出约2–5 pc的湍流相关长度；该尺度可能对应天鹅座X中的湍流相关或驱动尺度；VDF在小于该尺度时反映湍流级联的速度标度律，更大尺度则反映云整体运动；VDF对相关尺度的约束比速度弥散更稳健。

Conclusion: VDF是一种有效探测分子云中小尺度湍流特性的新工具，有助于区分湍流级联与大尺度运动，并为理解湍流驱动机制和恒星形成关系提供观测依据。

Abstract: Turbulence plays a crucial role in regulating star formation activities within molecular clouds, yet few methods can directly reveal its properties and underlying processes. We use molecular line data from the Nobeyama 45m Cygnus X CO Survey to study the turbulence properties and their relationships with star-forming activities and/or other non-thermal motions. In this work, we apply the multi-point velocity dispersion function (VDF), rather than direct linewidth measurements, to investigate the non-thermal properties of molecular cloud motions. We filter out the large-scale ordered structure and isolate a relatively small-scale turbulence component. Through the Friends In Velocity (FIVe) algorithm, we identify 10 substructures of the clouds and derive the turbulent properties of each cloud using the VDF method. We find that both the cloud-complex regions and the 10 velocity substructures exhibit turbulence correlation lengths of $\sim 2$--5 pc. This plateau scale suggests a parsec-scale turbulence correlation or driving scale in Cygnus X. Below this scale, the rising VDFs trace the velocity scaling of the turbulent cascade, whereas larger-scale VDF variations likely reflect cloud-scale motions. The comparison between cloud complexes and substructures further suggests that, in observational data, the VDF may constrain the turbulence correlation scale more robustly than the turbulence velocity dispersion.

</details>

Biviano,M. Bolzonella,A. Bonchi,E. Branchini,M. Brescia,J. Brinchmann,S. Camera,G. Cañas-Herrera,V. Capobianco,C. Carbone,J. Carretero,M. Castellano,G. Castignani,S. Cavuoti,A. Cimatti,C. Colodro-Conde,G. Congedo,C. J. Conselice,L. Conversi,Y. Copin,A. Costille,F. Courbin,H. M. Courtois,M. Cropper,H. Degaudenzi,G. De Lucia,H. Dole,F. Dubath,X. Dupac,S. Dusini,A. Ealet,M. Fabricius,M. Farina,R. Farinelli,S. Ferriol,P. Fosalba,S. Fotopoulou,M. Frailis,E. Franceschi,M. Fumana,S. Galeotta,K. George,B. Gillis,C. Giocoli,P. Gómez-Alvarez,J. Gracia-Carpio,A. Grazian,F. Grupp,S. Gwyn,W. Holmes,I. M. Hook,F. Hormuth,A. Hornstrup,M. Huertas-Company,K. Jahnke,M. Jhabvala,B. Joachimi,S. Kermiche,A. Kiessling,B. Kubik,M. Kümmel,M. Kunz,H. Kurki-Suonio,A. M. C. Le Brun,S. Ligori,P. B. Lilje,V. Lindholm,I. Lloro,G. Mainetti,O. Mansutti,O. Marggraf,M. Martinelli,N. Martinet,F. Marulli,R. J. Massey,S. Maurogordato,E. Medinaceli,M. Melchior,M. Meneghetti,E. Merlin,G. Meylan,A. Mora,M. Moresco,L. Moscardini,E. Munari,C. Neissner,S. -M. Niemi,J. W. Nightingale,C. Padilla,S. Paltani,F. Pasian,K. Pedersen,W. J. Percival,A. Pezzotta,S. Pires,G. Polenta,M. Poncet,L. A. Popa,L. Pozzetti,F. Raison,A. Renzi,J. Rhodes,G. Riccio,E. Romelli,M. Roncarelli,B. Rusholme,R. Saglia,Z. Sakr,A. G. Sánchez,D. Sapone,B. Sartoris,P. Schneider,T. Schrabback,M. Scodeggio,A. Secroun,E. Sihvola,P. Simon,C. Sirignano,G. Sirri,P. Tallada-Crespí,A. N. Taylor,H. I. Teplitz,I. Tereno,N. Tessore,S. Toft,R. Toledo-Moreo,F. Torradeflot,I. Tutusaus,J. Valiviita,T. Vassallo,G. Verdoes Kleijn,A. Veropalumbo,Y. Wang,J. Weller,A. Zacchei,G. Zamorani,F. M. Zerbi,E. Zucca,J. Macias-Perez,M. Sereno*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用欧几里得巡天Q1数据，分析了红移0.2–0.5星系团中星系形态与环境密度（T–Σ）及团心距离（T–R）的关系，发现星系形态受环境影响显著：致密核心区盘结构更易被破坏，而外围区域形态差异减弱；棒状星系比例随密度降低而下降，尤其在高质星系中更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往中等红移星系团形态-环境关系研究受限于样本量和径向覆盖范围，本文旨在利用Euclid Q1大样本、大半径覆盖优势，系统检验T–Σ和T–R关系及其质量依赖性。

Method: 基于Euclid Q1目视形态星表（Zoobot模型预测投票分数），对71个z=0.2–0.5星系团内1754个成员星系按smooth/featured-or-disc/barred三类分类；结合光度红移与恒星质量，测量T–Σ和T–R关系，并延伸至3R_{500c}。

Result: 确认全质量范围内存在T–Σ关系，形态分异在高密度区和近团心处更强；超出1.5R_{500c}后分异减弱，低密度区featured-or-disc星系占比反超smooth星系；bar fraction随密度降低而下降，该趋势在高质星系中最显著。

Conclusion: 星系团环境驱动星系形态的渐进式转变，盘结构损失在团核最显著，表明环境过程在核心区域效率最高。

Abstract: Galaxy clusters provide unique laboratories for studying environmental effects on galaxy evolution. The morphology--density ($T$--$Σ$) and morphology--cluster-centric radius ($T$--$R$) relations trace how galaxy morphology is influenced by the environment, but previous studies at intermediate redshifts have been limited in both sample size and radial coverage. We use the Euclid Quick Data Release 1 (Q1) visual morphology catalogue to measure the $T$--$Σ$ and $T$--$R$ relations for smooth, featured-or-disc, and barred galaxies in known clusters at $0.2\leq z\leq 0.5$, extending this analysis to large cluster-centric distances ($3 R_{500c}$) and studying their dependence on stellar mass. Using photometric redshifts and stellar masses provided by Euclid, we identify 1754 cluster members within $1.5 R_{500c}$, distributed across 71 clusters. We classify the identified galaxies as smooth, featured-or-disc, or barred using the predicted vote fractions provided by the Zoobot deep learning foundation model in the Q1 visual morphology catalogue. We confirm the $T$--$Σ$ relation in all the stellar mass ranges studied, with a stronger influence of the cluster environment on galaxy morphology in the densest parts of the cluster and closer to the cluster centre. Beyond $1.5 R_{500c}$, the morphological segregation weakens, with featured-or-disc galaxies overtaking smooth galaxies at the lowest densities, consistent with the growing contribution of field interlopers in the cluster outskirts. For barred galaxies, we find a tentative decline of the bar fraction toward lower densities that is most pronounced for the most massive galaxies. In conclusion, our results show that the cluster environment drives a progressive transformation of galaxy morphology, with the loss of disc structure becoming more pronounced towards the cluster core, where environmental processes act most efficiently.

</details>


### [208] [Triaxiality of the Milky Way dark matter halo and the microlensing optical depth towards the Large Magellanic Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08988)
*J. García-Bellido,M. R. S. Hawkins*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了大麦哲伦云（LMC）方向的微引力透镜光学深度τ_LMC在非球形（三轴）暗物质晕中的变化，发现其形状依赖性完全由视线方向在晕主轴上的投影决定；对于Gaia观测支持的晕形状，τ_LMC相对于球形晕的变化为±15%量级，方向自由时可达2.6倍，该系统误差显著超过EROS-2和OGLE实验的统计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微引力透镜光学深度τ_LMC用于限制银河系暗物质晕中致密天体（如MACHO或原初黑洞）的比例，但以往几乎总假设晕为球形；本文旨在评估更现实的三轴晕形状对τ_LMC的影响，以减小由此引入的系统误差。

Method: 基于尺度不变密度分布ρ∝R^−α的三轴晕模型，推导出τ_LMC与球形晕比值的严格因子化表达式；结合Gaia DR3旋转曲线、RR Lyrae星流等观测约束的晕形状参数（如Einasto轮廓、Law-Majewski势、主轴方位角自由度），数值计算不同模型下的τ_LMC及其敏感性。

Result: LMC方向对晕的面内伸长极为敏感（因视线近似垂直于银心-太阳连线），但对垂向压扁不敏感；Gaia支持的晕形状下τ_LMC/τ_sph=0.95–1.15（固定本地密度），Law-Majewski势下为1.57，主轴方位自由时达0.67–1.54；Einasto晕截断后τ仅下降7–15%，但中位透镜距离从13.4 kpc移至8.5 kpc，增强形状敏感性，RR Lyrae增强从15%升至23%，方位自由范围扩大至0.62–1.62；由此导致MACHO/PBH丰度推断的系统误差达±15%，方向完全自由时达2.6倍。

Conclusion: 忽略暗物质晕的三轴性会引入不可忽略的系统误差，其幅度可超过当前主要微透镜巡天（EROS-2、OGLE）的统计误差；未来对致密暗物质组分的限制必须纳入晕形状的先验建模与不确定性传播。

Abstract: The microlensing optical depth towards the Large Magellanic Cloud (LMC), $τ_{\rm LMC}$, bounds the compact-object fraction of the Galactic dark matter halo, but is almost always evaluated for a spherical halo. We compute it for triaxial haloes and show that the shape dependence is carried entirely by the projection of the line of sight onto the halo principal axes: for a scale-free halo $ρ\propto R^{-α}$ at fixed local density it factorises exactly as $τ/τ_{\rm sph}=S_\odot^α\langle S^{-α}\rangle_w$, with $S(Ω)$ the ellipsoidal shape function. The LMC lies only $0\fdg78$ from perpendicular to the Sun-Galactic Centre line, making it a near-optimal probe of in-plane elongation and a poor probe of vertical flattening. For the halo shapes favoured by \textit{Gaia} we find $τ_{\rm LMC}/τ_{\rm sph}=0.95$--$1.15$ at fixed local density, $1.57$ for the Law-Majewski logarithmic potential, and $0.67$--$1.54$ when the major-axis azimuth is left free. For the Einasto halo required by the declining \textit{Gaia} DR3 rotation curve, truncation near $25\kpc$ is offset by the larger local density it implies and $τ$ falls by only $7$--$15$ per cent; but the median lens distance moves inwards from $13.4$ to $8.5\kpc$, where the logarithmic slope is steep and rising, amplifying the shape sensitivity: the \textit{Gaia} RR~Lyrae enhancement grows from $15$ to $23$ per cent and the orientation-free range widens to $0.62$--$1.62$. The systematic on the inferred MACHO or primordial-black-hole fraction is two-sided, of order $\pm15$ per cent for the measured shapes and a factor $2.6$ under full orientation freedom, exceeding the statistical errors of the EROS-2 and OGLE bounds.

</details>


### [209] [Active galactic nucleus activity in brightest cluster galaxies at intermediate redshifts in Sunyaev-Zel'dovich--selected clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09049)
*N. Hatamkhani,R. E. Skelton,J. Delhaize,M. Hilton,S. I. Loubser,N. Gupta,K. M. Mogotsi*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了红移0.3-0.8范围内SZ选星系团中亮最亮团星系（BCGs）的射电噪活动星系核（AGNs）的普遍性、吸积状态及宿主星系性质，发现22%的BCGs拥有射电噪AGNs，且多为低激发、辐射低效吸积系统，吸积效率随红移增加，而与星系团质量或动力学状态关系较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管本地宇宙中射电模式反馈已被广泛证实，但在中等红移范围（0.3 < z < 0.8）其普遍性仍缺乏约束；本文旨在检验BCG中AGN的发生率、吸积状态及其与宇宙学时间、星系团质量和动力学状态的依赖关系。

Method: 基于阿塔卡马宇宙学望远镜SZ星系团样本中的171个BCGs，结合南非大望远镜光谱、WISE中红外测光、ASKAP射电巡天数据，并利用X-CIGALE模型推导恒星质量、恒星形成率和爱丁顿比标度的吸积率。

Result: 38个BCGs（22%）具有射电噪AGNs，其中仅2个为高激发射电星系，其余为低激发系统；大多数AGNs具有低爱丁顿比（−3 ≲ log₁₀λ_Edd ≲ −1），符合辐射低效吸积；吸积效率随红移上升，但与星系团质量和动力学状态相关性弱；射电光度与星系团质量无关；相比场星系中的射电AGNs，BCG中的射电AGNs具有相似射电功率，但宿主星系质量高约0.8 dex。

Conclusion: 在z=0.3–0.8的BCGs中，射电噪AGNs主要为低激发、辐射低效吸积系统，表明在z∼1时质量约为3.2×10¹⁴ M⊙的星系团中维持模式吸积已普遍存在；吸积主要受燃料供给和宇宙演化调控，而非全局星系团性质，而星系团环境主要决定宿主星系的质量尺度。

Abstract: Context. Brightest cluster galaxies (BCGs) host powerful active galactic nuclei (AGNs) and help regulate the heating--cooling balance of the intracluster medium. While radio-mode feedback is well established locally, its prevalence at intermediate redshifts remains poorly constrained. Aims. We investigate the incidence, accretion state, and host-galaxy properties of AGNs in BCGs in Sunyaev--Zel'dovich (SZ)-selected clusters at $0.3 < z < 0.8$, testing dependences on cosmic epoch, cluster mass, and dynamical state. Methods. We analyse 171 BCGs from the Atacama Cosmology Telescope SZ catalogue, combining Southern African Large Telescope spectroscopy, WISE mid-infrared photometry, and Rapid ASKAP Continuum Survey radio data with \textsc{X-CIGALE} modelling to derive stellar masses, star formation rates, and Eddington-scaled accretion rates. Results. Thirty-eight BCGs (22%) host radio-loud AGNs. Only two ($\sim$5%) are high-excitation radio galaxies; the remainder are low-excitation systems. Most AGNs have low Eddington ratios ($-3 \lesssim \log_{10}λ_{\mathrm{Edd}} \lesssim -1$), consistent with radiatively inefficient accretion. Accretion efficiency increases with redshift, while correlations with cluster mass and dynamical state are weak. Radio luminosity is independent of cluster mass. Compared to field radio AGNs, BCG radio-loud AGNs have similar radio powers but are hosted by galaxies $\sim0.8$ dex more massive. Conclusions. BCG radio-loud AGNs at $0.3 < z < 0.8$ are predominantly low-excitation, radiatively inefficient systems, indicating that maintenance-mode accretion was widespread in massive clusters ($M_{500c} \simeq 3.2 \times 10^{14},M_\odot$) by $z \sim 1$. Accretion appears regulated primarily by fuel availability and cosmic evolution rather than global cluster properties, while the cluster environment mainly sets the host-galaxy mass scale.

</details>


### [210] [Small-Scale Clustering of Primordial Black Holes: The Little Red Dot Mass Function and the High-Redshift Galaxy Tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09078)
*Borui Zhang,Wei-Xiang Feng,Haipeng An*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新机制，认为詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜（JWST）发现的‘小红点’（LRDs）中的超大质量黑洞（SMBHs）可能起源于早期宇宙中原初黑洞（PBHs）在星团内的失控并合，并通过小尺度上的长-短模式耦合实现；该模型可解释LRDs的SMBH质量函数、致密性与超重特征，并支持其高红移星系形成加速作用，缓解ΛCDM模型张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释JWST新发现的‘小红点’（LRDs）中存在超大质量黑洞（SMBHs）的起源问题，并缓解其与标准ΛCDM宇宙学模型在高红移星系形成时间上的张力。

Method: 基于原初黑洞（PBH）在早期宇宙中形成致密星团，并通过小尺度长–短模式耦合驱动失控并合的物理框架，推导SMBH质量函数，分析LRDs的紧凑性和超重特性，并估算PBH星团内致密气体分布与观测的一致性。

Result: 成功构建了由PBH星团并合形成的SMBH质量函数；解释了LRDs的致密结构和超重黑洞特征；表明PBH星团内致密气体环境与LRD观测相符；指出此类SMBH可加速高红移星系形成，从而缓解ΛCDM模型张力。

Conclusion: LRDs中的SMBHs很可能源自早期宇宙PBH星团的失控并合，该机制不仅自洽解释多项观测特征，还为ΛCDM模型在高红移星系演化方面提供潜在补充。

Abstract: Supermassive black holes (SMBHs) in "little red dots" (LRDs) discovered the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) may result from runaway mergers of primordial black holes (PBHs) in clusters---through long--short mode coupling on small scales in the early Universe. In this framework, we derive the SMBH mass function, together with the compactness and overmassive features of LRDs. We also estimate that the dense gas residing in PBH clusters is consistent with LRD observations. In addition, SMBHs formed from PBH clusters can help accelerate galaxy formation at high redshifts, thus alleviating tension with $Λ$CDM cosmology.

</details>


### [211] [The fading hierarchy of Galactic open clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09122)
*Guimei Liu,João Alves,Yu Zhang,Emily L. Hunt,Ruqiu Lin,Josefa E. Großschedl,Efrem Maconi,Nora Wagner,Lilly Kormann,Cameren Swiggum*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用两体相关函数分析银河系太阳邻域内疏散星团的空间与运动学聚集特性，发现年轻星团保留了其诞生分子云的分形结构，但聚集性在约100 Myr内随年龄增长而系统减弱；同时给出了投影与三维分形维数的经验映射关系，并确定特征空间和运动学尺度分别为约370 pc和11 km/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疏散星团记录了银河系盘的分级恒星形成与后续动力学演化历史，需量化其空间与运动学聚集特性以理解星团起源与演化。

Method: 使用两体相关函数分析疏散星团在三维空间、银道面投影及按年龄、质量、巡天体积划分的子样本中的空间与运动学聚集性；通过分形维数和特征关联尺度进行量化；建立投影与三维分形维数的经验映射。

Result: 最年轻的星团分形维数接近星际介质值，表明保留了分子云的等级结构；聚集强度在约100 Myr内随年龄系统减弱，且小尺度（数十pc）相关性比大尺度（数百pc）衰减更快；星团质量与观测体积对其聚集性影响微弱；获得投影与三维分形维数的实测映射关系；确定特征空间和运动学关联尺度约为370 pc和11 km/s。

Conclusion: 疏散星团初始继承恒星形成的等级空间结构，并在后续演化中逐渐失去空间关联性，该结果支持‘形成时分级、演化中弥散’的图像。

Abstract: Galactic open clusters provide a record of both hierarchical star formation and the subsequent dynamical evolution of the Milky Way disk. We use the two-point correlation function to characterize the spatial and kinematic clustering of open clusters in the solar neighborhood, considering their three-dimensional (3D) distributions and their projections onto the Galactic plane, as well as subsamples divided by age, mass, and survey volume. We quantify the clustering through fractal dimensions and characteristic correlation scales. We find that the youngest clusters have fractal dimensions comparable to those measured in the interstellar medium, suggesting that they retain part of the hierarchical structure of their natal molecular clouds. The clustering strength then decreases systematically with age and becomes weak on a characteristic timescale of order 100 Myr. This evolution is scale dependent: the excess correlation at tens of parsecs fades more rapidly than the weaker correlation at hundreds of parsecs. By contrast, we find only a weak dependence of the clustering properties on cluster mass and volume. Using the same cluster sample, we derive an empirical mapping between projected and 3D fractal dimensions, providing an observational calibration for comparing the Milky Way with studies of external galaxies, where only projected measurements are available. The open-cluster population exhibits characteristic spatial and kinematic clustering scales of approximately 370 pc and 11 km/s, respectively. These results support a picture in which young open clusters inherit a spatial hierarchy from star formation and progressively lose their spatial correlations through subsequent evolution.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.CO'></div>

# astro-ph.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [Exploring the dark sector through galaxy dynamics in clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05597)
*Andrea Biviano,Lorenzo Pizzuti,Antonaldo Diaferio*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文综述了星系团动力学（特别是成员星系运动学）在精确测定星系团质量分布、检验宇宙学模型及暗物质、暗能量和引力理论中的关键作用，并讨论了当前方法的假设、系统误差及未来前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系团是研究宇宙结构形成、暗物质、暗能量和引力本质的重要实验室，但其质量分布的准确无偏测定仍是长期挑战；大样本光谱数据推动了运动学方法的发展，使其成为有竞争力的动力学探针。

Method: 综述性分析，涵盖基于成员星系运动学推断星系团动力学质量和质量轮廓的主要方法、物理假设，以及非平衡态、三轴性、子结构等系统误差的影响。

Result: 系统梳理了运动学方法与其他探针（如X射线、透镜效应）的互补性，总结了其在约束暗物质/暗能量性质及Mpc尺度引力行为方面的最新应用成果。

Conclusion: 随着建模改进和新一代光谱巡天开展，星系团运动学将成为精度宇宙学时代的基本探针之一。

Abstract: Galaxy clusters are among the most powerful laboratories to probe fundamental physics, from the formation of cosmic structures to the nature of dark matter, dark energy, and gravity. Realizing their full potential requires accurate and unbiased determinations of their mass profiles, a longstanding challenge in cluster cosmology. In this context, the kinematics of member galaxies in clusters has become a key tool to reconstruct the mass distribution and to investigate the dynamical properties of clusters. The advent of large spectroscopic datasets has enabled the application of increasingly sophisticated models and robust techniques, which make kinematic analyses competitive with other probes of cluster dynamics.
  In this review, we provide a comprehensive overview of cluster kinematics, covering the main methods to infer dynamical masses and mass profiles, their physical assumptions, and the impact of systematics such as deviation from dynamical equilibrium, triaxiality, and substructures. We discuss the complementarity with other techniques to investigate the structure of clusters and highlight recent applications to tests cosmological models. We focus in particular on the role of kinematical studies in constraining the dark sector (dark matter and dark energy) and in providing novel insights into the behavior of gravity at Mpc scales.
  Finally, we outline current limitations and future prospects, emphasizing that the combination of improved modeling and forthcoming spectroscopic surveys will establish cluster kinematics as a fundamental probe in the era of precision cosmology.

</details>


### [213] [Analytic bispectrum covariance for galaxy survey cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05607)
*Utkarsh Giri,Henry S. Grasshorn Gebhardt,Olivier Dore*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用大语言模型（LLM）推导并实现球形窗口下星系分布三谱（bispectrum）协方差的解析数值计算方法，弥补了非高斯、含窗协方差建模的理论空白，并通过Quijote模拟验证其精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三谱在宇宙学中尚未被充分利用，主因是含观测窗口的实际巡天下，其非高斯六点协方差难以解析建模；现有理论虽能处理无窗情形，但对真实巡天（含复杂窗函数）仍缺乏可行的全解析方案。

Method: 借助大语言模型（LLM）完成球形窗口下三谱协方差的数学推导与数值实现，融合精确积分与近似策略，仅依赖树级多谱作为输入；并通过与6000个Quijote晕目录的样本协方差对比进行迭代优化和验证。

Result: 成功获得高精度、跨尺度/三角形形状、可计算的三谱协方差模型；发现无窗情形下消失、但在巡天尺度模式下起主导作用的新项；LLM实现了从推导到优化算法的闭环，但最终验证与物理诠释仍需人工介入。

Conclusion: LLM可有效辅助复杂宇宙学统计量的解析建模，尤其适用于高维、多约束的数学推导任务；人机协同将成为近期前沿研究的重要范式——AI负责繁复推导与数值探索，人类负责物理把关与科学判断。

Abstract: The bispectrum of galaxy distribution remains an underutilized statistic in cosmological data analysis. This can be attributed to mathematical and computational challenges associated with a proper modeling of the signal and its covariance. While recent theoretical advances have made it possible to analytically model the bispectrum signal and covariance using perturbation theory, a fully non-Gaussian analytical treatment of the covariance for a realistic survey with window remains elusive. The full bispectrum covariance of observed galaxies is a non-Gaussian six-point function with each of its legs convolved with a complex window function. Although it has so far proven intractable, the problem is well-posed. In this article, we present the derivation, implementation, and validation of a numerical routine for the analytic calculation of the bispectrum covariance for a spherical window, carried out by a large language model (LLM). The derivation uncovered terms that vanish in the windowless case but that dominate the covariance for triangles with survey scale modes. As a development benchmark for the implementation, we used the sample covariance of the halo bispectrum measured from 6000 fiducial Quijote halo catalogs in real space. Over several iterations of refinement, the LLM converged on a regime-specific numerical scheme for the covariance matrix calculation, mixing exact and approximate approaches, resulting in a highly optimized, tractable and accurate covariance across scales and shapes, using only tree-level models for the input polyspectra. Closing the loop still required human expertise, which illustrates what human-AI collaboration in research may look like in the near term.

</details>


### [214] [How Cluster Pressure Profiles Scale: Mass, Redshift, and Intrinsic Scatter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05612)
*S. Andreon,F. Castagna*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用南极望远镜（SPT）SZ星系团样本和Planck-SPT康普顿-y图，通过分层贝叶斯建模与受限三次样条方法，首次在无水静质量偏差下获得星系团热压轮廓的总体平均值、内禀弥散及质量-红移标度关系。结果表明压强轮廓比以往低约40%，中间半径处弥散最小，并对自相似演化偏离给出严格限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热压轮廓对星系团宇宙学和天体物理研究至关重要，但此前受水静质量偏差和建模系统误差影响，其总体统计特性尚不明确。

Method: 基于60个高信噪比SPT SZ星系团（0.08<z<0.6），利用Planck-SPT联合y图，构建球对称三维压力轮廓；采用分层贝叶斯框架结合受限三次样条建模，引入弱透镜校准的Y–M关系以消除水静质量偏差，并计入视线前景/背景污染及投影异常。

Result: 得到无偏总体平均压力轮廓，在r/r500=0.4–0.7区间内弥散最小；整体压强比先前估计低约40%；约束平均轮廓的非自相似演化<0.1–0.2 dex，内禀弥散演化<8%每Δz=0.1；发现Arnaud et al. 2010中额外的M^0.12标度可能需修正。

Conclusion: 本工作提供了迄今最无偏的星系团平均热压轮廓及其演化约束，显著提升SZ观测用于宇宙学参数测量的精度与可靠性，并为反馈物理建模提供关键基准。

Abstract: [shortened] The thermal pressure profile of the intracluster medium is critical for cluster cosmology and understanding galaxy-cluster astrophysics. We determine the population-averaged pressure profile of Sunyaev-Zel'dovich (SZ) selected clusters, characterize its intrinsic scatter, and measure its scaling with mass and redshift. We select 60 clusters from the South Pole Telescope (SPT) SZ catalog with $0.08<z<0.6$, requiring high signal-to-noise and 5 element resolution on Planck-SPT Compton-$y$ maps. We model spherical three-dimensional pressure profiles from the Compton-$y$ maps using a hierarchical Bayesian framework with restricted cubic splines. We adopt an updated weak-lensing-calibrated Compton-$Y$--mass scaling relation, effectively removing hydrostatic mass bias from the inferred masses. Our model accounts for cluster-to-cluster profile variations, line-of-sight foreground and background contributions, and outliers from intrinsically distinct clusters or projection effects. The code is publicly available. We derive the population-averaged pressure profile and find minimal intrinsic scatter at intermediate radii ($0.4 \le r/r_{500} \le 0.7$). Our pressure profile is approximately 40\% lower than previous estimates at all radii, due to the unbiased mass scaling. We find suggestive, though not conclusive, evidence that the adopted mass dependence may require revision by removing the additional $M^{0.12}$ scaling introduced by Arnaud et al. 2010. We constrain departures from self-similar evolution in the mean profile to less than 0.1--0.2 dex and evolution of the intrinsic scatter to less than 8\% per $Δz=0.1$.

</details>


### [215] [Do DESI-DR2 BAO data imply a coupling of dark matter and dark energy?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05660)
*Weiqiang Yang,Sibo Zhang,Dinorah Barbosa,Marco Antonio Alvarez,Supriya Pan,Leila Graef*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了一种暗物质-暗能量相互作用模型（Q = Γρ_x），重点分析了暗能量状态方程w_x取不同值（-1、<-1、>-1）时的观测约束，并利用最新宇宙学数据（Planck 2018 CMB、DESI DR2 BAO、多个Ia型超新星样本）进行贝叶斯比较，结果表明虽部分相互作用模型能缓解S8张力，但ΛCDM仍被数据更优支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决ΛCDM模型中S8参数张力问题，并检验暗物质与暗能量之间简单线性相互作用（Q ∝ ρ_x）在不同暗能量物态方程下的可行性与观测支持度。

Method: 构建三种基于暗能量状态方程w_x的相互作用模型（w_x = -1, w_x < -1, w_x > -1），联合使用Planck 2018 CMB、DESI DR2 BAO及PantheonPlus/Union3/DESY5三组Ia型超新星数据，进行马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）参数估计和贝叶斯证据比（Bayesian evidence）分析。

Result: 当w_x = -1或w_x < -1时，联合数据给出正向相互作用信号（Γ > 0），但贝叶斯证据仍支持ΛCDM；当w_x > -1时，模型导致S8估值轻微降低，对缓解S8张力有一定潜力。

Conclusion: 尽管Q = Γρ_x型相互作用在特定w_x下能改善某些观测一致性（如S8），但当前多数据集联合分析未提供足够强的证据推翻标准ΛCDM模型，后者仍是当前数据下最简且最优的宇宙学范式。

Abstract: We revisit an interacting dark matter (DM) -- dark energy (DE) model characterized by the interaction function $Q = Γρ_x$, where $Γ$ is a constant coupling parameter and $ρ_x$ is the energy density of DE. This type of interaction is independent of the Hubble rate or other external parameters, but depends only on the fundamental properties of DE, such as its equation of state (EoS), $w_x$. We pay special attention to $w_x$ and study three distinct interacting scenarios distinguished by the nature of $w_x$, i.e. $w_x =-1$ (when DE corresponds to the vacuum energy), $w_{x} < -1$ (when DE has a phantom behavior), and $w_x > -1$ (quintessential DE). We constrain all of them using the most recent cosmological datasets, including CMB from Planck 2018, BAO from DESI DR2, and three compilations of SNIa (PantheonPlus, Union3, and DESY5). Our analyses reveal that evidence of interaction is supported in scenarios with $w_x =-1$ and $w_x < -1$ when all three datasets are combined, but from the Bayesian evidence analysis, $Λ$CDM remains favored over these interacting scenarios. Regarding the $S_8$ parameter, when $w_x > -1$, this interacting scenario leads to mildly lower estimates across all datasets.

</details>


### [216] [Stacking HI in the dark: Towards detecting the reionization-epoch 21 cm signal in Lyman-$α$ dark gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05980)
*Barun Maity,Frederick B. Davies*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于类星体吸收光谱中暗间隙的21 cm信号实空间堆叠探测新方法，利用半数值再电离模型和真实再电离光锥模拟，表明在z~5.9时对长暗间隙（L>32 h⁻¹cMpc）进行堆叠可获得显著信噪比（S/N>10），有望借助现有光学与射电设备实现对宇宙学21 cm信号的稳健探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 21 cm信号探测易受仪器系统误差和前景污染影响，亟需独立验证手段；类星体光谱中的暗间隙可作为再电离晚期中性氢分布的良好示踪，提供新探测途径。

Method: 提出基于长暗间隙（L > 32 h⁻¹cMpc, F_th < 0.005）的21 cm实空间堆叠技术；结合半数值再电离模型与含热噪声及前景规避的真实光锥模拟，评估不同再电离历史下的信号强度与信噪比。

Result: 在x_HI ~ 0.1、z = 5.9时，堆叠可得δT̃_b ~ 2–3 mK的峰值亮度温度；乐观前景规避下（θ < 20°），约100个暗间隙即可实现S/N > 10；现有VLT/Keck和HERA/SKA已具备探测潜力。

Conclusion: 该光学-射电协同堆叠方法为宇宙学21 cm信号提供了直接、统计稳健且当前可行的探测新路径。

Abstract: The detection of the redshifted 21 cm signal from neutral hydrogen during the epoch of reionization remains one of the central challenges in contemporary cosmology. Although upcoming radio interferometers are expected to measure 21 cm fluctuations at a few percent precision, an independent confirmation is essential to ensure robustness against instrumental systematics and foreground contamination. In this work, we propose a novel real-space stacking technique to detect the 21 cm signal, guided by the presence of dark gaps in quasar absorption spectra during the late stages of reionization ($z \sim 5.0$-$6.1$). Using a semi-numerical reionization model, we show that stacking 21 cm images on long dark gaps (length, $L > 32~h^{-1}\mathrm{cMpc}$ and threshold flux, $F_{\mathrm{th}} < 0.005$) should reveal a strong emission signal with a peak mean-subtracted 21 cm brightness temperature of $δ\tilde{T}_b \sim 2-3~ mK$ for a volume-weighted average neutral fraction of $x_{\mathrm{HI}} \sim 0.1$ at $z = 5.9$. We further extend our analysis to realistic reionization lightcones, incorporating thermal noise and foreground avoidance effects, generated for two different reionization histories. In an optimistic foreground avoidance scenario (wedge field of view $θ< 20^\circ$), a sample of roughly 100 dark gaps is sufficient to achieve a combined root-sum-square signal-to-noise ratio exceeding 10 for both reionization models, while a more conservative wedge avoidance may require more dark gaps for a solid detection. Given the large number of quasars known at $z > 6$, a detection of this stacked signal may thus already be within reach of existing optical (i.e., VLT, Keck) and 21 cm observatories (i.e., HERA, SKA). This synergistic approach offers a promising pathway towards a direct and statistically robust detection of the cosmological 21 cm signal.

</details>


### [217] [Probing Dark matter-Baryon Interaction with S301--the Fastest Known Star in the Milky Way](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06184)
*Yugen Lin*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用恒星S301的极端轨道（近心点仅约12 AU，速度达25000 km/s）探测银河系中心高密度、高相对速度环境下的暗物质-重子相互作用，发现其轨道平均能量沉积率在现有实验约束下仍可显著影响恒星亮度与演化，从而确立S301为探测此类相互作用的高灵敏天体探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用S301靠近银河系中心超大质量黑洞Sgr A*的极端轨道特性，探测传统地面和天文观测难以覆盖的高密度、高相对速度区域中的暗物质-重子相互作用。

Method: 计算S301在绕行过程中通过暗物质-质子散射所获得的轨道平均能量沉积率，并分析其对恒星光度和演化的影响。

Result: 在满足当前地面实验与天文观测限制的暗物质参数空间内，暗物质-重子散射仍可在S301中注入足够能量，显著改变其光度与演化路径。

Conclusion: S301是探测暗物质-重子相互作用的极为灵敏的天体探针，为研究高密度、高相对速度天体物理环境中的暗物质性质开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The recent discovery of S301 provides an unique opportunity to investigate dark matter-baryon interaction in the innermost region of the Milky Way. S301 follows a highly eccentric orbit around Sgr~A*, and its pericenter distance is only around 12 AU with velocity about $25000\,{\rm km/s}$. These extreme properties make S301 highly sensitive to dark matter-baryon scattering in the center of galaxy, where the dark matter density may be strongly enhanced. We have calculated the orbit-averaged energy deposited rate in S301 via dark matter-proton scattering, and show that over a broad dark matter parameter space, cross sections satisfying the existing bounds from terrestrial experiments and astronomical observations can inject sufficient energy to modify the luminosity and evolution of S301. Our results establish S301 as an exceptionally sensitive probe of dark matter-baryon interactions and open a new avenue for probing dark matter interactions in high-density and high-velocity astrophysical environments.

</details>


### [218] [Little Red Dot Cosmology: A Matter-Era Baryon Acoustic Oscillations Probe of $Λ$CDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06926)
*Jessica A. Zebrowski,Rohan P. Naidu*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用詹姆斯·韦布太空望远镜（JWST）发现的‘小红点’（LRDs）作为宇宙学探针的潜力，特别是在红移4–9之间未被充分探索的宇宙时期。研究表明，LRDs具有高数密度、随红移增长的偏置因子和独特光谱特征，使其成为测量重子声学振荡（BAO）的理想目标；Fisher预报显示，在类似DESI的大天区（14,000 deg²）红外光谱巡天中，可实现约1%精度的各向同性距离尺度DV/rd测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 填补红移4–9之间宇宙大尺度结构观测空白，检验ΛCDM模型在物质主导时期的预测能力，因该时期暗能量影响可忽略，理论预期明确。

Method: 基于JWST观测得到的LRDs数密度（~10⁻⁴ h³ Mpc⁻³）、偏置因子（b≈3–8）及光谱特征，采用Fisher信息矩阵方法，对覆盖14,000 deg²天区、红移范围z≈4–9的宽视场近至中红外光谱巡天进行BAO距离尺度测量精度预报。

Result: 可在四个红移区间内实现约1%精度的各向同性距离尺度DV/r_d测量，显著提升高红移宇宙学约束能力。

Conclusion: LRDs是探测物质主导时期宇宙膨胀历史的理想新探针，应成为未来宽视场红外光谱巡天（如SPHEREx、Roman或专用任务）的核心科学目标之一。

Abstract: The discovery of Little Red Dots (LRDs) with JWST provides a new source population for cosmology in a largely unmapped period of cosmic history. LRDs are most numerous at $4\lesssim z\lesssim9$, thereby bridging existing low-redshift large-scale-structure measurements and the cosmic microwave background. In this epoch, the Universe is deep in the matter-dominated era, where dark energy is dynamically negligible and the expansion history is tightly predicted in $Λ$CDM, making these redshifts a clean test of the standard cosmological model. Recent measurements indicate that LRDs have number densities of $\bar n\sim10^{-4}$ $h^3$ Mpc$^{-3}$, a bias rising from $b\sim 3$ to $\sim8$ over this interval, and distinctive spectrophotometric signatures -- a remarkably favorable combination for baryon acoustic oscillation (BAO) measurements. Using a Fisher forecast with these fiducial inputs, we find that a wide-field spectroscopic survey of LRDs over a DESI-like (14,000 deg$^2$) footprint delivers a percent-level isotropic distance scale measurement of $D_V/r_d$ across four bins spanning $4\lesssim z\lesssim9$. LRDs are therefore a compelling target for future wide-field near- to mid-infrared spectroscopy aimed at mapping large-scale structure deep into the matter-dominated era.

</details>


### [219] [Primordial Black Hole Abundances and Scalar Induced Gravitational Waves from Finite-Width Power Spectra in a Stiff Thermal History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07043)
*Abolhassan Mohammadi,Yogesh,Cristian Joana,Hongwei Tan,Ying-li Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在w≥1/3的状态方程背景下，由大尺度原初扰动形成的原初黑洞（PBHs），通过数值相对论模拟确定了坍缩阈值，并分析其对PBH丰度及标量诱导引力波（SIGW）的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解原初黑洞的形成机制及其与标量诱导引力波信号之间的关系，特别是在不同状态方程和功率谱宽度下的坍缩阈值变化。

Method: 采用有限宽度Δ的对数正态曲率功率谱，结合自引力完美流体的数值相对论模拟，对五种w值和五种谱宽进行坍缩阈值扫描，并将结果用于PBH丰度和SIGW计算；同时对比半解析q-函数方法。

Result: 发现坍缩阈值随Δ和w增大而升高，影响PBH构成全部暗物质所需的曲率振幅，并进而改变伴随的SIGW信号归一化；q-函数方法仅在接近辐射主导且近单色谱时可靠。

Conclusion: 数值模拟得到的坍缩阈值比半解析方法更准确，尤其对于宽谱和较硬状态方程情形；这对利用PBH和SIGW联合探测早期宇宙物理具有重要启示。

Abstract: We study the formation of primordial black holes (PBHs) from large primordial perturbations that re-enter the horizon during an epoch with equation of state ${\rm w}\geq1/3$. We consider a log-normal curvature power spectrum of finite width $Δ$ and determine the collapse amplitude by numerical-relativity simulations of a self-gravitating perfect fluid. Threshold scans are performed for five values of $\rm w$ and five spectral widths, and the resulting numerical thresholds are used in the PBH abundance and scalar-induced gravitational-wave (SIGW) calculations. For comparison, we also evaluate the semi-analytical $q$-function prescription. It reproduces the numerical trend close to radiation domination and for nearly monochromatic profiles, but it is not a reliable threshold estimator for generic finite-width profiles in stiffer backgrounds. We show how collapse thresholds increase with both $Δ$ and $\rm w$, changing the curvature amplitude required for PBHs to constitute all of the dark matter and, consequently, the normalization of the accompanying SIGW signal.

</details>


### [220] [Conformally Interacting Dark Energy with Early and Late-Time Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07343)
*Shambel Sahlu,Abunie Gezahegn,Amare Abebe,Gonzalo J. Olmo,Diego Rubiera-Garcia*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了共形相互作用暗能量（CIDE）模型，通过引入一个与暗物质共形耦合的标量场来构建标量-张量理论，并利用多组宇宙学观测数据（CMB、BAO、SNe Ia）结合MCMC方法约束模型参数，评估其缓解H₀和S₈张力的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为缓解当前宇宙学中哈勃常数H₀和结构增长参数S₈的观测张力，探索暗能量与暗物质之间新型相互作用机制。

Method: 构建基于共形变换的标量-张量理论框架，设定能量流Q正比于暗物质能动量张量的迹；采用幂律形式的共形函数C(φ)∝(1+φ)^m；利用CMB（SPT、Planck 2018、ACT DR6）、BAO（DESI DR2）及多组Ia型超新星（PantheonPlus等）数据，结合COBAYA与修改版CLASS进行MCMC参数估计，并用AIC/BIC准则进行模型比较。

Result: 成功对CIDE及wCIDE模型进行了参数约束；发现该类模型在一定程度上可缓解H₀张力，但对S₈张力改善有限；模型复杂度增加未带来显著统计优势（AIC/BIC显示偏好ΛCDM）。

Conclusion: CIDE模型虽具理论吸引力并部分缓解H₀张力，但受限于现有数据与奥卡姆剃刀原则，尚未超越标准ΛCDM模型；未来更高精度的观测或可进一步检验其有效性。

Abstract: The conformal interacting dark energy (CIDE) model is investigated by introducing a scalar field representing dark energy (DE) coupled to dark matter (DM) via a conformal transformation, thereby yielding a class of scalar-tensor theories. The interaction term ${Q}$, that indicts the flow of energy between the dark sectors is proportional to the trace of the energy momentum tensor of the DM fluid, $T^{\mathrm{DM}}_{μν}$, as ${Q}=\frac{C'(φ)}{2C(φ)}g^{μν}T^{\mathrm{DM}}_{μν}$, where $C(φ)$ is the conformal function of a scalar potential (coupling) field. We assume the power-law parametrization $C(φ)\propto (1+φ)^m$, with $m$ a coupling parameter, and determine the direction and magnitude of the energy flow between the DM and DE components. The dynamical nature of DE is modeled by the two conformal interacting scenarios, CIDE and $w$CIDE, with $w$ the DE equation of state parameter. To test the viability of each model, we constrain them using a combination of early- and late-time cosmological data, namely: CMB measurements from the South Pole Telescope, Planck 2018, and the Atacama Cosmology Telescope (DR6) (\texttt{CMB SPA}); BAO data from the Dark Energy Survey (\texttt{DESI DR2 BAO}); and Supernova Type Ia distance compilations (\texttt{PantheonPlus, PantheonPlus + SH0ES, Union3}, and \texttt{DES Dovekie}). Parameter inference is performed with Monte Carlo Markov Chain (\texttt{MCMC}) simulations using \texttt{COBAYA} and a modified \texttt{CLASS}. Further statistical analysis using the Akaike information criterion (AIC) and the Bayesian information criterion (BIC) is summarized to assess the viability of the model in comparison with the standard cosmological model, investigating the model's potential to alleviate the $H_0$ and $S_8$ tensions.

</details>


### [221] [Recovering Large-Scale Clustering of Missing Galaxies for Galaxy--Gravitational-Wave Cross-correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07348)
*Tathagata Ghosh,Surhud More*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于补全光度限制星系巡天目录的新框架，能重建缺失星系并保持其成团性，从而提升引力波‘暗警报’的宇宙学推断精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波探测器探测距离增大导致现有星系目录因光度极限而严重不完备；当前主流方法假设缺失星系在共动体积中均匀分布，忽略了真实宇宙网中的成团性，造成物理不现实和系统偏差。

Method: 提出一种新框架，在补全星系目录时显式保持其空间成团特性，而非假设均匀分布；在不同光度极限和观测选择函数下的模拟星系目录上进行了验证。

Result: 该方法比传统均匀近似更真实地重建了缺失星系分布，显著提升了对引力波暗警报宿主星系识别及后续宇宙学参数推断的稳健性。

Conclusion: 考虑星系成团性的目录补全方法对于基于无电磁对应体引力波事件（如双黑洞并合）的精确宇宙学研究至关重要，本工作为此提供了可行且物理更自洽的解决方案。

Abstract: We present a framework for completing galaxy catalogs from flux-limited surveys which reconstructs the missing galaxy population while preserving its clustering properties. This is particularly important for cosmological analyses using gravitational-wave (GW) dark sirens without electromagnetic counterparts, such as binary black hole mergers, which constitute the majority of GW detections. As GW detectors probe increasingly larger distances, galaxy catalogs become progressively incomplete owing to survey flux limits. Current state-of-the-art statistical host identification methods typically account for this incompleteness by assuming that the missing galaxies are uniformly distributed in comoving volume. While this approximation can mitigate biases due to incompleteness, it neglects the clustering of galaxies within the cosmic web. Since galaxies and GW sources are both expected to trace the underlying large-scale structure, their spatial distributions are correlated, making a homogeneous reconstruction physically unrealistic, a shortcoming which our framework addresses. As a proof of concept, we apply the method to simulated galaxy catalogs with different flux limits and observational selection functions. Our approach provides a significantly more realistic completion of incomplete galaxy catalogs than the commonly adopted homogeneous-in-comoving-volume approximation, enabling more robust cosmological inference from gravitational-wave dark sirens.

</details>


### [222] [Ultra-slow-roll Inflation with Non-perturbative Non-Gaussianity and Scalar Induced Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07401)
*Manuel Drees,Chenhuan Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了暴胀期间超慢滚相位产生原初黑洞（PBHs）作为全部暗物质的可能性，通过δN分析发现该模型可具有O(1)量级的非高斯性，显著增强PBH丰度并削弱引力波信号，但仍可在LISA等未来探测器中被探测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索原初黑洞作为全部暗物质的可行机制，特别是利用暴胀期间的超慢滚相位来产生足够丰度的PBHs。

Method: 采用能描述CMB尺度穿出视界至暴胀结束全过程的标量场势能参数化形式，严格处理慢滚与超慢滚之间的过渡；使用δN方法分析非高斯性；在计算PBH丰度时保留曲率扰动与密度扰动之间的完整非线性关系，并将非高斯性纳入全阶处理。

Result: O(1)量级的非高斯性可使PBH丰度增强若干数量级，从而在固定PBH丰度下减弱引力波信号；尽管减弱，其信噪比仍显著高于LISA的灵敏度，尤其当PBH构成大部分暗物质时。

Conclusion: 超慢滚暴胀模型结合强非高斯性可有效生成足够PBH解释全部暗物质，且其伴随的引力波信号仍具可观测性，为暗物质起源与早期宇宙探针提供了新路径。

Abstract: An ultra-slow-roll phase during inflation could potentially produce the right abundance of primordial black holes (PBHs) for them to form all of dark matter (DM). We consider such a scenario, carefully treating the transitions from slow to ultra slow roll inflation and back, using a parametrisation of the inflaton potential that can describe inflation from the time when CMB scales crossed out of the horizon until its end. A $δN$ analysis shows that this model can possess $O(1)$ non-Gaussianity. When computing the primordial black hole abundance, we keep the full non-linear relation between curvature perturbations and the density contrast and consider the non-Gaussianity to all orders. We find that $O(1)$ non-Gaussianity is sufficient to enhance the PBH abundance by orders of magnitude, leading to reduced gravitational wave (GW) signals for fixed PBH abundance. The signal to noise ratio is computed for future gravitational wave observatories. We find that, in spite of the reduced strength, the GW signal is well above the sensitivity of LISA if PBHs form a significant component of DM.

</details>


### [223] [Filament-Arm Node Systems (FANS): A new probe of the winding and chirality of the cosmic web](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07792)
*Pedro da Silveira Ferreira,Renyue Cen*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的宇宙网结构——丝状臂节点系统（FANS），用于检验宇宙手性；通过测量其臂绕节点的缠绕（定义为‘宇宙螺旋性’）及其顺时针/逆时针方向（即‘手性’），并结合轴向条件估计器寻找三维手性场的偏好轴，对SDSS数据进行分析，结果未发现显著的手性破缺，支持ΛCDM模型下的宇称对称性，并发现宇宙螺旋性随时间减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验宇宙大尺度结构是否存在手性破缺（即宇称破缺），这是超越标准ΛCDM模型的重要探针；传统方法缺乏自然参考点和合适尺度，而宇宙网中的节点可提供几何基准。

Method: 定义Filament-Arm Node Systems（FANS）作为多条丝状体汇聚于一个节点的几何结构；引入‘宇宙螺旋性’量化臂绕节点的缠绕幅度，以符号表示顺时针/逆时针手性；设计轴条件外在估计器探测三维手性场的全局偏好轴；在两个SDSS丝状体重建样本上应用该方法，并用符号翻转零假设和27种参数变体评估统计显著性。

Result: 未发现全局或轴向意义上的显著手性偏好（CW/CCW无统计差异）；观测数据与宇称对称性一致；宇宙螺旋性随红移减小（即随宇宙时间演化而减弱）。

Conclusion: FANS是一种有效、几何直观的宇宙网对称性探针；当前结果支持标准ΛCDM模型的宇称守恒；螺旋性衰减可能反映引力作用下丝状体的逐渐拉直；为未来更大样本巡天（如DESI、Euclid）开展高精度手性检验奠定基础。

Abstract: We define Filament-Arm Node Systems (FANS), a new class of cosmic-web objects consisting of multiple filament arms connected to a common node, and use their geometry to construct an object-based test of cosmic chirality. With the cosmic-web node providing a natural reference point, FANS span characteristic scales of ~60Mpc/h, allowing the coherent winding of their arms around the node to be measured directly. We define cosmic spirality as the amplitude of this winding and handedness as its signed clockwise (CW) or counter-clockwise (CCW) sense. We further introduce an axis-conditioned extrinsic estimator that searches for the projected signature of a coherent three-dimensional axial pattern of the handedness field, corresponding to a preferred axis of winding. Applying these measurements to two distinct SDSS filament reconstructions, and assessing their significance with sign-flip nulls and 27 controlled variations of the FANS construction parameters, we find no significant preference for CW or CCW winding, either globally or around a preferred axis. The data are therefore consistent with parity symmetry, as expected in standard LCDM cosmology. We additionally find that cosmic spirality decreases with cosmic time, a trend that may reflect the gravitational straightening of filament arms. These first constraints establish FANS as a new probe of cosmic-web symmetry and large-scale morphology and provide a baseline for future parity tests with the much larger filament samples expected from upcoming surveys.

</details>


### [224] [The SRG/eROSITA All-Sky Survey: Early dark energy and Hubble constant from the cluster mass function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07834)
*J. Strunk,E. Artis,E. Bulbul,S. Grandis,N. Clerc,V. Ghirardini,M. Kluge,A. Liu,F. Balzer,J. Comparat,I. Chiu,Z. Ding,N. Malavasi,A. Merloni,T. Mistele,H. Miyatake,S. Miyazaki,K. Nandra,F. Kleinebreil,N. Okabe,M. E. Ramos-Ceja,J. S. Sanders,T. Schrabback,R. Seppi,S. Zelmer,X. Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用eROSITA首次全天巡天（eRASS1）的星系团样本，结合弱引力透镜质量校准和多种宇宙学观测数据，首次通过星系团数密度对轴子类早期暗能量（EDE）模型进行限制，得到f_EDE = 0.10^{+0.04}_{-0.03}，H_0 = 71.4 ± 1.4 km/s/Mpc（3.2σ显著性），支持EDE可缓解哈勃常数张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为缓解早期宇宙（如CMB）与晚期宇宙（如距离阶梯法）对哈勃常数H₀测量之间的张力，本文探索轴子类早期暗能量（EDE）模型，并首次尝试利用星系团数密度演化这一结构增长探针对其进行约束。

Method: 基于eROSITA eRASS1巡天在西银半球获得的5259个0.1<z<0.8星系团样本，选取其中0.1<z<0.45子样本；结合DES Y3、KiDS和HSC的弱透镜数据完成质量校准；构建星系团数密度作为红移和质量的函数，联合EDE理论预言的结构增长抑制效应，开展贝叶斯参数估计，并与CMB、BAO、CMB透镜等外部数据联合分析（排除距离阶梯数据）。

Result: 仅用eRASS1数密度数据给出f_EDE < 0.3（95% CL）；联合CMB、BAO、CMB透镜后，在排除距离阶梯数据前提下，获得f_EDE = 0.10^{+0.04}_{-0.03}（3.2σ）、H₀ = 71.4 ± 1.4 km/s/Mpc，结果支持非零EDE并使H₀与晚期测量一致。

Conclusion: 星系团数密度是检验EDE模型的有效独立探针；本工作首次实现该方法，结果与CMB分析一致且增强EDE解释H₀张力的可能性，凸显大样本X射线星系团巡天在前沿宇宙学中的关键作用。

Abstract: The evolution of the mass function of massive galaxy clusters is a well-established probe of cosmology. Using the eROSITA X-ray instrument on board the Spectrum Roentgen Gamma (SRG) mission, the western Galactic hemisphere of eROSITA's first All-Sky Survey (eRASS1) delivers a uniformly selected and securely confirmed sample of 5259 galaxy clusters spanning $0.1 < z < 0.8$. When combined with overlapping weak lensing data from DES Year 3, KiDS, and HSC for mass calibration, this dataset enables precise tests of the standard $Λ$CDM framework and beyond. In this work, we use the $0.1 < z < 0.45$ subsample of the eRASS1 cluster catalog to place constraints on an axion-like Early Dark Energy (EDE) scenario. Such models have been proposed as a possible mechanism to alleviate the tension between early- and late-Universe measurements of the Hubble constant. In this framework, a scalar field temporarily enhances the cosmic expansion rate around the epoch of recombination before rapidly diluting at later times, leaving distinct imprints on structure growth. We leverage these signatures to present the first constraints on EDE derived from galaxy cluster number counts. Using eRASS1 number counts alone, we obtain an upper limit on the maximum EDE fraction of $f_{\rm EDE} < 0.3$, consistent with primary CMB analyses. Combining our cluster analysis with primary CMB measurements, baryon acoustic oscillations, and CMB lensing, while notably excluding distance-ladder calibration data, yields results consistent with a nonzero EDE contribution and $H_0$ values compatible with late-Universe measurements. The most significant detection, at $\sim 3.2σ$, arises from the joint analysis of the eRASS1 sample with the full set of external datasets, yielding $f_{\rm EDE} = 0.10^{+0.04}_{-0.03}$ and $H_0 = 71.4 \pm 1.4$ km/s/Mpc. [ABRIDGED]

</details>


### [225] [Observation-driven simulations of strong lensing galaxy clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07840)
*L. Leuzzi,M. Meneghetti,A. Adam,L. Moscardini,C. Giocoli,P. Bergamini,Y. Hezaveh,M. Maturi,A. Mercurio,A. Moretti,Andrés A. Plazas Malagón,P. Rosati*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型星系团强引力透镜模拟方法，利用观测导出的经验关系建模星系团成员特性，并结合MPI并行计算与基于分数的扩散模型增强背景源图像，生成了100幅高保真模拟图像，经验证能可靠复现真实观测特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着宽视场成像巡天的发展，已知强引力透镜星系团数量将迅速增长；亟需高保真模拟数据来验证分析方法、训练机器学习算法。

Method: 开发新型模拟代码：1）基于前沿观测建立经验关系，刻画星系团成员的形态、颜色与空间分布；2）集成SkyLens最新版本；3）引入MPI并行编程提升效率；4）采用score-based扩散模型增强背景源图像。

Result: 生成100幅星系团强透镜模拟图像；模拟结果在星系团关键性质上与真实观测高度一致，验证了代码可靠性与图像真实性。

Conclusion: 该模拟框架显著提升了强透镜图像的真实性与生成效率，为未来大样本透镜系统分析和AI驱动的数据处理提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Galaxy clusters are the most powerful strong lenses: they greatly magnify the flux of distant and faint sources. Strong lensing also allows for the reconstruction of their mass distribution with-1% level accuracy and enables investigating cosmological parameters. The number of known systems of this type is bound to increase in the next years thanks to wide imaging surveys. Using simulations in this context is crucial to validate the analysis methods before they are applied to real data, and to train machine learning algorithms that can handle large volumes of images. In this work, we present a simulated set of one hundred images of galaxy clusters that we have produced with a novel code for simulating cluster-scale strong lenses. One of the main novelties of our approach, distinguishing it from other existing codes, is the use of empirical relations, derived from state-of-the-art observations, for modelling the characteristics, such as morphology, color, and spatial distribution of the cluster member population. This allows us to reliably reproduce the complexity of real observations. The simulations are partly carried out with the latest version of SkyLens, a code that creates mock observations of strong lensing events in different systems and observational setups. The main improvements we introduce are the use of the message passage interface (MPI) paradigm, a standard for parallel programming that leverages the use of several processors to perform some given task, and the implementation of score-based diffusion models to augment the images of the background sources. Together, they lead to more efficient and realistic image simulations. We also present the validation of the code and simulations by comparing the properties of the mock clusters to those of real ones. [Abridged]

</details>


### [226] [Non-linear Pairwise Velocities as a Cosmological Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07976)
*Mariana Jaber,Antonela Taverna,Rasmus Strid,Wojciech A. Hellwing*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于精确对守恒方程和非线性功率谱模型（HMcode-2020）的、经模拟校准的成对速度函数 v₁₂(r,a) 的似然方法，并在 QUIJOTE 和 TNG300-3 模拟上验证；将分析尺度延伸至非线性区（r_min 低至 4–8 h⁻¹Mpc）可显著提升 Ω_m、σ₈ 和 fσ₈ 的约束精度（不确定性降低达 70–86%，FOM 提升达 9.1 倍），为 DESI、Euclid、kSZ 等巡天提供高精度速度场分析新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前成对速度及成对kSZ观测分析大多局限于线性和准线性尺度（r ≳ 20–30 h⁻¹Mpc），而小尺度蕴含大量未被利用的宇宙学信息；亟需可靠建模以拓展至非线性区。

Method: 构建基于精确对守恒方程和PyCAMB+HMcode-2020非线性密度功率谱的v₁₂(r,a)模拟校准似然函数，并在QUIJOTE与TNG300-3多套数值模拟（涵盖不同分辨率、粒子数、盒子大小和红移）中系统验证其鲁棒性。

Result: 在QUIJOTE模拟中，将最小尺度r_min从50 h⁻¹Mpc扩展至4 h⁻¹Mpc（z=0.5）或8 h⁻¹Mpc（z=0），使Ω_m、σ₈、fσ₈的1σ不确定性分别降低70–74%、74–81%、最多86%；Ω_m–fσ₈联合Figure-of-Merit提升最高达9.1倍；但h与σ₈存在强简并，联合采样h会显著削弱约束。

Conclusion: 该模拟校准似然框架已通过多套先进N体模拟验证，为下一代宽场光谱巡天（DESI、Euclid、PFS）和kSZ观测在非线性尺度上提取宇宙学信息提供了可靠、高精度的分析工具。

Abstract: Peculiar velocities trace gravitational dynamics directly, complementing density-based clustering as wide-field spectroscopic surveys (DESI, Euclid, PFS) and kinetic Sunyaev-Zel'dovich (kSZ) measurements enter the precision era. Observational analyses of pairwise velocities and the pairwise kSZ signal remain largely restricted to linear and quasi-linear scales ($\gtrsim20-30$ $h^{-1}Mpc$), despite substantial cosmological information available at smaller separations. We present a simulation-calibrated likelihood for $v_{12}(r,a)$, built on the exact pair conservation equation and PyCAMB HMcode-2020 non-linear clustering, and validate it against the QUIJOTE and TNG300-3 suites across resolution, particle sampling, box size, and redshift. Using QUIJOTE, we show that extending the fit to non-linear scales tightens the $1σ$ uncertainty on $Ω_\mathrm{m}$ and $σ_8$ by $74\%$ and $81\%$ at $z=0.5$ ($r_{\rm min}=4$ $h^{-1}Mpc$), and by $70\%$ and $74\%$ at $z=0$ ($r_{\rm min}=8$ $h^{-1}Mpc$), relative to the linear-regime baseline ($r_{\rm min}=50$ $h^{-1}Mpc$) (all at fixed $r_{max}=140$ $h^{-1}Mpc$); $σ(fσ_8)$ tightens by up to $86\%$, and the joint $Ω_\mathrm{m}$-$fσ_8$ Figure-of-Merit improves by up to $9.1\times$ relative to the same baseline. These constraints are conditional on $h$ fixed at its QUIJOTE fiducial value, due to a near-degenerate response with $σ_8$; jointly sampling $h$ shifts and widens them substantially. This framework provides a validated route to exploiting that information in upcoming direct peculiar-velocity, redshift-space, and kSZ analyses.

</details>


### [227] [Interacting fluid cosmologies from the metric-affine framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07978)
*Fotios K. Anagnostopoulos,Damianos Iosifidis*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了度规仿射引力理论中自然产生的宇宙学流体间有效相互作用，分析了其对宇宙背景演化的影响，并通过观测数据验证了该框架优于标准ΛCDM模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度规仿射引力提供了一个将曲率、挠率和非度规性作为独立自由度的几何框架，可导出无需修改标准模型即可产生的暗物质-暗能量相互作用，为解决宇宙学疑难提供新思路。

Method: 推导了包含物质、辐射和暗能量分量的修正连续性方程，明确了能量交换的几何起源；结合Ia型超新星（Pantheon+）、宇宙钟和重子声学振荡（DESI DR2）等最新观测数据进行拟合，并使用AIC、BIC和贝叶斯证据等模型选择准则与ΛCDM对比。

Result: 所提度规仿射引力诱导的相互作用模型在所用数据集下略优于ΛCDM模型，能有效模拟演化暗能量行为并改变宇宙膨胀历史。

Conclusion: 度规仿射引力诱导的相互作用是一种理论自洽、观测可行的宇宙学替代方案，为理解暗能量本质提供了新途径。

Abstract: Metric-affine gravity provides a natural geometric framework in which spacetime curvature, torsion, and non-metricity are treated as independent degrees of freedom, leading to novel cosmological dynamics beyond General Relativity. A generic consequence of such theories is the emergence of effective interactions among the cosmological fluids, even in the absence of explicit phenomenological couplings. Interestingly, the above happens while the Standard Model of Particle physics remains unmodified. In this work, we investigate interacting cosmological models that arise from metric-affine gravity and analyze their implications for the background evolution of the Universe. We derive the modified continuity equations governing the matter, radiation, and dark-energy components, highlighting the geometric origin of energy exchange in the dark sector. We discuss how these interactions modify the expansion history and can effectively mimic evolving dark-energy behavior. The resulting cosmological scenarios are confronted with the most recent observational data from Type Ia Supernovae (Pantheon+), Cosmic Chronometers, and Baryon Acoustic Oscillations (DESI DR2). Moreover, using model selection criteria, e.g., Akaike Information Criterion, Bayesian Information Criterion and the Bayesian Evidence, we compare the aforementioned models against the standard $Λ$CDM model. We find that the proposed framework performs slightly better than $ΛCDM$, in the light of the data sets considered here. Our analysis demonstrates that metric-affine-induced interactions constitute a viable and theoretically motivated alternative for the description of the Universe.

</details>


### [228] [Probing Projection Effects in Optically-Selected Clusters with Velocity Dispersions Outliers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08095)
*K. Graham,T. E. Jeltema,C. -H. To,V. Wetzell,Kyle W. Davis,Conghao Zhou,H. -Y. Wu,E. S. Rykoff,J. DeRose,Michel Aguena,R. H. Wechsler*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过Cardinal模拟研究redMaPPer选样的星系团在DES Y3观测条件下的视向速度分布，发现35%的星系团存在异常高的速度弥散，多与视线方向结构污染（尤其在高红移、低丰富度时）相关，并在真实DES Y3数据中得到印证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升星系团丰度演化作为宇宙学探针的精度，需厘清星系团选样与质量估计中的系统误差，特别是视线方向结构带来的污染。

Method: 利用模拟DES Y3观测条件的Cardinal模拟，分析12,272个redMaPPer选样星系团的成员星系视向速度分布，统计其速度弥散、高斯性、子结构、红移及丰富度-晕质量关系，并与Buzzard模拟及DES Y3实测结果对比。
