Result: 35%的星系团为速度弥散异常体（偏高约400 km/s）；其中98%低丰富度异常体呈非高斯速度分布且更易含速度子结构；异常体倾向于更高红移、更大丰富度-质量散射；Buzzard模拟呈现类似趋势。

Conclusion: redMaPPer选样星系团样本存在显著的视线方向结构污染，尤其在高红移和低丰富度区域，这对基于丰富度的质量标定和宇宙学推断构成重要系统误差。

Abstract: The evolution of galaxy cluster abundance is a powerful probe of cosmology, but its efficacy depends on our ability to understand and control for systematics in the selection and mass estimation of clusters. We study redMaPPer-selected clusters in the Cardinal simulation, which simulates the DES Y3 observational conditions. Specifically, we investigate the line-of-sight velocity distributions of cluster member galaxies in 12,272 simulated clusters. We find that a significant fraction of clusters, 35%, have velocity dispersions that are high compared to their richness with an offset of $\sim 400$ km s$^{-1}$ between the outliers and the main population. A similar population of velocity dispersion outliers was found in the DES Y3 data. Nearly all of the outliers (98%) in a low-richness subsample have non-Gaussian velocity distributions, and they are more likely than the main population to have significant velocity substructure. These clusters also tend to have higher redshifts than clusters with typical velocity dispersions, and they show a larger scatter between halo mass and richness. Overall, our results point to a significant contamination of redMaPPer-selected samples from line of sight structure, particularly at high redshifts and low richness. Comparison to the Buzzard simulations shows similar results.

</details>


### [229] [Finding the distribution of matter using lenses - I: deconvolution-based reconstruction with CMB lensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08457)
*Ajoy Dawn,Jun-Qian Jiang,Dhiraj Kumar Hazra,Benjamin L'Huillier,Arman Shafieloo*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用Planck PR4、ACT DR6和SPT-3G的联合CMB透镜效应数据，通过协方差加权的修正Richardson-Lucy反卷积算法，重建了线性物质功率谱，并验证其在大尺度上与标准理论一致，在小尺度上增强可由非线性演化解释，且BAO特征信息部分保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物质功率谱是宇宙大尺度结构形成的关键探针，CMB透镜效应提供了跨红移的积分物质分布信息，但需从观测中高精度重建原始线性谱以检验结构形成模型。

Method: 采用协方差加权的修正Richardson-Lucy（MRL）反卷积算法，对Planck PR4、ACT DR6和SPT-3G联合CMB透镜测量数据进行处理，重建参考线性物质功率谱P_lin(k,0)；引入尺度依赖修正因子A(k)量化观测谱与2LPT非线性谱P_nl(k)的偏差。

Result: 重建谱在大尺度（k ≲ 0.1 Mpc⁻¹）与线性预测一致；在k ≳ 0.1 Mpc⁻¹出现系统增强，但A(k)在2σ内与1一致，表明增强源于标准非线性演化；BAO特征（k≈0.04–0.06 Mpc⁻¹）在重建谱中仍可识别。

Conclusion: CMB透镜联合数据能有效重建线性物质功率谱；观测到的小尺度增强无需新物理，可用现有非线性理论充分解释；BAO信息在透镜投影后仍有保留，支持其作为宇宙学标准尺的稳健性。

Abstract: The matter power spectrum is one of the primary statistical descriptors of the large-scale distribution of matter in the Universe and provides a powerful probe of cosmic structure formation. Measurements of cosmic microwave background (CMB) lensing offer an integrated view of the matter distribution over a wide range of redshifts, enabling the reconstruction of the underlying matter power spectrum. In this work, we reconstruct the reference linear matter power spectrum $ P_\text{lin}(k,0)$ from the baseline joint CMB lensing measurements of Planck PR4, ACT DR6, and SPT-3G using a covariance-weighted modified Richardson-Lucy(MRL) deconvolution algorithm. The reconstructed spectrum is found to be consistent with the fiducial linear prediction on large scales, while exhibiting a systematic enhancement for $k \gtrsim 0.1\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1}$, where nonlinear gravitational evolution becomes important. To investigate this behavior, we introduce a scale-dependent correction factor, $A(k)$, defined through $P(k)=A(k)\,P_{\rm nl}(k),$ where $P_{\rm nl}(k)$ is the fiducial nonlinear matter power spectrum obtained from 2LPT simulations. The reconstructed correction factor remains consistent with unity within $2σ$ confidence over the reconstructed range, indicating that the observed enhancement is well explained by the standard nonlinear evolution of the matter power spectrum. In addition, the reconstruction shows agreement with the fiducial BAO template around the BAO feature at $k\sim(0.04-0.06)\ {\rm Mpc}^{-1}$, indicating that some BAO-scale information survives the lensing projection.

</details>


### [230] [Finding the distribution of matter using lenses - II: deconvolution-based reconstruction with 3x2pt measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08460)
*Jun-Qian Jiang,Ajoy Dawn,Dhiraj Kumar Hazra,Benjamin L'Huillier,Arman Shafieloo*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于反卷积的框架，利用3×2pt观测数据检验晚期物质功率谱对基准宇宙学模型在不同尺度上的偏离，通过自由形式的尺度依赖调制A(k)和正则化修正Richardson-Lucy算法重建该调制，并在LSST十年数据模拟中验证了其对1%振幅振荡调制的检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验晚期物质功率谱是否严格遵循标准宇宙学模型预测，尤其是探测可能源于新物理（如暗能量或修改引力）引起的尺度依赖偏离，需一种模型无关、高分辨率的诊断方法。

Method: 构建尺度依赖调制A(k)对非线性物质功率谱的自由参数化；计算全天空、超越Limber近似下各类弱引力透镜与星系巡天观测（含密度、红移空间畸变、剪切、内禀对齐）对A(k)的线性响应；采用带弱lnk扩散的正则化修正Richardson-Lucy算法重建A(k)，并以迭代历史中最小χ²解为优选；引入后验加权一致性统计量，通过后验预测零假设模拟校准，避免Wilks定理依赖。

Result: 在Rubin/LSST Year 10类合成数据中，可恢复振幅≥1%、波数范围0.1–0.5 Mpc⁻¹、对数频率f≤10的振荡调制；1%振荡调制可在约2.6σ水平被探测；零假设A(k)=1与数据一致。

Conclusion: 正则化反卷积是一种有潜力的模型无关工具，可用于未来3×2pt巡天对物质功率谱进行尺度分辨的一致性检验。

Abstract: We present a deconvolution-based framework for testing scale-dependent departures of the late-time matter power spectrum from a fiducial cosmological model using $3\times2$pt measurements. We introduce a free-form scale-dependent modulation $A(k)$ of the fiducial nonlinear matter power spectrum and construct the linear response of binned galaxy-clustering, galaxy-galaxy-lensing, and cosmic-shear spectra to the discretized modulation $A(k)$. The response is evaluated with full-sky, beyond-Limber kernels including density, redshift-space-distortion, gravitational-shear, and intrinsic-alignment contributions. We reconstruct $A(k)$ using a regularized modified Richardson-Lucy algorithm, with weak diffusion in $\ln k$ and selection of the minimum-$χ^2$ solution along the iteration history. Using Rubin/LSST Year 10-like synthetic data, we find that oscillatory modulations with amplitudes $\gtrsim1\%$ can be recovered over $0.1\lesssim k\lesssim0.5\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1}$, provided the oscillation frequency $f\lesssim10$ on $\log_{10}[k/(0.2\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1})]$. We further introduce a posterior-weighted consistency statistic calibrated with posterior-predictive null mocks, thereby accounting for cosmological and nuisance-parameter uncertainties without relying on Wilks' theorem. The null case is consistent with $A(k)=1$, while a $1\%$ oscillatory modulation is detected at $\sim 2.6σ$. These results demonstrate the potential of regularized deconvolution as a model-independent consistency test of the matter power spectrum in future $3\times2$pt surveys.

</details>


### [231] [Vacuum Decay and Baryogenesis Associated with Primordial Black Holes at Finite Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08462)
*Qi Sun,Ligong Bian*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文在有限温度有效势框架下研究了原初黑洞（PBH）对真空衰变、引力波谱及重子不对称性的影响，发现PBH的引力扰动可促进真空衰变、增强引力波信号，并在特定质量范围内影响重子生成与洗脱过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究原初黑洞（PBH）如何通过其引力效应影响早期宇宙中的真空衰变动力学、引力波产生以及重子不对称性的演化，特别是当PBH霍金温度低于环境温度时的主导机制。

Method: 采用有限温度下的有效势框架，考虑PBH引力场对希格斯场构型的畸变效应，分析其对真空衰变率、引力波谱特征及 sphaleron 过程和BSM粒子衰变诱导重子不对称性的影响。

Result: PBH背景降低真空衰变指数、增强引力波峰值振幅并下移峰值频率；质量约10^11–10^13 g的PBH可降低sphaleron能量但洗脱效应可忽略；同质量范围PBH发射的BSM粒子衰变产生的重子不对称性足够小，不破坏BBN与CMB推断的重子丰度一致性。

Conclusion: PBH可通过纯引力机制显著改变真空衰变行为和引力波信号，并在特定质量窗口内对重子不对称性产生可控影响，为联合探测PBH、相变引力波与重子起源提供理论依据。

Abstract: Within finite-temperature effective potential framework, we study the effects of PBHs on vacuum decay, the associated gravitational wave spectrum, and the baryon asymmetry. For the PBHs considered, the Hawking temperatures are below the ambient temperature, and their dominant effect is taken to be the gravitational distortion of the Higgs field configuration. Relative to flat spacetime, the PBH background reduces the vacuum decay exponent, enhances the gravitational wave peak amplitude, and shifts the peak frequency downward. For PBH masses of roughly $10^{11}~{\rm g}-10^{13}~{\rm g}$, the sphaleron energy is reduced, while the small PBH abundance renders the associated washout negligible. Additionally, over roughly the same mass range, PBH-emitted BSM particle decays generate a baryon asymmetry too small to spoil the agreement between the baryon abundances inferred from BBN and CMB.

</details>


### [232] [Harnessing stellar kinematics to constrain dark energy with the double-source-plane gravitational lens SDSS J0946+1006](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08573)
*Daniel J. Ballard,Wolfgang J. R. Enzi,Thomas E. Collett,Coleman M. Krawczyk,Giovanni Granata,Tian Li,Geraint F. Lewis,Karl Glazebrook,Huimin Qu*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过多平面引力透镜建模结合动力学约束（HST成像与VLT-MUSE速度弥散测量），对强透镜系统SDSS J0946+1006进行分析，有效缓解质量片简并，获得与主流暗能量探针一致的w值，并在w₀-wₐ参数空间提供互补约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用SDSS J0946+1006这一多源强透镜系统精确限制暗能量状态方程，但传统多平面透镜模型存在质量片简并问题，需引入非透镜观测（如动力学）来破除。

Method: 联合HST近红外/近紫外成像重建与VLT-MUSE测得的速度弥散，构建含自由宇宙学参数的多平面透镜模型；考虑子结构与多极形变等密度剖面扰动，并结合Planck CMB与Pantheon超新星数据进行联合分析。

Result: 单独透镜分析得w = −1.01⁺⁰·⁰⁸₋₀.₁₃（Planck联合）或w = −0.99⁺⁰·¹³₋₀.₁₅（Pantheon联合）；在w₀-wₐ平面得(−0.87⁺⁰·¹⁰₋₀.₁₁, −0.22⁺⁰·²⁸₋₀.₂₅)，不加剧DESI BAO与ΛCDM的张力；第三光源贡献较弱但有潜力提升w₀wₐCDM约束。

Conclusion: 动力学信息增强的多平面透镜建模可单系统实现高精度、低张力的暗能量约束，是CMB与SNe之外的重要互补探针。

Abstract: SDSS J0946+1006 is an attractive target for measuring cosmological parameters through lens modelling. It is the best-studied galaxy-scale strong gravitational lens with multiple sources whose redshift separations are well-suited to constraining the dark energy equation of state. However, multi-plane lens models with free cosmological parameters risk a multi-plane mass-sheet degeneracy, although this can be lifted by a non-lensing deflector density profile tracer. We simultaneously reconstruct near-infrared and near-ultraviolet HST imaging whilst including a velocity dispersion measurement from VLT-MUSE to constrain the foreground deflector. Imaging is reconstructed faithfully regardless of whether the inferred kinematics are realistic, though we find the kinematic constraint essential for shifting the preferred cosmology into a region not in significant tension with other dark energy probes. Deflector density profile perturbations, via substructure and multipolar halo shape deformations, have only a modest effect on inferred cosmology. Combining our fiducial model with Planck CMB data yields $w=-1.01^{+0.08}_{-0.13}$; or, combined with Pantheon SNe Ia, $w=-0.99^{+0.13}_{-0.15}$. We further show that this system proves a remarkably valuable complementary probe in the $w_{0}$-$w_{a}$ plane of an evolving dark energy model, and that the DESI BAO tension with $Λ$CDM seen when combined with other datasets is not reproduced when combined with this lens, yielding $(w_{0}, w_{a})=(-0.87^{+0.10}_{-0.11}, -0.22^{+0.28}_{-0.25})$. The system's third source, visible with MUSE, only weakly constrains $w$CDM but may strengthen $w_{0}w_{a}$CDM constraints, though further mass-model complexity along its line of sight is required. Overall, kinematics-informed multi-plane lens modelling is a robust route to competitive dark energy constraints, even with a single system.

</details>


### [233] [$\texttt{LensFactory.jl}$: A general-purpose strong lens modeling package](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08649)
*Ashish Kumar Meena*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LensFactory.jl，一个基于Julia编写的高效、开源、通用强引力透镜建模软件包，支持单/多平面透镜建模与多种解析透镜轮廓，并通过模块化设计和原生多线程实现快速大规模透镜重建；在模拟星系团和JWST观测数据上验证了其质量重建能力，并提出了‘奇点图’作为新诊断工具评估透镜模型高阶导数差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强引力透镜建模工具在效率、可扩展性与灵活性方面存在局限，亟需一个高性能、易扩展、支持现代计算架构（如多线程）的开源建模框架，以应对JWST等新一代观测设备带来的高精度、大数据量建模需求。

Method: 开发基于Julia语言的LensFactory.jl软件包，利用其多重分派机制实现模块化设计，支持自定义透镜轮廓与推断方法；集成原生多线程加速关键计算步骤；在模拟数据（Ares、Hera）及真实JWST观测数据（SMACS J0723.3-7327）上进行质量重建验证；提出并应用‘奇点地图’作为评估透镜势高阶导数一致性的新诊断手段。

Result: LensFactory.jl可在桌面级机器上数小时内完成星系团尺度的全集群透镜重建；在模拟和真实数据中均获得高精度收敛与放大图；对SMACS J0723.3-7327建模达到图像平面RMS 0.46''，与已有结果一致；奇点图成功揭示不同模型在未被传统观测量约束的高阶结构上的差异。

Conclusion: LensFactory.jl是一个高效、灵活、可扩展的现代强透镜建模工具，兼具科学可靠性与工程先进性，其引入的奇点图拓展了透镜模型诊断维度，为未来高精度宇宙学透镜研究提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: We present $\href{https://github.com/akmeena766/LensFactory.jl}{\texttt{LensFactory.jl}}$, an efficient, open-source, general-purpose strong lens modeling package written in Julia. It supports single- and multi-plane lensing with a wide range of analytic lens profiles, and its modular design, built on Julia's multiple dispatch, makes it straightforward to add new profiles or inference methods, while native multi-threading parallelizes the computationally expensive steps, enabling full cluster-scale lens reconstructions within hours on a desktop-class machine. We validate the mass-reconstruction pipeline of $\href{https://github.com/akmeena766/LensFactory.jl}{\texttt{LensFactory.jl}}$ on two simulated galaxy clusters (Ares and Hera), recovering the convergence and magnification maps well where the lensing constraints lie, and on the JWST-observed cluster SMACS J0723.3-7327, obtaining a strong lens model (image-plane RMS of 0.46'') consistent with previously published results. In addition, we introduce singularity maps as a complementary diagnostic for comparing lens models, probing differences in the higher-order derivatives of the lensing potential, which remain largely unconstrained by standard observables such as image positions, time delays, and flux ratios. $\href{https://github.com/akmeena766/LensFactory.jl}{\texttt{LensFactory.jl}}$ is accompanied by a set of worked examples covering all core functionality.

</details>


### [234] [Why Azimuthal Averaging Works in Halo Lensing: Symmetry and Power Counting for Nonlinear Shear and Magnification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08825)
*Keiichi Umetsu*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了星系团弱引力透镜分析中，将二维透镜场压缩为方位角平均径向剖面时，非线性可观测量的环平均与基于平均场预测之间的差异（即分数残差），并利用投影的三轴NFW晕模型验证了该差异在不同质量-红移网格点及源平面下的大小，结果表明所有种群中位数残差均低于2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统星系团弱透镜分析常将二维透镜场压缩为方位角平均径向剖面，并从平均后的收敛率和剪切量计算非线性可观测量；但环平均的非线性可观测量通常不同于由平均场构造的预测值，该差异可能影响精度评估，因此需量化其大小及物理起源。

Method: 采用投影三轴NFW晕模型，在六个质量-红移网格点（$3\leq M_{200\mathrm c}/(10^{14}\,h^{-1}M_\odot)\leq20$, $0.2\leq z_l\leq0.5$）上模拟，固定源红移$z_s=1$（及对比的$z_s=2$），中心偏移服从尺度为$0.05r_{200\mathrm c}$的Rayleigh分布；在满足亚临界准则的径向范围$0.366\leq R/r_{200\mathrm c}\leq1$内，对各晕种群计算分数残差的中位数。

Result: 在$z_s=1$时，各可观测量的最大种群中位数分数残差分别为：约化剪切0.90%、逆放大率0.0093%、放大率0.64%、两种放大率偏差情形（$μ^{α-1}, α=0.3, 1.4$）最大0.18%；在$z_s=2$时所有残差增大，符合理论预期；在整个公共径向域和两个源平面上，所有种群中位数残差幅度均低于2%。

Conclusion: 该亚2%的残差精度源于一阶抵消、旋转对称性与球谐正交性，以及剩余非线性项的进一步弱透镜压低；表明当前主流的环平均弱透镜分析方法在典型观测条件下具有足够高的系统误差控制能力。

Abstract: Cluster weak-lensing analyses often compress two-dimensional lensing fields into azimuthally averaged radial profiles and evaluate nonlinear observables from the averaged convergence and shear. However, the ring average of a nonlinear observable generally differs from the mean-field prediction formed from the averaged fields. This difference, divided by the mean-field prediction, defines the fractional residual. For a complete ring, the difference begins at second order in angular fluctuations. For centered elliptical halos, rotational symmetry makes the shape contribution even in signed ellipticity, while the leading miscentering dipole is orthogonal to the shape quadrupole. We test these predictions using projected triaxial NFW halos at six mass-redshift grid points spanning $3\leq M_{200\mathrm c}/(10^{14}\,h^{-1}M_\odot)\leq20$ and $0.2\leq z_l\leq0.5$, for $z_s=1$. For the reference offset model, centering offsets follow a Rayleigh distribution with scale $0.05r_{200\mathrm c}$. For $0.366\leq R/r_{200\mathrm c}\leq1$, where at least $95\%$ of each population satisfies a conservative subcriticality criterion, we calculate the median of the fractional residuals across the retained halos for each population and radius. The largest population-median magnitudes are $0.90\%$ for reduced shear, $0.0093\%$ for inverse magnification, $0.64\%$ for magnification, and $0.18\%$ across the two magnification-bias cases $μ^{α-1}$ ($α=0.3, 1.4$). In the paired calculation at $z_s=2$, the maximum magnitude increases for every observable, consistent with weak-lensing power counting. Across both source planes and the common radial domain, every population median remains below $2\%$ in magnitude. This accuracy follows from first-order cancellation, rotational symmetry, and harmonic orthogonality, with further weak-lensing suppression of the remaining nonlinear terms.

</details>


### [235] [Optical Depths from the Thermal Sunyaev-Zel'dovich Effect with ACT DR6 and DESI DR1 Spectroscopic Galaxies and Optically-Selected Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08939)
*J. E. Moore,C. Popik,Y. Gong,Y-H. Hsu,E. M. Vavagiakis,N. Battaglia,R. Bean,D. Gruen,P. Gallardo,B. Hadzhiyska,G. Ockert,J. Aguilar,S. Ahlen,A. Aviles,F. Beutler,D. Bianchi,J. R. Bond,D. Brooks,E. Bulbul,A. Carnero Rosell,E. Chaussidon,T. Claybaugh,J. Comparat,A. de la Macorra,Biprateep Dey,P. Doel,S. Ferraro,A. Font-Ribera,J. E. Forero-Romero,E. Gaztañaga,Satya Gontcho A Gontcho,G. Gutierrez,K. Honscheid,M. Ishak,S. Juneau,T. Karim,R. Kehoe,M. Kluge,A. Kremin,A. Kusiak,O. Lahav,M. Landriau,L. Le Guillou,M. Manera,A. Meisner,R. Miquel,T. Mroczkowski,S. Nadathur,J. A. Newman,M. D. Niemack,H. E. Noriega,E. Paillas,W. J. Percival,F. Prada,I. Pérez-Ràfols,C. Ravoux,B. Ried Guachalla,G. Rossi,R. Ruggeri,L. Samushia,E. Sanchez,C. Saulder,D. Schlegel,J. Silber,M. Siudek,G. Tarlé,B. A. Weaver,E. J. Wollack,H. Zou*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用Atacama Cosmology Telescope DR6与Planck数据，对DESI DR1和eROMaPPer光学选源的星系团样本进行了堆叠tSZ效应测量，高显著性探测到tSZ信号，并系统评估和抑制了尘埃、射电源及CIB等前景污染，首次基于SZ观测直接建立了eROMaPPer样本的Compton-y与光学深度标度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确提取tSZ信号面临尘埃、射电源和宇宙红外背景（CIB）等前景污染的严峻挑战，尤其在小尺度上；需建立可靠的y-τ标度关系以支持宇宙学应用。

Method: 使用ACT DR6+Planck ILC y图和多频温度图，在~2'孔径内对LRG、BGS和eROMaPPer三类样本进行堆叠tSZ分析；通过模拟与实测结合，量化并扣除前景污染，构建y-τ标度关系。

Result: 在90 GHz图中，LRG、BGS和eROMaPPer样本tSZ信噪比分别达38、27和39；确认尘埃与射电源主导小尺度污染，CIB大尺度污染较弱；推导出的y-τ关系与kSZ独立测量结果一致；eROMaPPer样本的y-τ关系为首次直接由SZ观测得出。

Conclusion: 本工作实现了高精度tSZ堆叠测量与前景控制，验证了多波段联合分析的有效性，并首次为eROMaPPer样本提供了基于SZ的y-τ标度关系，为未来大视场巡天的星系团物理与宇宙学研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We present stacked thermal Sunyaev-Zel'dovich (tSZ) effect measurements for three samples of galaxy groups and clusters: those traced by the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Intstrument Data Release 1 (DESI DR1) luminous red galaxies (LRG) and the DESI DR1 Bright Galaxy Sample (BGS), and an eROMaPPer optically-selected sample from the DESI Legacy Imaging Survey. We use the latest Atacama Cosmology Telescope DR6 (ACT)+Planck component-separated internal linear combination (ILC) Compton-$y$ maps and ACT+Planck coadded 90, 150, and 220 GHz temperature maps to extract the tSZ signal within a $\sim2'$ disk aperture for sources binned by luminosity, richness, or mass. We measure the average tSZ signal with high statistical significance, with signal-to-noise ratios surpassing 38 for LRG, 27 for BGS, and 39 for the eROMaPPer sample using the 90 GHz ACT DR6+Planck map. We conduct a detailed study of systematics and foregrounds such as dust and cosmic infrared background (CIB) contamination, which remain a core challenge for tSZ analysis. For the LRG and BGS samples, we find that dust and radio source emission dominate the tSZ signal at scales near and below the disk aperture radius. Large-scale ($R>4'$) contamination from the CIB is less significant. We mitigate these contaminants to isolate the tSZ signal and use a combination of simulated and real measurements to develop Compton-$y-$optical depth ($\bar y-\bar τ$) scaling relations to infer optical depths, which are found to be in agreement with values measured using the pairwise kinematic SZ effect for the same tracer samples. The $\bar y-\bar τ$ scaling relation for the eROMaPPer sample is the first such relationship to be derived directly from SZ measurements.

</details>


### [236] [TILING I: Field-level Bayesian reconstruction of cosmological initial conditions during the epoch of reionization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09102)
*Nikolaos Triantafyllou,Andrei Mesinger,Steven Murray,Samuel Gagnon-Hartman*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出TILING方法，结合机器学习与宇宙学模拟，利用未来星系和21cm观测数据重建再电离时期（z≳5）的物质场初始条件，实现高精度（相关系数>0.8，功率谱误差<几%）的IC重建，并强调星系图对相位信息的关键约束作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 再电离时期（EoR）覆盖宇宙演化一半时间，但其初始条件（ICs）重建因辐射场建模困难而长期未被探索；亟需新方法融合多信使观测以提升IC约束能力。

Method: 提出TILING：一种混合机器学习流程，先用点估计获取IC初值，再通过基于分数的扩散网络生成后验样本；训练数据为不同UV星等极限的模拟星系图及对应含噪声和前景楔形污染的21cm图。

Result: 在典型巡天设定下，IC重建达到后验均值交叉相关系数>0.8、功率谱误差仅几%，可恢复被前景污染剔除的21cm模式；星系图主导IC相位约束，21cm干涉测量则更利于功率谱恢复。

Conclusion: TILING为EoR IC重建提供首个可行框架，支持后续观测规划、特定体积的再电离形态重建，并揭示JWST不可见星系对再电离的贡献，代码已开源。

Abstract: Reconstructing the initial conditions (ICs) of the matter field for an observed volume gives a complete picture of that region's temporal evolution. IC reconstruction is common at low redshifts but largely unexplored during the epoch of reionization (EoR; $z\gtrsim 5$), partly due to difficulties modeling inhomogeneous cosmic radiation fields. Yet the EoR spans half the observable Universe, offering unmatched potential for astrophysics and cosmology. Here we quantify how well upcoming galaxy and 21cm observations can constrain ICs during the EoR. We develop $\tt TILING$ ($\bf T$omographic $\bf I$nference of $\bf L$inear $\bf I$Cs via $\bf N$etwork $\bf G$rafting): a hybrid machine learning pipeline that first produces a point estimate of the ICs, which then improves a score-based diffusion network for generating posterior samples. We train $\tt TILING$ on mock galaxy maps at varying UV magnitude limits, plus corresponding 21cm maps at varying noise levels and foreground "wedge" contamination. For fiducial survey choices, we achieve accurate IC reconstruction (posterior mean cross-correlation coefficients >0.8 and power spectrum errors under a few percent) down to $\text{k}\lesssim 0.2 \text{cMpc}^{-1}$. While 21cm interferometry aids recovery of IC power spectra, most constraining power, especially for IC Fourier phases, comes from galaxy maps, underscoring the need for complementary observations when interpreting the 21cm signal. We show how $\tt TILING$ can recover 21cm power spectrum modes excised by foreground contamination. Our framework can also: (i) guide follow-up observations of sub-volumes of interest; (ii) reconstruct galaxy evolution and reionization morphology for specific volumes; and (iii) isolate the contribution to reionization from the vast majority of galaxies unobservable by optical/IR telescopes like $\it JWST$. Our code is publicly available.

</details>


### [237] [A Two-Dimensional Test of Cosmological Parity Violation with the 3-Point Correlation Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09106)
*William Ortolá Leonard,Zachary Slepian*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的对宇称破缺（PV）敏感的三维相关函数（3PCF）估计器，利用球壳上三角形的手性及有向面积矢量与壳法向量的点积实现PV探测，并提供了离散估计方法和巡天几何校正方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已利用四点相关函数（4PCF）的宇称奇模态搜寻宇宙大尺度结构中的宇称破缺，但需要一种互补且可交叉验证的新方法。

Method: 构建一种基于球面2D壳层上三角形手性的新3PCF估计器，通过其有向面积矢量与壳外法向量的点积引入宇称奇敏感性，并给出离散化实现及巡天几何修正方法。

Result: 成功设计出对宇称破缺敏感的奇宇称3PCF估计器，具备作为星系巡天中PV探测的补充检验与4PCF测量交叉验证的潜力。

Conclusion: 该奇宇称3PCF为宇宙学中宇称破缺探测提供了新工具，拓展了高阶相关函数在基本对称性检验中的应用。

Abstract: Searches for parity violation (PV) in the Universe's large-scale structure have recently been carried out using parity-odd modes of the galaxy 4-Point Correlation Function (4PCF). Here, we introduce a new estimator for a 3-Point Correlation Function (3PCF) that is sensitive to PV. The construction exploits the handedness of triangles formed on 2D spherical shells, where mirror-image configurations cannot be related by proper rotations within the surface. By dotting the oriented area vector with an externally-defined normal vector to the shell, we obtain sensitivity to PV. We present a discrete estimator for this statistic and also show how to correct for the survey geometry. This parity-odd 3PCF provides a complementary test of PV in galaxy surveys and a potential cross-check of PV 4PCF measurements.

</details>


### [238] [Mitigating baryonic effects in weak lensing with higher-order statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09131)
*Andreas Tersenov,Sacha Guerrini,Jean-Luc Starck,Martin Kilbinger*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了弱引力透镜中小尺度统计受重子反馈影响的问题，比较了功率谱、星形峰值计数和星形ℓ1-范数三种统计量对重子反馈的敏感性，并提出BNT变换以优化尺度截断，结果表明高阶统计量在无需重子建模时即显著优于功率谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱引力透镜是重要的宇宙学探针，但小尺度统计受重子反馈污染严重；而高阶统计虽能捕获非高斯信息，其对反馈的敏感性仍制约Stage IV巡天精度。

Method: 基于cosmoGRID V1模拟套件（含重子校正模型的收敛图）与基于仿真的神经后验估计方法，量化功率谱、星形峰值计数和星形ℓ1-范数在不同巡天面积下的重子反馈偏差，并评估BNT变换对尺度截断的优化效果。

Result: 未建模反馈导致Stage IV尺度下所有三类统计量的宇宙学参数偏差超2σ；仅用‘重子安全’大尺度时，星形ℓ1-范数FOM仍近似为功率谱的2倍；BNT变换将功率谱FOM提升约1.4倍，但会放大高阶统计的形状噪声。

Conclusion: 高阶统计量（尤其星形ℓ1-范数）在无需重子建模时已明显优于功率谱，且随重子建模改进优势更大；BNT变换有助于功率谱，但不适用于噪声敏感的高阶统计。

Abstract: Weak gravitational lensing is a premier cosmological probe, but its small-scale statistical power is compromised by baryonic feedback. Higher-order statistics capture non-Gaussian information that the power spectrum misses, yet their sensitivity to feedback remains a concern for Stage IV surveys.
  We quantify how unmodeled feedback biases the cosmological parameters inferred from the angular power spectrum (PS), starlet peak counts, and the starlet $\ell_1$-norm, and we determine the scale cuts needed to remove that bias. We also test the Bernardeau-Nishimichi-Taruya (BNT) transform as a strategy for more precise scale cuts. Our analysis is based on the cosmoGRID V1 suite, which imprints feedback on dark matter convergence maps with a baryon correction model, and on simulation-based inference with neural posterior estimation, carried out across footprints ranging from Stage III-like to the full sky.
  We find that biases grow with survey area, exceeding $2σ$ for all three statistics at Stage IV-like footprints, and removing them costs a substantial fraction of the signal. Restricted to these ``baryon-safe'' scales, the starlet $\ell_1$-norm still reaches a figure of merit almost twice that of the PS. The BNT transform localizes the baryonic sensitivity to the lowest transformed redshift bin and improves the PS figure of merit by a factor of $\sim$1.4, while its linear mixing of shape noise inflates the contours of the map-based higher-order statistics. Higher-order statistics therefore deliver a substantial gain over the power spectrum with no baryonic modeling at all, and an even larger one as modeling improves.

</details>


### [239] [Baryonification IV: Constraining baryonic feedback with X-ray gas fractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09144)
*Jozef Bucko,Andrina Nicola,Aurel Schneider,Michael Kovač,Sambit K. Giri,Robert Reischke,Esra Bulbul,Nicolas Clerc,Ang Liu,Iacopo Bartalucci,Alexandre Refregier*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于baryonification模型的前向建模框架，将星系团和星系群的三维热气体密度与温度分布，与X射线表面亮度/光度观测及物质功率谱压制联系起来；利用CHEX-MATE和eFEDs样本校准并测量了不同质量尺度下的热气体分数，并约束了重子反馈对功率谱的压制幅度（6%–23%），同时指出L_X–M关系与反馈强度存在简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重子反馈会再分布暗物质晕周围的气体，压制物质功率谱，而X射线观测是追踪热气体分布与性质的主要手段之一；亟需统一框架将X射线观测、气体含量与功率谱压制定量关联。

Method: 构建基于baryonification模型的前向建模框架，连接三维气体密度/温度剖面与X射线表面亮度/光度剖面，并进一步关联至物质功率谱压制；使用CHEX-MATE（SZ选）和eFEDs（X射线选）样本进行校准与测量，显式处理X射线选择效应。

Result: 在M_500c = 3×10¹³ M⊙和3×10¹⁴ M⊙处测得热气体分数分别为0.029±0.006和0.078±0.004；在k=1 h/Mpc和k=5 h/Mpc处功率谱压制分别为6%和23%；发现L_X–M关系与反馈强度存在简并，不同反馈模型可产生相同L_X–M但不同X射线剖面形状。

Conclusion: 该框架成功桥接X射线观测与重子反馈对宇宙大尺度结构的影响；结果支持强反馈作用，为未来eROSITA等巡天开展模拟驱动推断奠定基础。

Abstract: Baryonic feedback redistributes gas around dark matter halos, suppressing the matter power spectrum at scales now probed by weak lensing surveys. X-ray observations directly trace this hot gas, and are one of the main probes of its distribution and properties. We present a forward-modelling framework, built on the baryonification model, linking the three-dimensional gas density and temperature profiles of groups and clusters to observed X-ray surface brightness and luminosity profiles on one side, and to matter power spectrum suppression on the other. We validate the model against independent three-dimensional density reconstructions from the literature, and examine our temperature and metallicity treatment in the group-scale regime. Applying this framework to the SZ-selected CHEX-MATE and X-ray-selected eFEDs samples, we measure gas fractions across the group-to-cluster mass range while accounting for X-ray selection effects, with the first published gas fractions based on CHEX-MATE data. Combining both samples, we derive a joint constraint on the hot gas fraction retained by groups and clusters as a function of mass and on the baryonic suppression of the matter power spectrum. We find $f_{\rm gas} = 0.029 \pm 0.006$ at $M_{500c} = 3\times 10^{13}M_\odot$, $f_{\rm gas} = 0.078 \pm 0.004$ at $M_{500c} = 3\times 10^{14}M_\odot$, and suppression of 6% at $k=1\,h/\rm Mpc$ and 23% at $k=5\,h/\rm Mpc$. Our findings are consistent with recent kinematic Sunyaev-Zel'dovich results, hinting at strong feedback. We also show that the $L_X$-$M$ relation is degenerate with feedback strength, and that different feedback scenarios produce distinct X-ray profile shapes that map onto the same $L_X$-$M$ point. This work is a first step toward extending the framework to forward-model diffuse X-ray emission at the map level for simulation-based inference in upcoming wide-area X-ray surveys such as eROSITA.

</details>


### [240] [Differential Polarization Calibration: A Consistency Test for Cosmic Birefringence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09149)
*Anto I. Lonappan,Brian Keating,Kam Arnold*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种不依赖前景EB模型的、对宇宙微波背景（CMB）手性旋转角β不敏感的差分估计器，用于独立检验基于前景辅助的手性分析（如Minami-Komatsu方法）中探测器偏置角的校准结果；应用于Planck NPIPE数据，验证了MK校准模式的可靠性，从而增强了对其推断出的birefringence角β的信心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMB手性旋转角β与仪器偏振角偏移完全简并，MK似然函数虽利用银河系前景极化分离二者，但其推断的探测器角度需一个不复用前景EB模型的独立诊断手段。

Method: 构建一种对手性旋转角β盲的差分估计器，基于反对称与对称交叉谱；该估计器对地图对的公共旋转完全抵消，直接测量探测器间相对角度差Δα_ij，并在规范固定网络中重构可观测的探测器依赖校准模式。

Result: 应用于8个Planck NPIPE探测器集地图，差分重构结果与MK校准模式一致（χ²=8.18, dof=7, PTE=0.32），所有共用数据残差均低于1.8σ；条件共模重构得β=0.37±0.12°。

Conclusion: 该差分检验虽非独立数据验证，但提供了独立建模路径，显著增强了MK推断β所依赖的校准基础的可信度；该方法可作为未来EB基手性分析的标准验证层。

Abstract: The cosmic microwave background birefringence angle $β$ is exactly degenerate with a common instrumental polarization-angle offset. The Minami--Komatsu(MK) likelihood separates these quantities using Galactic foreground polarization, so its inferred detector angles merit a diagnostic that does not reuse the foreground-$EB$ model. We develop a birefringence-blind differential estimator from antisymmetric and symmetric cross-spectra and apply it for the first time as an explicit consistency test of a foreground-assisted birefringence analysis. For a map pair, the common rotation cancels identically and the estimator measures $Δα_{ij}=α_i-α_j$; combining all pairs in a gauge-fixed network reconstructs the observable detector-dependent calibration pattern. Applied to eight Planck NPIPE detector-set maps, the differential reconstruction agrees with the MK pattern, giving $χ^2=8.18$ for seven degrees of freedom (PTE=0.32), with all shared-data residuals below $1.8σ$. Because both reconstructions use the same maps, this is an independent estimator and modeling route rather than an independent-data confirmation. The agreement therefore strengthens confidence in the calibration foundation of the MK-inferred $β$ without providing an independent absolute measurement of it. A conditional common-mode reconstruction gives $β=0.37\pm0.12^\circ$. The differential test uses existing data and can serve as a validation layer for future $EB$-based birefringence analyses.

</details>


### [241] [Sizing the Universe with DESI Galaxy Sizes: Plain Fundamentals of Fundamental-Plane Lensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09151)
*Kun Xu,Jiaqi Wang,Ravi K. Sheth,J. Aguilar,S. Ahlen,F. Beutler,D. Bianchi,D. Brooks,A. Carnero Rosell,T. Claybaugh,A. de la Macorra,P. Doel,J. E. Forero-Romero,E. Gaztañaga,G. Gutierrez,K. Honscheid,C. Howlett,M. Ishak,J. Jimenez,S. Juneau,R. Kehoe,D. Kirkby,O. Lahav,M. Landriau,L. Le Guillou,M. Manera,A. Meisner,R. Miquel,J. Moustakas,S. Nadathur,J. A. Newman,W. J. Percival,F. Prada,I. Pérez-Ràfols,C. Ravoux,G. Rossi,R. Ruggeri,E. Sanchez,C. Saulder,D. Schlegel,M. Schubnell,H. Seo,J. Silber,M. Siudek,G. Tarlé,B. A. Weaver,H. Zou*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于光谱学的星系-星系透镜效应放大测量方法，利用DESI巡天中326万个亮红星系（LRG）的基面（FP）尺寸残差（δ_r）进行弱引力透镜分析，克服了传统形状测量中的系统误差，结果与剪切测量一致，且对尺寸测量误差鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱引力透镜测量主要依赖深场成像的星系形状畸变，易受本征对齐、测光红移误差和形状测量偏差等系统误差影响，亟需一种更干净、独立的测量手段。

Method: 利用DESI亮红星系（LRG）的基面关系预测其本征尺寸，通过速度弥散σ₀和面亮度Iₑ拟合有效半径Rₑ，得到尺寸残差δ_r；结合透镜放大效应与放大偏差校准，构建收敛（κ）响应模型，并测量面密度剖面。

Result: 获得的面密度轮廓精度与第三阶段剪切巡天（500万–2000万高红移源）相当；结果与剪切测量一致；收敛乘性偏差仅约为尺寸偏差的-0.2倍，表明方法对尺寸测量误差鲁棒。

Conclusion: 基于基面的透镜效应测量是一种干净、光谱学驱动、互补的宇宙结构探针，可规避传统形状测量的主要系统误差。

Abstract: Weak gravitational lensing provides a powerful way to map cosmic structure, but most current measurements rely on galaxy shape distortions from deep imaging surveys and are affected by systematics such as intrinsic alignments, photometric-redshift uncertainties and shape-measurement biases. Here we present a spectroscopic galaxy-galaxy lensing magnification measurement using Fundamental-Plane (FP) size residuals, $δ_r\equivΔ\log_{10}R_\mathrm{e}$, from 3.26 million DESI luminous red galaxy (LRG) sources behind DESI Bright Galaxy Survey lenses. The FP-like relation predicts the intrinsic sizes of LRGs from lensing-invariant quantities, including velocity dispersion $σ_0$ and surface brightness $I_\mathrm{e}$, with a scatter of about 0.06-0.07 dex. Lensing magnifies LRG sizes, giving the direct convergence response $δ_r(κ)=κ/\ln 10$, but we show that the full lensing response is modified by magnification bias, because fitting $R_\mathrm{e}$ with $I_\mathrm{e}$ inevitably induces a magnitude dependence in $\barδ_r(m)$. After calibrating this response, we recover surface-density profiles with uncertainties comparable to those from individual Stage-III shear surveys using 5-20 million higher-redshift sources. The corresponding excess surface-density profiles agree with shear-based measurements. We further show that the estimator is robust to size-measurement uncertainties, with a convergence multiplicative bias only $\simeq -0.2$ times the size bias. FP lensing therefore provides a clean, spectroscopic and complementary probe of cosmic structure.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [242] [Cosmology, Cluster Algebras, and $\boldsymbol{u}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05606)
*Daniel Glazer,Austin Joyce*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了共形耦合标量场在德西特时空中的波函数和关联子所具有的簇代数结构，通过u-变量将宇宙学运动学与簇代数联系起来，并利用簇相容性条件引导符号（symbol）的构造；链状费曼图对应A_{2n−2}型簇代数，环状图对应B_{2n−1}型，且波函数与关联子在链情形共享相同簇结构，而在环情形中波函数被簇结构唯一确定，关联子则不满足相同相容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将宇宙学可观测量（如波函数、关联子）与数学上结构清晰的簇代数建立联系，以揭示其内在对称性与约束，并为高阶计算提供引导原则。

Method: 利用u-变量作为桥梁，将宇宙学图（链与环状费曼图）的运动学参数映射到对应簇代数（A_{2n−2}与B_{2n−1}型）的簇变量；借助簇相容性限制符号字母的选择，进而实现‘bootstrap’式构造。

Result: 确立了链状与环状动量积分图分别对应A_{2n−2}与B_{2n−1}簇代数；发现链情形下波函数与关联子共享同一簇结构，而环情形中仅波函数严格满足簇相容性，关联子不满足；由此簇结构可唯一确定环状波函数。

Conclusion: 簇代数为德西特时空中标量场波函数提供了自然的组合与代数框架；其相容性是一种强约束，不仅能分类可能的符号结构，还能在特定拓扑（如环）下唯一确定物理量，暗示簇结构可能是宇宙学振幅更深层的组织原理。

Abstract: We explore the cluster algebra structures present in the wavefunction and correlators of conformally coupled scalar fields in de Sitter space. To connect cosmological kinematics to cluster variables, we utilize the so-called $u$-variables that appear on both sides. Since $u$-variables parameterize a rigid space, it is natural to identify the ones that appear from cosmological graphs and those that arise from cluster algebras. This provides a mapping between kinematic variables and cluster variables. For $n$-site chain Feynman graphs, this gives a coordinate-invariant relation to $A_{2n-2}$-type cluster algebras, while in the case of $n$-site cycle graph momentum integrands, the relevant cluster algebra is $B_{2n-1}$, as found previously. Since kinematic variables are mapped to nonlinear combinations of cluster variables, cluster compatibility imposes strong conditions on possible symbol entries. We use this notion of compatibility to bootstrap the symbol. In the chain case, both the cosmological wavefunction and products of lower-point functions satisfy cluster compatibility, with the correlator a particular combination, so that the wavefunction and correlator share the same cluster structure. In the cycle case, however, the wavefunction appears to be uniquely selected by the cluster structure, and the correlator does not display the same compatibility as the wavefunction.

</details>


### [243] [Matrix Holography on an Optical Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05613)
*Robin Löwenberg,Julian Sonner*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于原子系综晶格和时间平均Floquet动力学的模拟协议，用于实现BFSS类矩阵量子力学中的四体势，具有多项式标度和强耦合可调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为克服门电路数字实现中电路深度随矩阵尺寸N增长的问题，寻求一种更易实验实现的大N标度量子模拟方案。

Method: 采用原子系综晶格与时间平均Floquet动力学构建四体相互作用，通过固定数量的控制阶段每周期实现目标模型，控制频率范围是主要标度成本。

Result: 证明控制频率需求至多按O(N³)增长，实现了多项式标度；并验证耦合参数可调至远大于1，支持强耦合全息相模拟。

Conclusion: 该模拟协议为大N矩阵模型提供了实验友好、可扩展且支持强耦合物理的模拟路径。

Abstract: We propose an analog quantum-simulation protocol based on a lattice of atomic ensembles and time-averaged Floquet dynamics to construct the four-body potentials that typically appear in bosonic BFSS-like matrix quantum mechanics. In contrast to gate-based digital implementations requiring deep circuits, our approach generates the target model through a fixed set of control stages per Floquet cycle, keeping the number of consecutive unitaries constant with respect to the matrix size $N$. The principal scaling cost in our construction is given by the frequency range required to control the growing lattice. We show that this cost grows at most as $\mathcal{O}(N^3)$, giving a polynomial scaling route to large$-N$, attractive to experimental implementation. Additionally, we demonstrate that the coupling parameter can be tuned beyond $\mathcal{O}(1)$, enabling the simulation of strong-coupling physics necessary for holographic phases.

</details>


### [244] [Equation of State of a Strongly Coupled Perfect Fluid with Spin from Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05630)
*Andres Anabalon,Horatiu Nastase*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用全息原理推导了2+1维强耦合、有限自旋的相对论完美流体的状态方程，其对偶引力描述为AdS₄中的旋转黑洞；研究发现存在一个临界旋转速率，超过后流体热力学失稳，并给出夸克胶子等离子体的旋转约束条件；此外，同一引力解还对应另一个非旋转黑洞及其具有‘奇异’状态方程的旋转边界流体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立强耦合、有限自旋相对论流体的热力学描述，并揭示黑洞旋转与流体角动量及热力学稳定性之间的全息联系。

Method: 基于AdS/CFT对应，将2+1维边界流体映射到4维反德西特时空（AdS₄）中的旋转黑洞，利用霍金温度、角速度和贝肯斯坦-霍金熵导出流体状态方程，并分析其局域热力学稳定性。

Result: 得到强耦合自旋流体的状态方程；发现临界旋转阈值，给出约束条件4πT/ω > 2.77239；揭示同一引力解可对应两个不同边界——其中一个对应具有奇异状态方程的旋转流体。

Conclusion: 全息方法成功给出了有限自旋强耦合流体的状态方程，建立了黑洞旋转、内在角动量与流体热力学稳定性之间的直接联系，并拓展了对双边界全息结构的理解。

Abstract: We derive the equation of state of a strongly coupled relativistic perfect fluid with finite spin in $2+1$ dimensions using holography. The dual gravitational description is provided by a spinning black hole in $AdS_4$, whose Hawking temperature, angular velocity, and Bekenstein-Hawking entropy determine the thermodynamic properties of the boundary fluid. We obtain the equation of state and analyze its local thermodynamic stability, finding a critical rotation above which the fluid becomes thermodynamically unstable providing a bound for the rotation of a strongly coupled quark gluon plasma at temperature $T$ and angular velocity $ω$ given by $\frac{4πT}ω> 2.77239$. Remarkably, the same gravitational solution contains a second black hole associated with a second boundary. Although this black hole is non-spinning, its dual fluid rotates and possesses a distinct, ``exotic'' equation of state. Our results provide a holographic equation of state for strongly coupled matter with finite spin and establish a direct connection between black-hole rotation, intrinsic angular momentum, and the thermodynamic stability of relativistic fluids.

</details>


### [245] [Birth and Death in Two-color ABJM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05653)
*Marco S. Bianchi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文精确求解了U(2)×U(2) ABJM理论中半BPS算符两点度规的主导量子变形，发现其两圈修正在Schur基下构成作用于两行杨图链上的Jacobi算子，并通过基变换与带约束的SU(2) Casimir及随机过程等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解ABJM理论中半BPS算符的量子修正结构，特别是度规在有限秩下的精确变形，以揭示其代数与随机过程深层联系。

Method: 在耦合无关的Schur基下计算树图归一化的两圈修正；引入基态变换将其映射为生灭过程；利用正交多项式（Racah多项式）分析本征模；考察大n极限与连续极限下的动力学行为；建立与受限SU(2) Casimir的等价性。

Result: 两圈修正为作用于两行杨图链上的Jacobi算子；该算子经基态变换后对应于以Plancherel测度（限制于至多两行）为平稳分布的可逆生灭过程；本征函数为Racah多项式；大n极限趋近径向Ornstein-Uhlenbeck动力学；等价于受限SU(2) Casimir算子。

Conclusion: ABJM理论中半BPS两点度规的量子变形展现出丰富的代数结构（SU(2)对称性）、组合结构（杨图与Plancherel测度）和随机结构（生灭过程），三者统一于Jacobi算子框架下。

Abstract: We solve exactly the leading quantum deformation of the two-point metric of half-BPS operators in $U(2)\times U(2)$ ABJM theory at finite rank. In the natural coupling-independent Schur frame, its tree-normalized two-loop correction is a Jacobi operator on the chain of two-row Young diagrams. A ground-state transform maps it to a reversible birth--death process whose stationary distribution is the Plancherel measure conditioned on diagrams with at most two rows. Its eigenmodes are Racah polynomials, its large-$n$ limit approaches radial Ornstein--Uhlenbeck dynamics, and an equivalent two-spin description identifies the Jacobi operator with a restricted $SU(2)$ Casimir.

</details>


### [246] [Searching for Bell-CHSH-Violating Bose Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05685)
*T. Newton,S. P. Sorella*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过精确的谱纤维方法，为相对论性自由玻色场中的贝尔不等式构建了显式的有界楔形局域可观测量，并严格证明了其楔形局域性、自伴性、幺正性和精确反对易性；平移单元可观测量能明确展示贝尔违背（含可控无穷尾误差），加入反射分支后违背程度显著增大，表明简单有界局域可观测量可构造性地揭示相对论量子场论中的贝尔非定域性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在相对论量子场论中构造显式的、有界且局域的可观测量以揭示贝尔非定域性，并验证此前平移单元结果并非人为假象而是严格有效的下界。

Method: 采用精确的谱纤维（spectral-fibre）方法，将相关正则对转化为可测的纤维对合算子，建立算子代数框架下的楔形局域性证明。

Result: 成功构造出满足楔形局域性、自伴性、幺正性和精确反对易性的有界可观测量；平移单元方案实现可控误差下的贝尔违背，加入反射分支后违背程度显著增强。

Conclusion: 简单有界局域可观测量确实能在相对论量子场论中构造性地暴露贝尔非定域性；平移单元所得贝尔违背是一个严格成立的下界，而非计算假象。

Abstract: We construct explicit bounded wedge-local observables for Bell inequalities in a relativistic free Bose field using an exact spectral-fibre approach. The construction turns the relevant canonical pair into measurable fibre involutions and provides an operator-algebraic proof of wedge locality, self-adjointness, unitarity, and exact anticommutation. Translation-cell observables yield an explicit Bell violation with a controlled infinite-tail error, while inclusion of reflection branches produces a substantially larger violation. These results demonstrate that simple bounded local observables can expose Bell nonlocality constructively in relativistic quantum field theory and that the translation-cell result is not artifact but a strictly valid lower bound.

</details>


### [247] [AMSB in Truly Confining Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06093)
*Riku Ishikawa,Hitoshi Murayama,Shota Saito*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有小反常介导超对称破缺的超对称t-禁闭规范理论的变形，并确定了其中全局对称性的破缺模式，结果原则上可与非超对称理论的格点模拟进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超对称t-禁闭理论在小反常介导超对称破缺下的变形行为，并建立其与非超对称理论（如格点模拟）之间的可比性。

Method: 理论分析，重点研究全局对称性破缺模式。

Result: 识别出该类超对称理论中全局对称性的具体破缺模式；除一种伪实且手征的情形外，其余结果原则上可与非超对称理论的格点模拟对比。

Conclusion: 超对称t-禁闭理论在小反常介导破缺下的变形具有明确的对称性破缺特征，为连接超对称与非超对称强耦合物理提供了理论桥梁。

Abstract: We study deformation of supersymmetric $t$-confining (truly confininig) gauge theories with small anomaly mediation of supersymmetry breaking. We identify breaking pattern of global symmetries in the theories. These results can be compared in priciple to lattice simulation of non-supersymmetric theories (except for one of the cases where the theory is pseudoreal and chiral).

</details>


### [248] [Landau and cluster structures of one-loop amplitudes in ${\mathcal N}=4$ SYM in dimensional regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05716)
*Athanasia-Konstantina Angelopoulou,Ruth Britto,Matteo Parisi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过研究一维平面N=4超对称杨-米尔斯理论中任意多重性和螺旋度的一圈散射振幅，揭示了广义幺正性、朗道分析和簇邻接性之间的内在联系，并证明了其符号在特定簇代数中满足邻接性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义幺正性、朗道分析和簇邻接性分别从不同角度刻画散射振幅的结构，但它们之间的具体联系尚不明确。本文旨在通过具体计算（一维平面N=4 SYM一圈振幅）使三者接口显式化。

Method: 利用维度正则化下的一圈平面N=4 SYM振幅，对其权为2的符号进行分解；引入LS部分、代数四质量扇区与剩余项；借助BCFW类全局留数定理证明两质量三角关系；结合树级振幅体几何投影解释该关系；并系统验证簇邻接性在Fl_{2,4;n}旗簇代数中的普适成立性。

Result: 1）一阶符号可分解为LS部分、四质量代数部分与残余项；2）盒积分贡献的某些字母存在抵消，由两质量三角关系解释；3）该关系可通过全局留数定理证明，并有树级振幅体几何诠释；4）全有理符号（含红外发散部分）对所有多重性与螺旋度均满足Fl_{2,4;n}旗簇代数中的簇邻接性；5）在仅依赖动量扭子四括号的扇区中，提出并部分证明了Gr(4,n)中更强的簇性质；6）四质量字母虽非有理，但呈现类Sklyanin括号模式。

Conclusion: 广义幺正性、朗道奇点结构与簇邻接性并非孤立现象，而是在振幅符号层面深度统一；本文建立的几何与代数框架为理解散射振幅深层结构提供了新范式，并为高圈或更一般理论中的类似性质研究铺平道路。

Abstract: Generalized unitarity, Landau analysis, and cluster adjacency encode complementary aspects of scattering amplitudes. We use one-loop planar $\mathcal N=4$ SYM amplitudes in dimensional regularization, at arbitrary multiplicity and helicity, to make their interface explicit. The weight-two symbol decomposes into an LS part, in which maximal-cut leading singularities furnish the coefficients and Landau loci associated with nested cuts organize the ordered symbol entries, an algebraic four-mass sector, and residual terms. Cancellations of certain letters contributed by individual box integrals, as well as further simplifications, are explained by the two-mass triangle relations among box coefficients. We prove these relations using a BCFW-like application of the global residue theorem and show that they can be understood geometrically as different dissections of the same region in the tree amplituhedron obtained by projecting the loop geometry of a triple cut. We then prove that the full rational symbol, including its infrared-divergent part, obeys cluster adjacency in the flag cluster algebra $\mathrm{Fl}_{2,4;n}$ for all multiplicities and helicities. Within the sector depending only on momentum-twistor four-brackets, we conjecture a stronger cluster property in $\operatorname{Gr}(4,n)$ for all helicities and prove it for NMHV amplitudes: the amplitude admits a representation in which every pole of each coefficient is compatible with both symbol entries. Finally, we observe that the algebraic four-mass letters, although non-rational, exhibit a suggestive Sklyanin-bracket pattern.

</details>


### [249] [Non-critical M-Theory and the Resolved Conifold: Refinement and Nonperturbative Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06075)
*Fengjun Xu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Hořava-Keeler对应关系，将有限温度非临界M理论与分辨构形A模型的精炼版本联系起来，通过角化学势变形Ω-背景，使大势精确匹配Hattab-Palti和Chuang提出的非微扰构形补全，并为这些补全提供了微观谱学实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立非临界M理论与精炼Gopakumar-Vafa展开之间的精确对应，并解释其在非微扰构形补全中的微观起源。

Method: 利用非临界M理论的谱与预解式推导一粒子Schwinger被积函数及其积分路径，结合角化学势调节Ω-背景参数，实现与精炼A模型的匹配。

Result: 实现了旋转非临界M理论真空的大势与精炼Gopakumar-Vafa展开的精确对应；在有限热半径下，严格复现了已有的非微扰构形补全形式；给出了该补全的微观谱学解释。

Conclusion: 分辨构形单个原始无自旋超多重态是实现该对应的关键；推广至更一般的局部Calabi-Yau几何需额外引入荷与自旋依赖的微观数据。

Abstract: We extend the Hořava--Keeler correspondence between finite-temperature non-critical M-theory and the resolved-conifold A-model to the refined theory. The grand potential of the stationary rotating non-critical M-theory vacuum reproduces the refined Gopakumar--Vafa expansion, with the angular chemical potential deforming the $Ω$-background away from the self-dual locus $\eps_1=-\eps_2$. At finite thermal radius, we establish an exact match with the nonperturbative conifold completions proposed by Hattab--Palti and by Chuang in the unrefined and refined cases, respectively. Both the one-particle Schwinger integrand and its integration cycle arise from the non-critical M-theory spectrum and resolvent, providing a microscopic spectral realization of these completions. The correspondence fixes the nonconstant BPS sector and the cubic local contribution, while the remaining polynomial ambiguity and the modulus-independent constant-map sector require separate normalization. We explain why the single primitive spinless multiplet of the resolved conifold makes this identification possible, and discuss what additional charge- and spin-dependent microscopic data would be needed for an extension to more general local Calabi--Yau geometries.

</details>


### [250] [The Wilson-line-dressed charged sector of scalar QED: superselection and the infraparticle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06224)
*Gordon W. Semenoff,Conor Waterfield*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种规范与洛伦兹不变的微扰计算方法，研究标量量子电动力学中威尔逊线修饰算符所产生带电态在有限时空演化中的行为；通过欧氏反射与拼接，将两个修饰态的重叠表示为欧氏两点函数，并用Stueckelberg光子质量作为红外截断，在重整化微扰论中显式计算；结果揭示了带电荷扇区若干结构性特征：非平行威尔逊线修饰态呈‘云正交’，其重叠随红外调节器幂律消失，指数由夹角决定且单圈精确；电荷态按修饰方向超选择；同一超选择扇区内两点函数红外有限但呈现红外粒子特性（质量壳极点被支割线端点取代），导致其渐近时空展宽服从单圈精确的幂律标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对带电荷态红外结构（如云正交性、超选择、红外粒子行为）的研究多依赖非微扰或代数方法，缺乏显式、可计算的微扰描述；本文旨在建立一种规范与洛伦兹不变、且具明确红外截断的微扰框架，以定量揭示这些结构性特征。

Method: 采用Osterwalder-Schrader反射与欧氏拼接技术，将希尔伯特空间中 dressed 态的内积转化为欧氏两点函数；引入Stueckelberg光子质量作为BRST、规范及洛伦兹不变的红外截断；在该框架下进行重整化微扰计算，显式求得重叠、两点函数及谱性质。

Result: 1) 非平行威尔逊线修饰态之间云正交，重叠按红外调节器的幂律消失，指数由两线夹角决定，且单圈精确；2) 带电态按修饰方向超选择；3) 同一超选择扇区内两点函数红外有限，但谱中质量壳极点退化为支割线端点，体现红外粒子特性；4) 渐近时空展宽服从单圈精确的幂律，指数依赖于修饰方式与运动方向；5) 该标度律在较宽固有时窗内稳健成立。

Conclusion: 本文建立的微扰框架首次在显式、可计算层面统一解释了云正交性、超选择和红外粒子行为等关键红外现象，证实其深层联系，并表明部分红外指数具有单圈精确性与几何本质（如夹角依赖），为理解带电荷态的量子场论结构提供了新范式。

Abstract: We develop a manifestly gauge and Lorentz invariant perturbative calculus to study the finite time and distance evolution of the electrically charged states created by Wilson-line-dressed scalar field operators in scalar quantum electrodynamics. By an Osterwalder-Schrader reflection and Euclidean gluing, the overlap of two dressed states is expressed as a Euclidean 2 point function of dressed operators, which is then evaluated in renormalized perturbation theory with a Stueckelberg photon mass as a gauge, BRST and Lorentz invariant infrared cutoff. This renders explicit and computable in closed form several structural features of the charged sector that have so far been accessible mainly through non-perturbative or algebraic arguments. We find that states dressed by non-parallel Wilson lines are ``cloud orthogonal". Their overlap vanishes as a power of the infrared regulator, with an exponent that depends only on the angle between the two dressings and is an infrared analogue of the cusp anomalous dimension with the difference that it is one-loop exact whenever the charged matter is mass-gapped. Charged states are thereby superselected by the orientation of their dressing. Within a superselection sector the 2 point function is infrared finite but exhibits the infraparticle. The mass shell pole is replaced by the edge of a branch cut. As a result, the asymptotic spread of a dressed charge is a power law in proper time with a one-loop-exact computable exponent depending on the dressing and on the direction of motion. We show that although cloud orthogonality and the infraparticle are each extremely sensitive to fundamental infrared cutoffs, the infraparticle scaling law persists across a wide window of proper time.

</details>


### [251] [Finite stress-tensor moment tomography of boundary-graviton coherences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06363)
*Dyuman Bhattacharya,Robert B. Mann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdS3引力中边界引力子的量子态层析成像问题，发现使用N阶应力张量矩进行测量时信息不完整，而使用N+1阶则可完整重构量子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在2+1维爱因斯坦引力中，虽然体空间无局域引力子，但AdS3在Brown-Henneaux边界条件下存在由Virasoro后代描述的边界引力子；需探究如何通过边界应力张量矩来探测这些状态。

Method: 基于最高权模和非退化Shapovalov形式假设，分析有限后代支撑未知态的应力矩层析成像能力，考察不同阶数应力矩对量子态区分能力的影响。

Result: 发现对于水平N≥2的后代态，N阶应力矩层析不完整，而N+1阶完整；类似结论适用于L≥2的有限窗口情形及左右手全理论；在重态半经典极限下，该阈值标志着有限支撑边界引力子态首次可被区分。

Conclusion: 应力矩层析的最小完备阶数为后代水平加一，揭示了边界观测者分辨边界引力子量子态所需的信息量阈值。

Abstract: Einstein gravity in 2+1 dimensions is a topological theory that does not admit the existence of local bulk gravitons. However, $\mathrm{AdS}_3$ gravity with Brown-Henneaux boundary conditions does admit the existence of boundary gravitons, which are described by Virasoro descendants. An observer living on the asymptotic boundary can use finite order moments of the boundary stress tensor as a probe of these states. We fix one highest-weight module and assume that the Shapovalov forms are nondegenerate. We also assume that the unknown state the observer is probing has a known finite descendant support and that interlevel coherence is part of the unknown data. We find that, given a descendant at level $N\geq2$, stress moment tomography at order $N$ (meaning $N$ stress moment insertions) is informationally incomplete, while tomography with stress moments of order $N+1$ is informationally complete. This is to say that, on the unitary regular locus, orthogonal pure states can have the same complete order-$N$ record. Likewise, for exact finite-window result for levels through $L\geq2$, tomography of order $L$ is incomplete while tomography of order $L+1$ recovers every density-matrix block. Corresponding exact thresholds hold in the full left-right theory. In heavy sectors for which a semiclassical bulk interpretation exists, these thresholds say when finite-support boundary-graviton states first become distinguishable.

</details>


### [252] [Fermionic hidden zeros](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06552)
*Yong Zhang,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发现并证明了一类在无质量费米子树级振幅中普遍存在的隐藏零点，这些零点出现在特定的运动学区域，并通过世界面分析揭示了其普适性根源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示无质量费米子振幅中隐藏零点的普遍规律及其在规范与引力理论中的统一结构。

Method: 利用世界面分析方法，结合局域融合通道（如ff→g、fg/gf→f、gg→g）和物种依赖的桥接条件，对任意多重数进行严格证明。

Result: 在D=4,6,10维中，任意偶数个无质量实伴随费米子与胶子排列的色序规范振幅，在矩形运动学区域加桥接条件下恒为零；该机制可推广至含任意偶数个引力子和引力微子的十维振幅，以及含胶微子与引力子的单迹爱因斯坦-杨-米尔斯振幅。

Conclusion: 费米子隐藏零点背后存在有限且普适的局域结构，贯穿规范与引力振幅，体现了深层的统一性。

Abstract: We uncover and prove a general class of hidden zeros in tree-level amplitudes with massless fermions. For arbitrary even numbers and arbitrary arrangements of massless real adjoint fermions and gluons, color-ordered gauge-theory amplitudes in $D=4,6,10$ vanish on general rectangular kinematic loci supplemented by species-dependent bridge conditions. The fermion--fermion bridge is governed simply by the vector current $χ_iγ^μχ_j$, while mixed and gluonic bridges are fixed by the corresponding gauge-covariant contractions. A worldsheet analysis proves the result at arbitrary multiplicity and traces its universality to the local fusion channels $ff\to g$, $fg/gf\to f$, and $gg\to g$, with no new primitive bridge structures appearing at higher fermion multiplicity. The same mechanism extends to ten-dimensional amplitudes with gravitons and arbitrary even numbers of gravitinos in factorized gamma-traceless polarizations, and to single-trace Einstein-Yang-Mills amplitudes with adjoint gluinos and gravitons. These results expose a finite local structure underlying fermionic hidden zeros across gauge and gravitational amplitudes.

</details>


### [253] [Constructing the Monopole Formula for $A$-Type Good Quivers via Quiver Yangians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06711)
*Tiantai Chen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于quiver Yangian/Coulomb分支代数对应猜想，为3D N=4良构A型quiver规范理论（即T_ρ(SU(N))理论）的磁单极子公式提供了quiver Yangian解释，并通过半-BPS涡旋上的代数作用重构了其Hilbert级数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立quiver Yangian与3D N=4良构A型quiver规范理论（T_ρ(SU(N))）磁单极子公式之间的深刻联系，验证其代数结构与几何表示的一致性。

Method: 利用quiver Yangian在1/2-BPS涡旋上的作用，将截断平移quiver Yangian的生成元重组织为边界适配生成元，并将其经典关系表达为Slodowy切片S^gl_N_ρ上的U(N) Casimir算子。

Result: 成功将T_ρ(SU(N))理论的磁单极子公式的Hall-Littlewood表达式重构为截断平移quiver Yangian的Hilbert级数，且边界适配生成元与Casimir关系具有正确fugacity。

Conclusion: quiver Yangian框架能统一描述T_ρ(SU(N))理论的代数、几何与组合结构，支持quiver Yangian/Coulomb分支代数对应猜想，并为三维超对称规范理论提供新的代数工具。

Abstract: Based on the conjectured (and checked for tree-type quivers) quiver Yangian/Coulomb branch algebra correspondence, we give a quiver Yangian interpretation of the monopole formulas for 3D $\mathcal N=4$ good $A$-type quiver gauge theories, also known as the $T_ρ(SU(N))$ theories. Using the algebra action on the $\frac12$-BPS vortices, we reorganize the generators of the truncated shifted quiver Yangian into boundary-adapted generators, and obtain the classical relations in terms of $U(N)$ Casimirs on the Slodowy slice $\mathcal S^{\mathfrak{gl}_N}_ρ$, whose coordinates are given by these boundary-adapted generators. We also show that the boundary-adapted generators and the Casimir relations have the correct fugacities as predicted by the Hall-Littlewood expression of the monopole formula for $T_ρ(SU(N))$, hence reconstructing it as the Hilbert series of the truncated shifted quiver Yangian.

</details>


### [254] [Dual N=1 Lagrangians for Argyres-Douglas theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06995)
*Minseok Cho,Jaewon Song*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发现了一组新的四维N=1拉格朗日规范理论，其红外极限对应于特定类型的N=2 Argyres-Douglas理论，并给出了SU(2)^n类的对偶理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解N=2 Argyres-Douglas理论提供新的N=1拉格朗日描述，并建立与已有Sp(n)或SU(n+1)规范理论的对偶关系。

Method: 构造SU(2)^n型多节点quiver规范理论，引入若干规范单态flip场，分析其红外流动行为。

Result: 成功构建了对应于(A1, A2n)、(A1, A2n+1)、(A1, D2n+1)和(A1, D2n+2)型Argyres-Douglas理论的新型N=1拉格朗日对偶理论。

Conclusion: 该工作拓展了对Argyres-Douglas理论的拉格朗日理解，揭示了quiver图中秩与节点数之间的对偶交换关系。

Abstract: We find a new set of four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ Lagrangian gauge theories that flow in the infrared to $\mathcal{N}=2$ Argyres-Douglas theories of type $(A_1, A_{2n})$, $(A_1, A_{2n+1})$, $(A_1, D_{2n+1})$, and $(A_1, D_{2n+2})$. The new dual theories are given by $SU(2)^n$ quiver gauge theories with a number of gauge-singlet flip fields. This description provides dual theories for the previous $\mathcal{N}=1$ Lagrangian realizations given by $Sp(n)$ or $SU(n+1)$ gauge theories, exchanging the rank and the number of nodes in a quiver diagram.

</details>


### [255] [Tachyon-massless couplings at order $α'$ in bosonic string theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07221)
*Mohammad R. Garousi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用T-对偶性推导了玻色弦理论中tachyon与无质量场在α'阶（四导数阶）的有效作用量耦合项，通过规范选择和最小基构造，将独立项从14个约化为6个，并由零tachyon极限和四tachyon散射振幅确定两个自由参数；结果支持tachyon势能形式为(2/α')(-1+cos T)，暗示tachyon凝聚至T=π处并产生具有Λ=-4/α'的AdS时空。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决玻色弦理论经典有效作用量中tachyon耦合项的结构问题，特别是验证其仅含偶数个tachyon场的猜想，并在α'阶下系统构建满足T-对偶性的耦合项。

Method: 采用T-对偶性约束，结合高阶导数场重定义选取规范，构造四导数阶下仅含tachyon协变导数与无质量场的最小耦合项基，再通过零tachyon极限和球面级四tachyonS矩阵元进行参数固定。

Result: 得到6个非零四导数耦合项，含两个待定参数；其中一个由无tachyon极限确定，另一个由四tachyon散射振幅确定；发现T^4项系数为正，且tachyon质量项与四次项共同支持势能形式(2/α')(-1+cos T)。

Conclusion: tachyon势能(2/α')(-1+cos T)表明tachyon凝聚于T=π，导致生成具有负宇宙常数Λ=-4/α'的Anti-de Sitter时空，为闭弦tachyon凝聚提供具体有效作用量实现。

Abstract: Recently, it has been proposed that the classical effective action of bosonic string theory should contain only couplings with an even number of tachyon fields. We employ the T-duality procedure to derive such couplings at order \(α'\) (four-derivative order) for the tachyon and massless fields. We first observe that, through higher-derivative field redefinitions of the tachyon potential term, one can choose a scheme in which, apart from the tachyon potential itself, all remaining couplings involve only covariant derivatives of the tachyon and the massless fields. We then construct a minimal basis of such couplings at four-derivative order, consisting of 14 independent terms. Imposing T-duality reduces this set to 6 nonzero couplings, expressed in terms of two unfixed parameters. One of these parameters is determined by matching to the known effective action in the zero-tachyon limit, while the other is fixed by comparison with the sphere-level S-matrix element of four tachyon vertex operators. This latter comparison also establishes that the coefficient of the \(T^4\) term in the tachyon potential is positive. Remarkably, the tachyon mass term and this quartic term are consistent with a potential of the form \((2/α')(-1+\cos T)\), which implies that the closed string tachyon condenses to the minimum of the potential at \(T=π\), thereby generating an Anti-de Sitter spacetime with cosmological constant \(Λ=-4/α'\).

</details>


### [256] [Can one hear the shape of a black hole singularity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07514)
*Sean A. Hartnoll,Alexander Zhiboedov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过WKB分析方法，研究了渐近AdS黑洞的大overtone准正则频率，发现其可提取内部奇点附近的Kasner指数，并揭示Kasner跃迁与准正则频率的关系，建立了overtone数与到奇点固有时的映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞内部奇点附近的时空结构及其在边界可观测量（如准正则频率）中的体现，特别是BKL混沌中Kasner跃迁的可探测性。

Method: 采用WKB近似方法分离由近奇点时空决定的非解析指数与非普适数据（如边界到奇点的光学距离），并结合数值模型验证。

Result: 发现大overtone数n下准正则频率对边界动量k²的色散∂ωₙ/∂k²在k=0处的行为可干净提取Kasner指数；Kasner跃迁在频率谱中可被‘听见’，且overtone数n与到奇点的固有时存在对应关系。

Conclusion: 准正则频率不仅携带黑洞整体几何信息，还敏感反映内部奇点附近的动力学结构，为通过边界观测探知黑洞内部奇点物理提供了新途径。

Abstract: We compute the large overtone quasinormal frequencies $ω_n$ of asymptotically AdS black holes with interior near-singularity Kasner epochs. We show that the Kasner exponents can be extracted cleanly from the large $n$ behaviour of the dispersion $\partial ω_n/\partial k^2$ at $k=0$, where $k$ is a boundary momentum. We show that Kasner transitions, as occur in BKL near-singularity chaos, are audible in the quasinormal frequencies and reveal a map between overtone number $n$ and proper time to the singularity. Our results are obtained using a WKB analysis that isolates non-analytic exponents, determined by the near-singularity spacetime, from non-universal data such as the optical distance from the boundary to the singularity. We check our formulae in several numerical models.

</details>


### [257] [Exact spin form factors and correlations at a massive Kramers-Wannier interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07612)
*Zoltan Bajnok,Yizhuang Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文精确计算了标度伊辛模型中分离有序与无序相的Kramer-Wannier界面的有限体积自旋形因子和两点关联函数，结合两种格点实现与连续极限描述，得到精确的NSR自旋矩阵元和因子化的Fredholm行列式形式的界面关联函数，并通过缺陷TCSA和费米子自旋链计算验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究标度伊辛模型中连接有序与无序相的拓扑界面（Kramer-Wannier界面）的精确量子关联性质，填补其有限体积下自旋算符矩阵元与关联函数的严格解空白。

Method: 融合自对偶非均匀伊辛链（具符号变化的Majorana质量剖面）与时空交换后的混合热平均（适用Toeplitz/Fredholm方法），推导Neveu-Schwarz-Ramond自旋矩阵元；辅以缺陷TCSA数值方法和独立的费米子自旋链计算进行交叉验证。

Result: 获得界面自旋形因子与两点关联函数的精确解析表达式：包括全部NSR扇区的自旋矩阵元，以及一个可因子化的Fredholm行列式形式的界面关联函数；数值上确认了理论预言的能谱与矩阵元。

Conclusion: Kramer-Wannier界面在标度极限下具有完全可解的自旋结构，其关联函数可通过统一的解析-数值框架精确刻画，为理解临界界面与缺陷共形场论提供了严格范例。

Abstract: We determine exact finite-volume spin form factors and two-point correlations for a massive Kramer--Wannier interface in the scaling Ising model, separating ordered and disordered phases. The interface admits two complementary lattice realizations: a self-dual inhomogeneous Ising chain whose continuum limit has a sign-changing Majorana mass profile, and, after exchanging Euclidean space and time, a mixed thermal average amenable to Toeplitz/Fredholm methods. Combining these descriptions, we obtain the exact Neveu-Schwarz-Ramond spin matrix elements and a factorized Fredholm determinant for the interface correlator. Defect TCSA and independent fermionic spin-chain calculations confirm the predicted spectrum and matrix elements.

</details>


### [258] [(Anti-)De Sitter null strings and Carroll-Weyl symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07765)
*Ulf Lindström,Bo Sundborg*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过将标准无张力弦提升到R^{1,d}空间，并规范化世界面尺度对称性，构造了一种新型d维德西特（dS_d）无张力弦，其作用量对应于R^{1,d}中的Carroll-Weyl对称弦作用量，但具有明确的d维目标空间解释；该工作澄清了该模型并非(d+1)维闵可夫斯基空间中的弦，解决了此前争议，并可推广至反德西特（AdS）背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Carroll-Weyl弦模型在目标空间解释上的模糊性与争议，特别是其是否真正描述(d+1)维平直时空中的弦；同时探索在弯曲时空（如dS/AdS）中构建无张力弦的代数方法。

Method: 将标准无张力弦提升至嵌入空间R^{1,d}，通过对世界面尺度对称性进行规范固定，引入破坏平移不变性但保持SO(1,d) dS对称性的项；选择dS对称的规范，导出d维有效理论；分析所得作用量的对称性与物理含义。

Result: 成功构造出具有明确d维dS_d目标空间解释的无张力弦模型，其作用量即为R^{1,d}中的Carroll-Weyl对称弦作用量；澄清该模型不对应(d+1)维闵氏空间弦，消除了先前误解；并指出该代数构造法可自然推广至AdS情形。

Conclusion: 无张力弦可在dS_d和AdS_d中通过代数方式纯几何地构造，无需依赖传统弯曲时空中的复杂处理；Carroll-Weyl弦在R^{1,d}中的出现实为d维dS弦的自然实现，具有更深刻的对称性与几何起源。

Abstract: We construct a $dS_d$ null string in a novel way by lifting the standard tensionless string to $R^{1,d}$. Gauging worldsheet scale symmetry introduces terms in the action breaking translation invariance, but preserving the $dS_d$ symmetry $SO(1,d)$. Choosing a De Sitter symmetric gauge fixing of the scale symmetry yields a $d$ dimensional theory which we show has all the expected properties of $dS_d$ tensionless strings. The action happens to be the intriguing Carrol-Weyl symmetric string action in $R^{1,d}$, now with a clear target space interpretation in $d$ dimensions. That it does not, as recently claimed, represent a ($d+1$)-dimensional string in flat ($d+1$)-dimensional Minkowski space settles an apparent issue with the Carrol-Weyl string model. Furthermore, while there are clear obstacles for similar formulations of tensile strings in (A)dS backgrounds, our new insights are easily modified to the formulation of Anti-De Sitter tensionless strings. That our construction generates a curved geometry purely algebraically may have even wider applications.

</details>


### [259] [Eikonal Scattering of Two D0-branes with Graviton Emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07788)
*Paolo Di Vecchia,Dawid Maskalaniec*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用边界态形式主义研究了两个D0-膜散射中无质量闭弦态的辐射，计算了玻色弦理论和II型超弦理论中引力子与胀子辐射的五点振幅，并分析其场论极限；对于非零碰撞参数，所得振幅可通过对应低能理论的世界线方法独立重现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解D0-膜散射过程中闭弦激发（如引力子、胀子）的辐射行为，及其在低能极限下与有效场论的一致性。

Method: 采用边界态形式主义计算五点散射振幅，并对比世界线方法在低能极限下的结果。

Result: 成功计算了玻色弦与II型超弦中引力子和胀子辐射的五点振幅，并验证其在非零撞击参数下与世界线低能计算一致。

Conclusion: 边界态形式主义与世界线方法在描述D0-膜散射辐射现象上具有一致性，支持弦理论低能有效描述的自洽性。

Abstract: We study the emission of a massless closed-string state in the scattering of two D0-branes using the boundary-state formalism. We compute the corresponding five-point amplitudes for graviton and dilaton emission in bosonic string theory and in type II superstring theory, and analyze their field-theory limit. For nonzero impact parameter, the resulting amplitudes are reproduced by an independent calculation in the worldline formulation of the corresponding low-energy theory.

</details>


### [260] [Logarithmic correction to the entropy of near-extremal higher-curvature black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07801)
*Pablo Bueno,Pablo A. Cano,Robie A. Hennigar,Javier Moreno,Ángel J. Murcia,Guido van der Velde*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过将D维球对称引力理论映射为二维Horndeski理论，推导出近极端黑洞低能有效理论——Jackiw-Teitelboim（JT）引力，并证明其熵的对数量子修正具有普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解近极端黑洞低能动力学及其熵的量子修正是否具有普适性，超越爱因斯坦引力框架。

Method: 利用D维球对称引力与二维Horndeski理论的对应关系，构造近视界有效理论；基于JT引力的一圈精确配分函数，分析熵的对数修正及其破缺温度。

Result: 证明了熵的对数修正S_JT = (3/2) log(T / T_breakdown) 在所有具备二维Horndeski描述的引力模型中普适成立，并给出了T_breakdown用D维理论参数表达的一般形式。

Conclusion: 近极端黑洞的低能量子引力行为（特别是熵的对数修正）由JT引力统一刻画，且该现象不依赖于高维具体理论细节，仅取决于其球对称有效二维结构。

Abstract: The spherically symmetric sector of broad classes of $D$-dimensional gravitational theories can be effectively described by general two-dimensional Horndeski theories. Exploiting this correspondence, we study the low-temperature dynamics of near-extremal black hole solutions of the parent theories by deriving a universal near-horizon effective description in terms of Jackiw--Teitelboim (JT) gravity. We explicitly show that the logarithmic quantum correction to the entropy, $S_{\rm JT}=\frac{3}{2}\log \left(T/T_{\rm breakdown} \right)$, previously derived for near-extremal black holes in Einstein gravity coupled to matter from the one-loop exact JT partition function, is universal across all models admitting an effective two-dimensional Horndeski description. We determine the general form of the scale at which this correction becomes dominant, $T_{\rm breakdown}$, in terms of the data of the $D$-dimensional theories. We illustrate our general results with charged black holes in Lovelock gravities and regular black holes in Quasitopological gravities.

</details>


### [261] [SolutionsX: supergravity theories/solutions as code, with an agentic workflow for machine-verifiable physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07826)
*Vasil Dimitrov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SolutionsX，一个基于Mathematica和xAct的可验证、可复用的符号计算工具包，用于形式化并自动验证广义相对论、超引力等理论中的解析解；它支持机器可读、可重放、自带验证的物理推导，并已成功应用于AI代理在数小时内自动编码新论文解的工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超引力等理论解难以独立复现与验证：因约定不明、笔误频出、代数繁复；亟需一种能将物理推导转化为机器可验证、可复用、自包含记录的工具。

Method: 开发SolutionsX Mathematica包，依托xAct框架，支持抽象张量、微分形式、任意维旋量；集成自动验证机制（每次声明前在干净内核实时校验）；设计可重放工作流与数据库结构；结合AI编码代理进行盲测实验。

Result: 成功复现并验证多个已发表超引力解；组件性能媲美原生xAct；三轮盲测中AI代理均在3小时内完成新论文解的完整形式化编码；构建了首个面向物理解的机器可验证数据库雏形。

Conclusion: SolutionsX不仅提升了理论物理推导的可靠性与可复现性，更作为AI代理的‘内部验证器’，为物理知识的自动化构建与学习提供了基础设施。

Abstract: A supergravity solution in the literature is an artifact that cannot be verified without re-deriving it: conventions might not be clearly stated, papers have typos, algebra is tedious. SolutionsX is a Mathematica package that turns an xAct environment, whatever physics it may describe, into a stored, machine-verifiable record: code that replays in a fresh kernel, carries its own verification, and can later be fetched for re-use or extension. The shipped examples are supergravity theories and their solutions, but the scope is anything xAct reaches: abstract tensors, differential forms, spinors in any dimension. We follow one published solution through the entire workflow, benchmark the package's component engine against xAct's, and report on three blind runs of an agentic workflow in which an AI coding agent, given the package and a small toolkit, correctly codified a recent paper in under three hours. A database of such records is useful to researchers today and gives agents worked examples to learn from. The package acts as an internal verifier during the thought process of an agent: a claim is checked in the kernel before it is made.

</details>


### [262] [Unitary quantum conformal Bondi-Metzner-Sachs field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07828)
*Daniel Grumiller,Iva Lovrekovic*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有量子共形Bondi-Metzner-Sachs代数对称性的二维场论的幺正性约束，得出中央电荷c的取值范围为0<c<2.053，并进一步限定为仅四个可能值：c = 1, 3/2, 13/7, 2；并计算了对应初级算子谱，验证其满足BPS类与全息不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为构建具有渐近平直时空渐近对称性与共形对称性结合的二维幺正场论，需施加幺正性与顶点代数正定性约束。

Method: 通过分析量子共形BMS代数的幺正表示，结合顶点代数正定性条件，推导中央电荷c的允许取值，并计算各允许c值下的初级算子谱，检验其是否满足BPS类及全息不等式。

Result: 得到中央电荷c必须满足0<c<2.053，且仅c=1, 3/2, 13/7, 2四种取值能使关联顶点代数正定；对应四类理论的初级谱均满足BPS类与全息不等式。

Conclusion: 仅有四个特定中央电荷值（c = 1, 3/2, 13/7, 2）能支持满足幺正性与正定顶点代数的二维量子共形BMS场论，且其谱结构兼容基本物理约束。

Abstract: We impose unitarity constraints on two-dimensional field theories whose symmetries form the quantum conformal Bondi-Metzner-Sachs algebra, which combines conformal symmetries with asymptotic symmetries of asymptotically flat spacetimes. Positivity constraints put bounds on the central charge, 0<c<2.053. Positivity of the associated vertex algebra allows only four central charges, c = 1, 3/2, 13/7, 2. Thus, only four candidates for unitary field theories remain. We calculate their spectra of primaries and show that they are compatible with BPS-like and holographic inequalities.

</details>


### [263] [Diffeomorphism-invariant Approach to Asymptotically Safe Quantum Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07829)
*Friederike Ihssen,Benjamin Knorr,Silas Mezger,Jan M. Pawlowski,Paul P. Sprenger*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型的微分同胚不变的渐近安全度规量子引力方法，通过物理信息驱动的重整化群流实现，并首次以显式微分同胚不变方式计算了Reuter不动点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有量子引力方法中难以同时保持微分同胚不变性与算符相关性计数控制的问题，提升背景无关性和理论自洽性。

Method: 构建保证每步重整化群演化都保持微分同胚不变性的重整化群核，发展物理信息驱动的重整化群流方法。

Result: 获得量子微分同胚不变且背景无关的有效作用量；成功以显式不变方式计算Reuter不动点，并系统评估了截断近似与正则化依赖带来的误差。

Conclusion: 该方法为渐近安全量子引力提供了更可靠、更自洽的框架，强化了其作为量子引力候选理论的可信度。

Abstract: We provide a novel diffeomorphism-invariant approach to asymptotically safe metric quantum gravity. It is based on physics-informed renormalisation group flows with a renormalisation group kernel that guarantees diffeomorphism invariance at each renormalisation group step. Importantly, it also allows us to control and maintain the relevance counting of operators in metric quantum gravity. The resulting effective action is quantum diffeomorphism-invariant and background-independent. As a first non-trivial application, we compute the Reuter fixed point in a manifestly diffeomorphism-invariant way. This computation is augmented with a detailed discussion of regularisation dependence and a systematic estimate of the errors arising from approximations.

</details>


### [264] [Truncated Polyakov bootstrap for BCFTs: Neumann-Dirichlet flow and Ising special transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07841)
*Kaushik Kangsabanik,Apratim Kaviraj,Astha Tiwari*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将截断Polyakov bootstrap方法扩展到边界共形场论（BCFT），利用解析泛函框架求解BCFT交叉方程，并通过广义自由场（GFF）的形变流追踪BCFT数据，成功应用于3d Ising特殊相变，结果与格点计算高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展适用于边界共形场论（BCFT）的非微扰bootstrap方法，弥补现有技术在BCFT中解析求解交叉方程的不足。

Method: 基于解析泛函框架的截断Polyakov bootstrap；通过固定边界条件下的广义自由场（GFF）形变流（如Neumann→Dirichlet）识别非微扰解；结合Wilson-Fisher微扰描述刻画3d Ising特殊相变。

Result: 获得3d Ising特殊相变的BCFT谱和OPE系数，与已有格点结果高度吻合，部分结果为首次给出。

Conclusion: 截断Polyakov bootstrap可有效推广至BCFT；GFF形变流是定位非微扰BCFT解的有效路径；该方法兼具解析性与数值精度，为强耦合BCFT研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: We extend the recently proposed truncated Polyakov bootstrap to boundary conformal field theories (BCFTs). The formalism uses the analytic functional framework to find approximate solutions of BCFT crossing. We show that a generic nonperturbative solution can be identified by relating it to a family of deformations around generalized free fields (GFF) with a fixed boundary condition. We show this by tracking the flow of BCFT data from GFF with Neumann boundary condition to that with Dirichlet. The same method is then used for the 3d Ising special transition, using the perturbative Wilson-Fisher description as the deformation to identify it. The BCFT data obtained are in remarkable agreement with existing lattice results where available, and are otherwise new.

</details>


### [265] [Scattering of Null strings - Flipped Vacuum & CHY](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07845)
*Arjun Bagchi,Sachin Grover,Sharang Rajesh Iyer,Amartya Saha,Stephan Stieberger*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了零张力弦的散射，基于ILST作用量和2维共形Carroll代数（CCA）的不同真空态，构建了“翻转”真空下的量子理论，并通过新型顶点算符导出了与CHY公式一致的无质量态散射振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索零张力弦在不同真空和CCA表示下的量子理论，特别是‘翻转’真空所对应的最高权表示，以理解其散射行为。

Method: 基于ILST作用量和2维共形Carroll代数，构造‘翻转’真空下的量子理论；引入与紧化零弦密切相关的新型顶点算符，构建散射振幅。

Result: 在‘翻转’真空下，无质量态的散射振幅自然导出Cachazo-He-Yuan（CHY）公式。

Conclusion: 零张力弦在‘翻转’真空下的量子理论能统一地重现CHY形式论，为弦理论与散射振幅新结构之间提供了重要桥梁。

Abstract: We investigate the scattering of null tensionless strings. Classical null strings, given by the ILST action, give rise to three distinct quantum theories built on different vacua and representations of the underlying 2d Conformal Carroll algebra (CCA) which form the residual gauge symmetries on the null worldsheet. In this paper, we are interested in the quantum theory built out of the so-called ``flipped'' vacuum which realises the highest weight representation of the 2d CCA. By considering a novel class of vertex operators, intimately connected to the compactified null string, we build scattering amplitudes of the theory. We show that one naturally obtains the Cachazo-He-Yuan (CHY) formulae when one considers scattering of massless states in the null ``flipped'' string.

</details>


### [266] [Double Copy from the Flipped Null String](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07846)
*Arjun Bagchi,Sachin Grover,Sharang Rajesh Iyer,Amartya Saha,Stephan Stieberger*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了玻色零弦在翻转真空中的树图振幅的双拷贝结构，揭示了其与CHY公式、BCJ关系及KLT表示之间的深刻联系，并为双拷贝提供了直接的Carrollian世界面起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索弦理论中双拷贝结构的世界面起源，特别是与高导数规范理论和引力理论的对应关系。

Method: 基于Carrollian世界面分析玻色零弦的级联态相关器，结合CHY公式、Parke-Taylor因子、BCJ排序关系与KLT表示进行推导。

Result: 发现一级矢量关联器给出$(DF)^2$规范理论的CHY运动学半积分，特定动量-缠绕扇区生成Parke-Taylor因子；二级因子化态实现Weyl立方引力的对称双拷贝；晶格扇区复现BCJ关系与KLT表示。

Conclusion: 双拷贝结构可直接源于Carrollian世界面，且涌现的颜色结构可能由兼容$O(d,d)$晶格结构的流代数实现。

Abstract: We study the double-copy structure of tree amplitudes of the bosonic null string in the flipped vacuum. We show that its level-one vector correlator supplies the Cachazo-He-Yuan (CHY) kinematic half-integrand of the higher-derivative $(DF)^2$ gauge theory, while distinguished compact momentum-winding sectors generate the complementary Parke-Taylor factor. For factorized level-two states, the same Carrollian world-sheet produces two kinematic half-integrands and hence the symmetric double copy to the six-derivative Weyl-cubed graviton sector. Lattice sectors with common external momenta reproduce the Bern-Carrasco-Johansson (BCJ) ordering relation and the corresponding field-theory Kawai-Lewellen-Tye (KLT) representation. These results provide a direct Carrollian world-sheet origin of the double copy and point toward a current-algebra realization of the emergent color sector compatible with the $O(d,d)$ structure of the compact lattice.

</details>


### [267] [Entanglement Wedge Reconstruction Beyond the Large N Limit via the Twirled Petz Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07866)
*Arash Alipour Shahmiri,Maryam Sharifian,Niloofar Vardian*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用量子纠错框架，系统地将纠缠楔重建推广到超越大N近似的情形，通过twirled Petz映射引入1/N修正，并给出由次领头Petz核控制的双局域修正，从而为全量子引力下的体空间重建提供可控路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠楔重建在大N领头阶已有清晰理解，但其在非半经典（即超越大N） regime 中的推广仍不明确。

Method: 采用量子纠错理论框架，结合twirled Petz映射，系统引入并计算1/N次领头修正，导出显式的双局域修正项。

Result: 重现了已知的大N领头阶重建公式；获得了包含1/N修正的显式重建方法；推导出由次领头Petz核控制的双局域修正；确立了超越大N的可控体空间重建路径。

Conclusion: 量子纠错是描述超越大N近似的体空间重建的自然且可控框架，显著强化了其在全息原理中的基础地位。

Abstract: Entanglement wedge reconstruction provides the sharpest formulation of bulk locality in AdS/CFT, identifying the entanglement wedge as the largest bulk region reconstructible from a boundary subregion. While this statement is well understood at leading order in the large N expansion through both modular flow and Petz map reconstruction, its extension beyond the semiclassical regime has remained unclear. In this work, we show that quantum error correction provides the natural framework for addressing this problem. At leading order, we reproduce the known leading-order reconstruction formulas, and by employing the twirled Petz map, we obtain a systematic and explicit method for incorporating subleading 1/N corrections. Beyond the leading approximation, we have obtained an explicit bi-local correction controlled by a subleading Petz kernel. Our results establish a controlled approach to bulk reconstruction beyond large N and sharpening the role of quantum error correction in holography. This framework provides a controlled path toward understanding bulk reconstruction in fully quantum gravitational settings.

</details>


### [268] [ABJM/BMN Index Matching in the Plane-Wave Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07872)
*Chi-Ming Chang,Kangning Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了ABJM理论与BMN矩阵模型在平面波极限下、任意正陈-西蒙斯水平k时的精炼超对称指标的系数级相等性，将单膜推广至多膜情形，并揭示了磁单极子与模糊球膜之间的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解M理论中不同极限（如ABJM理论与BMN矩阵模型）之间的精确对应关系，尤其是多膜构型下的超对称指标一致性。

Method: 通过分析固定陈-西蒙斯水平k下ABJM理论与BMN矩阵模型的精炼超对称指标，结合标量修饰的等通量磁单极子、Higgs留数、对角全纯积分及单极-谐波/矩阵-谐波核的比较，研究平面波极限与有限动量修正。

Result: 证明了在所有正整数k下、固定膜多重数（即固定ABJM规范秩）时，ABJM与BMN的指标在系数级严格相等；明确了ABJM磁单极子通量与BMN膜沿M理论圆周动量的对应；指出非平面波余项随k增大向更高配分函数阶跃移，而谐波核与截断贡献保持不变。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了从单膜到多膜情形的指标对应推广，为M理论不同表述间的精确对偶提供了更坚实、更精细的指标证据。

Abstract: We establish coefficientwise equality between the refined supersymmetric indices of ABJM theory and the BMN matrix model in the plane-wave limit at every fixed positive Chern-Simons level k. For multi-membrane families with fixed multiplicities, and hence fixed finite ABJM gauge rank, the equality holds as all longitudinal momenta tend to infinity with their pairwise differences fixed.
